R 60,T 5

((tolkāppiyam, collatikāram,cēṉāvariyar uri))

((1. kiḷaviyākkam))

((1. tiṇi))

TC 1 c

 [TRANSLATION]

uyartiṇi eṉmaṉār makkaḷ+ cuṭṭu ē
aḵṟiṇi eṉmaṉār avar ala piṟa ē
āy iru tiṇiyiṉ icikkumaṉa col= ē

{{C CENV}}

{{Par. 1}} niṟutta muṟiyāṉ -ē col= uṇarttiya eṭuttu+-koṇṭār; ataṉāliv= atikāram^ col=-atikāram eṉṉum peyarttu* āyiṟṟu.

{{Par. 2}} col= āvatu eḻuttōṭu orupuṭiyāṉ oṟṟumi uṭittu āy+ poruḷkuṟittu varuvatu. uriyāciriyar um eḻuttu ātal+ taṉmiyoṭupuṇarntu eṉpār `eḻuttoṭu puṇarntu' eṉṟār ākaliṉ, oru-puṭioṟṟumi -~ē kūṟiṉār. taṉmiyoṭu puṇarntu eṉṉākkāl,ōr-eḻuttu*-oru-moḻikku ##eḻuttoṭu puṇartal# iṉmiyiṉ+ ##col=ātal# eytātu eṉka. poruḷ kuṟittu vārāmiyiṉ acinili col= ākā eṉiṉ:-- {{"āvayiṉ āṟu muṉṉili aciccol"}} {{[col:274]}} eṉṟu* um,{{"viyaṅkōḷ aciccol"}} {{[col:273]}} eṉṟu* um ōtutalāṉ,avi -~um iṭam* mutal ākiya poruḷ kuṟittu vantaṉa ~eṉka.{{"yā kā piṟa piṟakku"}} {{[col:279]}} eṉṉum^ toṭakkattaṉa-~ō ~eṉiṉ, avi -~um mūṉṟu* iṭattiṟku* -~um uriya ~āy+kaṭṭuri+ cuvi paṭa varutaliṉ poruḷ kuṟittaṉa ē ām. i+karuttu ē paṟṟi aṉṟē āciriyar {{"ellā+ col= um poruḷkuṟittaṉa ē"}} {{[col:155]}} eṉṟu ōtuvār āyiṟṟu eṉka.

{{Par. 3}} col+ tāṉ iraṇṭu vakippaṭum, taṉimoḻi um^ toṭarmoḻi um eṉa.avaṟṟu uḷ, taṉimoḻi -~āvatu camaya āṟṟalāṉ+ poruḷviḷakkuvatu. toṭarmoḻi -~āvatu avāyniliyāṉ um takutiyāṉ umaṇmiyāṉ um iyintu poruḷ viḷakkum^ taṉimoḻi īṭṭam.

{{Par. 4}} peyarccol= um, viṉiccol= um, iṭiccol= um, uriccol um eṉa+taṉimoḻi nāṉku vaki+ paṭum. {maram} eṉpatu peyarccol.{uṇṭāṉ} eṉpatu viṉiccol. {maṟṟu} eṉpatu iṭiccol. {naṉi}eṉpatu uriccol.

{{Par. 5}} iru moḻi+ toṭar um pal% moḻi+ toṭar um eṉa+ toṭarmoḻi iraṇṭuvaki+ paṭum. {cāttaṉ vantāṉ} eṉpatu iru moḻi+ toṭar. {aṟamvēṇṭi aracaṉ ulakam purantāṉ} eṉpatu pal% moḻi+ toṭar

{{Par. 6}} atikāram eṉṉum^ coṟku+ poruḷ pala uḷa ēṉum, īṇṭu atikārameṉṟatu oru poruḷ nutali varum pala ōttiṉatu tokutiyi. vaṭanūḷār um ōr iṭattu niṉṟa col+ pala cūttiraṅkaḷōṭu ceṉṟuiyitali -~um oṉṟaṉatu ilakkaṇam paṟṟi -varum pala cūttira+tokutiyi -~um atikāram eṉpa. col= atikāram colli uṇarttiyaatikāram eṉa viriyum. a+ colli yāṅṅaṉam uṇarttiṉār -ō eṉiṉ,tammi -~ē eṭuttu*-ōti -~um ilakkaṇam^ kūṟi -~um uṇarttiṉāreṉpatu.

{{Par. 7}} vaḻu+ kaḷintu coṟkaḷi ākki+ koṇṭamiyāṉ, iv= ōttu+ kiḷaviākkam āyiṟṟu. ākkam amittu+ kōṭal, {noy um* nuṟuṅku um^kaḷintu arici amittāri} {arici ākkiṉār} eṉpa ākaliṉ.coṟkaḷ poruḷkaṇ mēl ām āṟu uṇarttiṉamiyāṉ+ kiḷavi ākkamāyiṟṟu eṉiṉ um amiyum. potu vakiyāṉ+ kiḷavi eṉṟamiyāl,taṉimoḻi um^ toṭarmoḻi um^ koḷḷappaṭum. kiḷavi, col, moḻieṉṉum^ toṭakkattaṉa ellām oru poruḷ+ kiḷavi.

{{Par. 8}}

{{WFW}} i+ cūttirattiṉ poruḷ, makkaḷ eṉṟu karutappaṭum poruḷiāciriyar uyartiṇi eṉṟu colluvar; makkaḷ eṉṟu karutappaṭātapiṟa poruḷi aḵṟiṇi eṉṟu colluvar; av= iru tiṇi mēl um^coṟkaḷ nikaḻum eṉṟa āṟu. eṉa ē, uyartiṇi+ col= um aḵṟiṇi+col= um eṉa+ col= iraṇṭu eṉṟa āṟu ām.

{{Par. 9}}

{{C CENV}} makkaḷ+ cāti ciṟantamiyāṉ uyartiṇi eṉṟār.

{{Par. 10}} {{"eṉmaṉār"}} eṉpatu ceyyuḷ% muṭipu eyti-niṉṟatu* -ōr ārīṟṟu etir-kāla muṟṟu+-col. «eṉṟiciṉōr, kaṇṭiciṉōr» eṉpaṉamutal āyiṉa av= āṟu vanta iṟantakāla muṟṟu+ col. {eṉpa} eṉṉumuṟṟuccolliṉatu pakaram^ kuṟittu maṉ= um. ār um eṉa iraṇṭuiṭiccol+ peytu virittār eṉṟu uriyāciriyar kūṟiṉār āl eṉiṉ:-- {{"eṉmaṉār"}} eṉpatu iṭarpaṭṭuḻi+ ciṟupāṉmi vārātu, nūluḷ= um^ cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷ uḷ= um payiṉṟu varutalāṉ um, iciniṟieṉpatu maṟuttu+ poruḷ kūṟukiṉṟār piṉ= um iciniṟi eṉṟalmēṟkōḷ%-malivu* ātalāṉ -um. avarkku* atu karuttu* aṉṟu*eṉka. māṇākkarkku uṇarvu perukal vēṇṭi veḷippaṭa+ kūṟātuuyttu uṇara vittal avarkku iyalpu ākalāṉ+ ceyyuḷ% muṭipueṉpatu kūṟār āyiṉār.

{{Par. 11}} eṉmaṉār āciriyar eṉa ē, uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi eṉpaṉa tol=āciriyar kuṟi ām. āciriyar eṉṉum peyar veḷippaṭātu-niṉṟatu.

{{Par. 12}} makkaḷ ākiya cuṭṭu yātaṉkaṇ* ^nikaḻum atu {{"makkaṭcuṭṭu"}}eṉa+ paṇpu+-toki+ puṟattu+ piṟanta aṉmoḻi+-toki. īṇṭu{{"makkaḷ"}} eṉṟatu makkaḷ eṉṉum uṇarvi. eṉa -~ē, makkaḷ -ēāyiṉum makkaḷ eṉṟu cuṭṭātu poruḷ eṉṟu cuṭṭiya -vaḻiuyartiṇi eṉappaṭātu eṉpatu ām.

{{Par. 13}} iṉi avar ala eṉṉātu piṟa eṉṟu ē viṭiṉ yātu allāta piṟa eṉṟuavāy niṟkum ākaliṉ, avar ala eṉṟār. makkaḷ+ cuṭṭu ē eṉṟumēl* ^niṉṟamiyiṉ makkaḷ allāta piṟa eṉṟu uṇartal ām eṉiṉ:--āṟṟaṉ mutal āyiṉavaṟṟāṉ+ koḷvatu col= il vaḻi eṉa maṟukka.iṉi avar ala eṉṟu ē oḻiyiṉ avaṟṟatu pakuti ellām eñcāmal+taḻuvāmiyiṉ, eñcāmal+ taḻuvutaṟku avar ala piṟa eṉṟār.

{{Par. 14}} ceyyuḷ ākalāṉ āy iru tiṇi eṉa+ cuṭṭu nīṇṭatu. variyaṟiiṉmiyiṉ īṇṭu yakara uṭampaṭumey+ āyiṟṟu.

{{Par. 15}} col% nikaḻccikku+ poruḷ iṭam ākaliṉ, āy iru tiṇiyiṉkaṇ= eṉaēḻāvatu virikka. iṉ cāriyi vēṟṟumi-~urupu paṟṟi -~um paṟṟātu* -um -niṟkumeṉṟu uriyāciriyar iraṇṭāvatu virittār -āl eṉiṉ:
-- {{"cāriyi uḷvaḻi+ cāriyi keṭutal um^
cāriyi uḷvaḻi+ taṉ= urupu niḷiyal um"}}
{{[eḻuttu:158]}} eṉṟu iraṇṭāvataṟku+ tiripu ōtinamiyāṉ um,
{{"celaviṉ um varaviṉ um^ taraviṉ um^ koṭiyiṉ um"}}
{{[col:28]}} eṉpuḻi -~um piṟāṇṭu* -um ellām ēḻāvatu virittaṟku ēṟpa+poruḷ urittamiyāṉ -um, av= uri pōli-~uri eṉka. āy irutiṇiyiṉ -um eṉṉum ummi vikāra vakiyāṉ+ tokku-niṉṟatu.

{{Par. 16}} icikkum eṉpatu ceyyum eṉṉu muṟṟuccol.

{{Par. 17}} maṉ= eṉṉum iṭiccol maṉa eṉa īṟu tirintu-niṉṟatu. maṉ= eṉṟupāṭam ōtuvār um uḷar.

{{Par. 18}} ēkāram īṟṟaci.

{{Par. 19}} col variyaṟuttal ē i+ cūttirattiṟku+ karuttu āyiṉ,{{"āṭūuvaṟicol makaṭūuvaṟicol="}} eṉṟāṟpōla uyartiṇi+ colaḵṟiṇi+ col= eṉa amiyum, uyartiṇi makkaḷ aḵṟiṇi piṟaeṉal veṇṭā eṉiṉ: uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi eṉpaṉa tol= āciriyar kuṟiākalāṉ āṭūu makaṭūu+ pōla vaḻakkoṭu paṭuttu+ poruḷ uṇartalākāmiyiṉ, uyartiṇi makkaḷ aḵṟiṇi piṟa eṉal vēṇṭum eṉpatu.iv= āṟu oru poruḷ nutaliṟṟu āka uriyākkāl, cūttiram oṉṟu āmāṟu illi eṉka. ((1))

((2. pāl))

((uyartiṇiyil mūṉṟupāl))

TC 2 c

[TRANSLATION]

āṭūu aṟi col% makaṭūu aṟi col+
pallōr aṟiyum^ colloṭu civaṇi
a+ mu+ pāl+ col uyartiṇiyav+ ē

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: {{"āṭūuvaṟicol="}} um {{"makaṭūuvaṟicol="}} um pallōraṟiyum^ collōṭu poruṉti a+ mūṉṟu kūṟṟu+ col= umuyartiṇiyaṉa ām. e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} āṇmakaṉi āṭūu eṉṟal um, peṇṭāṭṭiyi makaṭūu eṉṟal umpaṇṭiyār vaḻakku.

{{Par. 3}} aṟivu mutal āyiṉavaṟṟāṉ āṇmakaṉ ciṟantamiyiṉ{{"āṭūuvaṟicol*"}} muṉ+ kūṟa+ -paṭṭatu. paṉmi iru pāl -umpaṟṟi varutaliṉ+ pallōr aṟi col+ piṉ+ kūṟa+ -paṭṭatu.

{{Par. 4}} iraṇṭām veṟṟumi uyartiṇikkaṇ= ciṟupāṉmi tokappeṟutaliṉ+tokku-niṉṟatu. tirinta puṇarcci aṉmiyiṉ vikāra -vakiyāṉ+tokkatu eṉpār -um uḷar.

{{Par. 5}} civaṇi eṉṉum viṉiyeccam uyartiṇiya eṉṉum viṉikkuṟippu+koṇṭatu, āṟām vēṟṟumi ēṟṟu-niṉṟa col+ peyar āy umviṉikkuṟippu āy um niṟkum ākalāṉ. uyartiṇiya ām eṉṉum mu+pāl+ colliṉ viṉi āṭūuvaṟicol makaṭūuvaṟicol eṉṉum iraṇṭaṇviṉi ākiya civaṇi eṉṉum^ ceytu eṉ eccattiṟku viṉimutalviṉi āyiṉa āṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ:-- uyartiṇiya ātal, āṭūuvaṟicolmakaṭūuvaṟicol eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku um eytutalāṉ, viṉimutal viṉiām eṉka, {{"mutaṉili mūṉṟu um viṉimutal% muṭipiṉa"}}{{[col:230]}} eṉpuḻi viṉimutal viṉi eṉṉum^ tuṇi allatupiṟitu oṉṟaṟku+ potu ākātu viṉimutaṟku ē viṉi ātal vēṇṭumeṉṉum variyaṟi iṉmiyiṉ. allākkāl, {ivaḷ um ivaṉ um^ciṟṟil iḻittu um^ ciṟu paṟi aṟintu um viḷiyāṭupa} eṉpaṉapōlvaṉa amiyātu eṉka.

{{Par. 6}} āṭūuvaṟicol+ mutal āyiṉavaṟṟi uṇartti allatu avaṟṟatuilakkaṇam uṇarttal ākāmiyiṉ, pāl uṇarttum eḻuttu vakuppa ēavi tām um peṟapaṭum i+ cūttiram vēṇṭā eṉpatu kaṭā aṉmiuṇarka.

{{Par. 7}} uyartiṇi+ col= uṇartti ataṉatu pākupātu kūṟukiṉrār ākaliṉ,a+ mu+ pāl+ col uyartiṇiya eṉṟār āyiṉum uyartiṇi+ colmūṉṟu pākupāṭu paṭum eṉpatu karuttu* āka koḷka. ikkaruttāṉaṉṟē, āciriyar `a+ mu+ pāl+ col= um' eṉa {{"iṉittu eṉaaṟinta"}} poruḷ+ tokutikku+ koṭukkum ummi koṭār āyiṉum,uriyāciriyar -um uyartiṇi eṉappaṭṭa pakuppi virippuḻiittuṇi allatu viri-paṭātu eṉpatu īṇṭu+ kūṟiyatu eṉṟuurittatūum eṉka. {{"āy iru pāl+ col aḵṟiṇiyav+ ē"}}{{[col:3]}} eṉpataṟku um ītu okkum.

((aḵṟiṇiyil iraṇṭu pāl))

TC 3 c

[TRANSLATION]

oṉṟu aṟi col= ē pala aṟi col= eṉṟu
āy iru pāl+ col aḵṟiṇiyav+ ē

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ. oṉṟaṉi aṟiyum^ col= um palavaṟṟi aṟiyum^ col= um eṉaav= iraṇṭu kūṟṟu+ col= um aḵṟiṇiyaṉa ām. e-ṟu. ((3))

(("pēṭi" mutaliya coṟkaḷ ippakutiyil aṭaṅkum muṟi))

TC 4 c

[TRANSLATION]

peṇmi cuṭṭiya uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ
āṇmi tirinta peyar nili kiḷavi um
teyvam^ cuṭṭiya peyar nili kiḷavi um
iv= eṉa aṟiyum antam tamakku ila ē
uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ pāl pirintu icikkum.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: uyartiṇi iṭattu+ peṇmi+ taṉmi kuṟitta āṇmi tirintapeyarccol= um, teyvatti kuṟitta peyarccol= um ivi eṉa+ tamporuḷi vēṟu aṟiya-niṟkum īṟṟu eḻuttiṉi+ tamakku uṭiyaalla, uyartiṇi iṭattu ataṟku uriya pāl āy vēṟupaṭṭuicikkum. e-ṟu

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} pāl vēṟupaṭṭu icittal -āvatu, tām uyartiṇi+ peyar āyāṭūuvaṟicol mutal āyiṉavaṟṟiṟku uriya īṟṟu eḻuttu ē tamviṉikku īṟu āka icittal.

{{Par. 3}} eṭuttukkāṭṭu: {pēṭi vantāḷ, pēṭar vantār, pēṭiyar vantār}eṉa -~um; {tēvaṉ vantāṉ, tēvi vantāḷ, tēvar vantār} eṉa -~umvarum.

{{Par. 4}} {ali+} peyariṉ nīkkutaṟku+ {{"peṇmi cuṭṭiya"}} eṉṟu* -um,{makaṭūu+} peyariṉ nīkkutaṟku {{"āṇmi tirinta"}} eṉṟu* -umkūṟiṉār.

{{Par. 5}} {{"peṇmi cuṭṭiya"}} eṉa ē peṇmi cuṭṭā+ {pēṭu} eṉpatu umoḻikkappaṭṭatu ām.

{{Par. 6}} peṇmi tirital um uṇṭu ēṉum āṇmi tirital perumpāṉmi ākalāṉ {{"āṇmi tirinta"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 7}} {pēṭiyar pēṭimār pēṭikaḷ} eṉpaṉa um aṭaṅkutaṟku+ `pēṭi eṉṉumpeyar +nili+ kiḷavi' eṉṉātu {{"peṇmi cuṭṭiya āṇmi tirintapeyar nili+ kiḷavi"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 8}} {{"peṇmi cuṭṭiya"}} eṉṉum peyareccam {{"peyar nili+kiḷavi"}} eṉṉum peyar koṇṭatu. {{"āṇmi tirinta"}} eṉpatuiṭi nili.

{{Par. 9}} {{"taṉmi tiri peyar"}} {{[col:56]}} eṉṟāṟpōla+ colloṭuporuṭku oṟṟumi karuti {{"āṇmi tirinta peyar nili+kiḷavi"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 10}} {{"uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ+ pāl pirintu icikkum"}} eṉa ē tamakkueṉa vēṟu īṟu iṉmi peṟappaṭutaliṉ, {{"iv= eṉa aṟiyum antamtamakku ila ē"}} eṉal vēṇṭā eṉiṉ:-- tamakku eṉa vēṟu īṟuuṭiya āy āṭūuvaṟicol mutal āyiṉavaṟṟiṟku uriya īṟṟāṉ umicikkum^ kol eṉṉum aiyam nīkkutaṟku, {{"antam tamakku ilaē"}} eṉal vēṇṭum eṉpatu.

{{Par. 11}} pāl uḷ aṭaṅkāta pēṭiyi um, tiṇi uḷ aṭaṅkāta teyvatti um,pāl uḷ= um tiṇi uḷ= um aṭakkiya āṟu.

{{Par. 12}} {{"cuṭṭiya"}} eṉpatu ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉiyeccam eṉṟu* -um,āṇmi tirital coṟku iṉmiyiṉ {{"peyar nili+ kiḷavi"}}eṉpatu ākupeyar āy+ poruḷ% mēl niṉṟatu* eṉṟu* -umuriyāciriyār kūṟiṉār āl eṉiṉ: -- āṇmi tirital peṇmi+ taṉmi eytutal+ poruṭṭu aṉṟi+pēṭikku iyalpu ākaliṉ peṇmi cuṭṭa vēṇṭi āṇmi tirinta eṉṟalporuntāmiyāṉ um, poruḷ ē kūṟal uṟṟār āyiṉ āciriyar pēṭi umteyvam um eṉṟu tām karutiya poruḷ iṉitu viḷaṅka+ curuṅkiyavāypāṭṭāṉ+ cūttirippār ākalāṉ um, avarkku atu karuttuaṉmiyāṉ, uriyāciriyarkku um atu karuttu aṉṟu eṉka.

{{Par. 7}} `ciṟappu uṭi+ poruḷi+ tāṉ iṉitu kiḷattal' eṉpataṉāṉ aimpāl uṇarttutal+ ciṟappu uṭiya paṭarkki viṉi paṟṟi ōtiṉārēṉum, {{"taṉmi+ col= ē aḵṟiṇi+ kiḷavi"}} eṉṟu* -um {{"muṉṉilicuṭṭiya orumi+ kiḷavi"}} {{[462]}} eṉṟu* -um peyar vaḻuamippār ākaliṉ, taṉmi muṉṉili+ pāl aṟi kiḷavi ummayaṅkaṟka eṉpatu īṇṭi+ koḷḷappaṭum.

{{Par. 8}} u-m. {yāṉ vantēṉ; yām vantēm.} e-m. {nī vantāy; nīyirvantīr.} e-m. varum. {yāṉ vantēm; nīyir vantāy} eṉṉum^toṭakkattaṉa vaḻu ām.

{{Par. 9}} mayaṅkal kūṭā eṉṟatu mayaṅkutali+ poruntā eṉṟa āṟu.mayaṅkal kūṭā, tam marapiṉa ē eṉpaṉavaṟṟu uḷ oṉṟaṉatuāṟṟalāṉ ēṉiyataṉ poruḷ um uṇarappaṭutaliṉ oṉṟu ē amiyumeṉiṉ:-- col= il vaḻiyatu uyttuṇarvatu eṉka.

{{Par. 10}} tam marapiṉa ē eṉpataṉi+ pirittu vēṟu ōr toṭar ākki+coṟkaḷ% marapu piṟaḻā tam marapiṉa ē eṉa marapu-vaḻu+kāttatu āka urikka. itu yōkavipākam eṉṉum* nūṟpuṇarppu.muṉ= iru poruḷ paṭa urippaṉa ellām i+ nūṟpuṇarppu āka+koḷka. {yāṉi mēyppāṉi+ pākaṉ} eṉṟal um, {yāṭu mēyppāṉiiṭiyaṉ} eṉṟal um marapu. mēyttal oppumiyāṉ {yāṉimēyppāṉi iṭiyaṉ} eṉṟal um, {yāṭu mēyppāṉi+ pākaṉ} eṉṟalum marapu vaḻu.

{{Par. 11}} ceppu vaḻā nili um, viṉā vaḻā nili um, ciṟappu vakiyāṉōtappaṭum iṭa vaḻā nili um, ivvāṟu ōtappaṭu marapu vaḻānili um oḻittu, oḻintaṉa i+ cūttirattāṉ+ kāttār.((11))

(("pēṭi" eṉṉum col immuṟiyil amiyum vaki))

[TRANSLATION]

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ. ceppiṉi um viṉāviṉi um vaḻuvāmal+ pōṟṟuka. e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} ceppu eṉpatu viṉāya poruḷi aṟivuṟuppatu. aḵtu iraṇṭuvakippaṭum, cevvaṉ iṟi um iṟi payappatu um eṉa. {uyir e+taṉmittu} eṉṟu viṉāyavaḻi {uṇartal+ taṉmittu} eṉṟal cevvaṉiṟi ām. {uṇṭi ō} eṉṟu viṉāyavaḻi {vayiṟu kuttiṟṟu} eṉṟal,{uṇṇēṉ} eṉpatu payantamiyiṉ, iṟi payappatu ām.

{{Par. 3}} {karuvūrkku+ cellāy ō cāttā} eṉa+ {paru nūl paṉṉiru toṭi}eṉpatu ceppu vaḻu ām. {col= e+ poruḷ uṇarttum} eṉṟuviṉāyavaḻi+ {col= oru poruḷ um uṇarttātu} eṉpatu um atu,viṉāya poruḷi viṉāvāl uṇarntu tāṉ kuṟitta poruḷi+ ceppāluṇarttukiṉṟāṉ ākaliṉ eṉpatu.

{{Par. 4}} viṉā iṉṟi um^ ceppu nikaḻtaliṉ viṉāya poruḷi eṉal vēṇṭāeṉiṉ, viṉāya poruḷi eṉṉātu aṟivuṟuppatu ceppu eṉiṉ, aṟiyaluṟaviṉi aṟivuṟuttaliṉ viṉā um^ ceppu āy aṭaṅkutalāṉ um,ceppu eṉpatu uttaram eṉpataṉōṭu oru poruḷ+ kiḷavi ākalāṉ um,viṉāya poruḷi aṟivuṟuttal ē ilakkaṇam ām eṉpatu. {kumariāṭi+ pōntēṉ cōṟu tammiṉ} eṉa viṉā iṉṟi nikaḻnta col yāṇṭuaṭaṅkum eṉiṉ:-- aṟiyal uṟutali uṇarttātu oṉṟaṉi aṟivuṟuttuniṟṟaliṉ+ ceppiṉ pāṟpaṭum.

{{Par. 5}} viṉā etirviṉātal, ēval, maṟuttal, uṟṟatu urittal, uṟuvatukūṟal, uṭampaṭutal eṉa+ ceppu aṟu vakippaṭum eṉṟuuriyāciriyar kūṟiṉār āl eṉiṉ:-- {uyir e+ taṉmittu}eṉṟavaḻi {uṇartal+ taṉmittu} eṉṟal mutal āyiṉa avaṟṟu uḷaṭaṅkāmiyāṉ um, maṟuttal um uṭampaṭutal um viṉāvappaṭṭārkaṇaṉṟi ēvappaṭṭārkaṇṇa ākalāṉ um, aṟu vakippaṭum eṉṟu piṟarmatam* mēṟkoṇṭu kūṟiṉār eṉpatu.

{{Par. 6}} viṉā -~āvatu aṟiyal uṟavu veḷippaṭuppatu. atu mūvakippaṭum: aṟiyāṉ viṉā um, aiya viṉā um, aṟi poruḷ viṉā umeṉa. aṟiyāṉ viṉā {uyir e+ taṉmittu} eṉpatu. oru-puṭi ~āṉumaṟiyappaṭāta poruḷ viṉāvappaṭāmiyiṉ+ potu vakiyāṉaṟiyappaṭṭu+ ciṟappu vakiyāṉ aṟiyappaṭāmi nōkki aṟiyāṉviṉā āyiṟṟu. aiya viṉā {kuṟṟi ō makaṉ ō tōṉṟukiṉṟa uru}eṉpatu. aṟi poruḷ viṉā aṟiyappaṭṭa poruḷi ē oru payaṉ*^nōkki av vāypāṭṭiṉ oṉṟaṉ viṉāvuvatu. payaṉ vēṟu aṟital umaṟivuṟuttal um* mutal āyiṉa. {kaṟakkiṉṟa erumi pāl ō ciṉiō} eṉpatu viṉā vaḻu ām. {oru poruḷ kāṭṭi itu neṭitu ō kuṟituō} eṉpatu um atu.

{{Par. 7}} uriyāciriyar aṟi poruḷ viṉāvi aṟivu oppukkāṇṭal um, avaṉaṟivu tāṉ kāṇṭal um, mey= avaṟku+ kāṭṭal um eṉa virittu,ēṉiya kūṭṭi, aintu eṉṟār.

{{Par. 8}} iru vaki+ ceppiṉ um, mū vaki viṉāviṉ um, cevvaṉ iṟi um,aṟiyāṉ viṉā um, aiya viṉā um vaḻā nili ākaliṉ, vaḻāal ōmpaleṉpataṉāṉ+ koḷḷappaṭum. ēṉiya vaḻu amippuḻi+ kāṇappaṭum.viṉā vaḻīiyiṉa iṭattu amiyātu eṉṟu uriyāciriyar kūṟiṉār āleṉiṉ:-- aṟṟu aṉṟu: {{"yātu eṉa varūum viṉāviṉ kiḷavi"}} eṉa-~um {{"vaṉpuṟa varūum viṉā uṭi viṉiccol"}} eṉa -~ummuṉṉar vaḻu amippar ākalāṉ, atu pōli uri eṉka.((13))

«muḷ+ tāḻi»,

{{[naṟṟiṇi:78_1]}} eṉa+ tici um uṟuppu um^ toḻil um* mutal āya aṭi aṭuttuiṉam^ cuṭṭātu varuvaṉa oṉṟu eṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāṉ+ ceyyuḷāṟu eṉa amittu+ koḷḷappaṭum.

{{Par. 6}} oṉṟaṉatu āṟṟalāṉ oṉṟu peṟappaṭutaliṉ, vaḻakku āṟu alla eṉṟu*āṉum^ ceyyuḷ āṟu eṉṟu* āṉum kūṟa amiyum eṉiṉ:-- uyttuuṇarvatu col= il vaḻi eṉa maṟukka.

{{Par. 7}} paṇpu koḷ peyar iṉam^ kuṟittu varutal+ marapu; a+ marapuvaḻakkiṉkaṇ vaḻuvaṟka eṉṟu* -um, ceyyuṭkaṇ vaḻuvamikkaeṉṟu* -um^ kātta āṟu āyiṟṟu.((18))

TC 21 c

[TRANSLATION]

ākkam^ tāṉ ē kāraṇa mutaṟṟu ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ. ceyaṟki+ poruḷi ākkamoṭu kūṟuṅkāl, kāraṇatti muṉ+kūṟi ataṉ vaḻi ākkam^ kūṟuka; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} {{"tāṉ -ē"}} eṉpatu ceyyuḷ+ cuvi kuṟittu-niṉṟatu.

{{Par. 3}} e-ṭu: {kaṭu+ kalanta kipiḻi eṇṇey peṟṟamiyāṉ mayir nallaāyiṉa}; {eru+ peytu iḷam^ kaḷi kaṭṭu nīr kāl yāttamiyālpim^ kūḻ nalla āyiṉa}; {nīr kalattalāl nilam melituāyiṟṟu}; {tī+ cārtalāl nīr veytu āyiṟṟu} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 4}} {mayir nalla, payir nalla} eṉa+ ceyaṟki+ poruḷ ākkam peṟātuvantaṉa āl eṉiṉ:-- a+ {naṉmi} poruṭku+ piṉ tōṉṟātu uṭaṉtōṉṟiṟṟu ēl iyaṟki ē ām; avvāṟu aṉṟi, {muṉ tīya āy+ piṉnalla āyiṉa} ēṉum, {tīya nilimi kāṇātāṉ mayir nalla mayirnalla eṉṟāl} paṭum iḻukku eṉṉi? atu ceyaṟki ~āvatuuṇarntāṉ ākkam^ koṭātu colliṉ aṉṟē vaḻu ~āvatu eṉka.

{{Par. 5}} ceyaṟki+ poruḷi ākkam um kāraṇam um^ koṭuttu+ collukaeṉpatu ē karuttu āyiṉ cūttiram oṉṟu ākal+ pāṟṟu eṉiṉ:--avvāṟu ōtiṉ, ##ākkam -um^ kāraṇam -um^ ceyaṟki+-poruṭkaṇotta ~āyiṉa# eṉpatu paṭṭu+ {{"kāraṇam iṉṟi -~um pōkku*iṉṟu"}} {{[col:22]}} eṉpataṉōṭu māṟukōṭalāṉ -um, kāraṇam^ceyaṟki+-poruḷatu* -ō ākkattatu* -ō ~eṉa aiyuṟa+-paṭutalāṉ-um, {{"ākkam^ tāṉ ē kāraṇa mutaṟṟu*"}} eṉa+ pirittu+kūṟiṉār eṉpatu.

{{Par. 6}} iyaṟki+ poruḷ ākkam um kāraṇam um peṟātu varutal umceyaṟki+ poruḷ avi peṟṟu varutal um ilakkaṇam eṉa ē,ivvāṟu aṉṟi varuvaṉa marapu vaḻu eṉpatu ām.

{{Par. 7}} nuṇ= uṇarvu uṭiyārkku+ {{"tam marapiṉa ē"}} {{[col:11]}}eṉa aṭaṅkuva āyiṉum, ēṉi uṇarviṉārkku iv vēṟupāṭu uṇaralākāmiyiṉ virittu+ kūṟiṉār.((21))

TC 22 c

[TRANSLATION]

ākka+ kiḷavi kāraṇam iṉṟi ~um
pōkku iṉṟu eṉpa vaḻakkiṉ uḷ= ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ. kāraṇa mutaṟṟu eṉappaṭṭa ākka+ col kāraṇam iṉṟivariṉum^ kuṟṟam iṉṟu vaḻakkiṉ uḷ; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu. {mayir nalla āyiṉa, payir nalla āyiṉa} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 3}} vaḻakkiṉ uḷ+ kāraṇam iṉṟi ~um varum eṉa ē, ceyyuḷ uḷ+kāraṇam peṟṟu* -ē -varum eṉpatu ām.

{{Par. 4}} e-ṭu.
«paricilarkku aruṅkala nalka um^ kuricil
valiya ākum niṉ tāḷ tōy taṭa+ ki»
{{[puṟam:14_10/11]}} eṉa -~um,
«teri kaṇi eḵkam tiṟanta vāy ellām
kuruti paṭintu uṇṭa kākam -- uru iḻantu
kukkil+ puṟatta ciral vāya cem^ kaṇ māl
tappiyār aṭṭa kaḷattu»,
{{[kaḷavaḻi:5]}}
«varu maḻiya vāy koḷḷum vāṭā+ cīr vaṇ ki+
karu murukaṉ cūṭiya kaṇṇi -- tiru nutāl
iṉṟu eṉ kuraṟ-kūntal peytamiyāl paṇṭi+ taṉ
cāyala āyiṉa tōḷ» eṉa -~um kāraṇam peṟṟu vanta āṟu kaṇṭu koḷka. «kukkil+ puṟatta ciralvāya» eṉa ākkam iṉṟi vantaṉa āl eṉiṉ:-- tokkum vaḻi+tokuttal eṉpataṉāṉ āṇṭu ākkam^ tokku-niṉṟatu eṉpatu.

eḷiya ākiya taṭa mel% tōḷ ē»
{{[kuṟun:77]}} eṉa -~um,
«nallavi ellāam^ tīya ām^ tīya um
nalla ām^ celvam^ ceyaṟku»
{{[kuṟaḷ:305]}} eṉa -~um, ceyaṟki+ poruḷ ceyyuḷ uḷ kāraṇam iṉṟi vantaṉa āl eṉiṉ: -- {kaḷavu+ kālattu ariyaṉa i+ kālattu eḷiya āyiṉa} eṉpatukaruttu ākalāṉ, {i+ kālam^} kāraṇam eṉpatu peṟappaṭutalāṉum, {ūḻāl} eṉpatu atikārattāṉ varum ākalāṉ um, avi kāraṇamiṉṟi vantaṉa eṉappaṭā eṉpatu.((22))

TC 24 c

[TRANSLATION]

urupu eṉa moḻiyiṉum aḵṟiṇi+ pirippiṉ um
iru vīṟṟu um urittu ē cuṭṭum^ kāli.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ. urupu eṉa+ collum iṭattu um, orumi um paṉmi um āka+pirikkappaṭum aḵṟiṇi+ potu+ coṟkaṇ= um, iv= iru kūṟṟiṉ umaiyappula+ potu+ col= ātal urittu; e-ṟu.

{{C CENV}} eṉa ē, {āṇmakaṉ kol= ō peṇṭāṭṭikol+ ō tōṉṟāniṉṟa urupu} eṉa-~um, {oṉṟukol+ ō palakol+ ō cey pukka urupu} eṉa -~um, {kuṟṟikol= ō makaṉ kol= ō tōṉṟāniṉṟa urupu} eṉa -~um, {oṉṟu kol= ōpala kol= ō cey pukka peṟṟam} eṉa -~um colluka eṉpatu ām.

{{Par. 2}} aiya+ kiḷavi eṉpatu atikārattāṉ+ peṟṟām.

{{Par. 3}} `urupiṉ um' eṉṉātu {{"urupu eṉa moḻiyiṉum eṉṟataṉāṉ"}},{urupiṉ} poruḷa ākiya {vaṭivu piḻampu piṇṭam} eṉṉum^toṭakkattaṉa um koḷka.

{{Par. 4}} paṉmi kūṟal uyartiṇi+ pāl aiyattiṟku urittu eṉṟu* -um,urupu eṉa moḻital tiṇi aiyattiṟku urittu eṉṟu* -um,uriyāciriyar kūṟiṉār āl eṉiṉ:-- avi avaṟṟiṟku ē uriyaāyiṉ, aḵṟiṇi+ pirippu eṉṟāṟpōla uyartiṇi+ pāl% mayakkuuṟṟa eṉṟu* -um, tiṇi aiyattu eṉṟu* -um, vitantu ōtuvarāciriyar; avvāṟu ōtāmiyāṉ um, naṭi uḷ avi potu āyvarutalāṉ um, avi pōliyuri eṉka.

{{Par. 5}} {oruvaṉ kol= ō palar kol= ō} eṉṟal+ ^toṭakkattu uyartiṇi+paṉmi orumi+ pāl aiyattiṟku {urupu} mutal āyiṉa ēlāmi um,tiṇi aiyattiṟku um ēṉi+ pāl aiyattiṟku um ēṟpuṭimi umcuṭṭi uṇarka eṉpatu pōtara+ {{"cuṭṭum^ kāli"}} eṉṟār.cuṭṭutal -- karututal.((24))

((avi, tuṇintapiṉ amiyum vaki))

TC 25 c

[TRANSLATION]

taṉmi cuṭṭal um urittu eṉa moḻipa
aṉmi+ kiḷavi vēṟu iṭattāṉa.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ. aiyuṟṟu+ tuṇiyum vaḻi aṉmi+ kiḷavi aṉmi+ taṉmiyi+cuṭṭi niṟṟal um urittu eṉṟu colluvar. tuṇintu taḻīi+koḷḷappaṭṭa poruḷiṉ vēṟu āya poruḷ iṭattu; e-ṟu.

{{C CENV}} eṉṟatu, {kuṟṟi kol= ō makaṉ kol= ō} eṉṟu* āṉum, {oruvaṉ kol=-ō orutti kol= -ō} eṉṟu* -āṉum, {oṉṟu kol= -ō pala kol= -ō}eṉṟu* -āṉum, aiyam nikaḻntavaḻi+ tuṇital uṇṭu; piṟa naṭi uḷtuṇintavaḻi maṟukka+-paṭum poruḷ% mēl aṉmi+-taṉmi ēṟṟiṉumamiyum eṉṟa -~āṟu.

{{Par. 2}} {makaṉ} eṉṟu tuṇintavaḻi+ {kuṟṟi aṉṟu makaṉ} eṉa -~um,{kuṟṟi} eṉṟu tuṇintavaḻi {makaṉ allaṉ kuṟṟi} eṉa -~um,{āṇmakaṉ} eṉṟu tuṇintavaḻi+ {peṇṭāṭṭi allaḷ āṇmakaṉ} eṉa-~um, {peṇṭāṭṭi} eṉṟu tuṇintavaḻi {āṇmakaṉ allaṉ peṇṭāṭṭi}eṉa -~um, {oṉṟu} eṉṟu tuṇintavaḻi+ {pala alla oṉṟu} eṉa-~um, {pala} eṉṟu tuṇinta vaḻi {oṉṟu aṉṟu pala} eṉa -~ummaṟukkappaṭum poruḷ% mēl aṉmi+ kiḷavi aṉmi+ taṉmiyi+cuṭṭi-niṉṟa -~āṟu.

{{Par. 3}} ummi etirmaṟi ākalāṉ, vēṟu iṭattu+ cuṭṭāmi ē perumpāṉmieṉpatu ām. āka ē, {iv= urupu kuṟṟi aṉṟu makaṉ} eṉa aiyampulam ākiya potu+ poruḷ% mēl āṉum, {kuṟṟi allaṉ makaṉ} eṉa+tuṇi poruḷ mēl āṉum aṉmi+ kiḷavi aṉmi cuṭṭi niṟṟalperumpāṉmi eṉpatu ām; eṉṉi? vēṟu*-iṭattu+ cuṭṭāmi -~āvatuīṇṭu+ cuṭṭal -ē ~ākalāṉ.

{{Par. 4}} {kuṟṟi aṉṟu makaṉ} eṉṟavaḻi {makaṉ} eṉpatu niṉṟu vaṟṟumeṉiṉ:
-- {{"evvayiṉ+ peyar um veḷippaṭa+ tōṉṟi
av= iyal niliyal cevvitu eṉpa."}}
{{[col:68]}} eṉpataṉāṉ eḻuvāy veḷippaṭātu niṟṟal um uṇmiyāṉ,veḷippaṭātu-niṉṟa {ivaṉ} eṉṉum eḻuvāykku* -um atu payaniliākaliṉ, niṉṟu vaṟṟutal yāṇṭiyatu eṉka allaṉavaṟṟiṟku um ītuokkum.

{{Par. 5}} {kuṟṟi allaṉ} eṉpuḻi+ {kuṟṟi} eṉpatu {allaṉ} eṉṉum^ collōṭuiyinta āṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ:-- {iv= urupu kuṟṟi ām} eṉṟavaḻi āmeṉpataṉōṭu {kuṟṟi} eṉpatūum eḻuvāy āy-niṉṟu* -ēiyintāṟpōla, {ivaṉ kuṟṟi allaṉ} eṉpuḻi {ām} eṉpataṉetirmaṟi ākiya aṉmi+ kiḷaviyōṭu um {kuṟṟi} eṉpatu eḻuvāyāy-niṉṟu* -ē iyiyum eṉpatu. eḻuvāy āy iyitaliṉ aṉṟē {yāṉnī allaṉ} eṉpuḻi {nī} eṉpatu vēṟṟumikku ēṟṟa ceykipeṟātu-niṉṟatu eṉka.((25))

((vaṇṇa+ ciṉiccol amiyum vaki))

TC 26 c

[TRANSLATION]

aṭi ciṉi mutal eṉa muṟi mūṉṟu um mayaṅkāmi
naṭi peṟṟu iyalum vaṇṇa+ ciṉi+ col

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ. paṇpuccol= um, ciṉiccol= um, mutaṟcol= um eṉa mūṉṟuum^ kūṟappaṭṭa muṟi mayaṅkāmal, vaḻakki+ porunti naṭakkum,vaṇṇa+ collōṭu toṭarnta ciṉi+ colli uṭiya mutaṟcol; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} vaṭivu mutal ākiya piṟa paṇpu um uḷa ēṉum, vaṇṇa+ paṇpiṉatuvaḻakku+ payiṟci nōkki {{"vaṇṇa+ ciṉi+ col"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 3}} e-ṭu. {cem^ kāl nāri, perum^ tali+ cāttaṉ} eṉa varum. {kālcem^ nāri, tali perum^ cāttaṉ} eṉa muṟi mayaṅki variṉ,marapu vaḻu ām eṉka.

{{Par. 4}} vaḻakkiṉ uḷ mayaṅkātu varum eṉa ē,
«kavi cem^ tāḻi+ kuvi puṟattu irunta
cevi cem^ cēval um pokuval um veruvā
vāy val% kākki um^ kūki um^ kūṭi»
{{[puṟam:238_1/2/3]}} eṉa+ ceyyuḷ uḷ mayaṅki um vara+ peṟum eṉṟa āṟu ām. {cevicem^ cēval, vāy val% kākki} eṉpaṉa, {cem^ cevi+ cēval, valvāy+ kākki} eṉṉum poruḷ paṭa-niṉṟamiyiṉ, vaṇṇa+ ciṉiccolmayakkam āyiṉa. aḵtēl, ciṉi aṭi ākiya {cemmi um vaṉmium} mutal aṭi āyiṉa āṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ:-- ciṉiyoṭu mutaṟkuoṟṟumi uṇmiyāṉ avi mutaloṭu um iyipu uṭiya eṉpatu.

{{Par. 5}}
«perum^ tōḷ ciṟu nucuppiṉ pēr amar+ kaṇ pēti» eṉpuḻi mūṉṟām vaḻi mutal kiṭavātu piṉ= um aṭi um^ ciṉi umpuṇarttamiyāṉ vaṇṇa+ ciṉiccol ceyyuḷ uḷ mayaṅki vantatueṉṟu uriyāciriyar kūṟiṉār āl eṉiṉ:-- mūṉṟām vaḻi+puṇarkkappaṭum «pēti» eṉṉum mutaṟcol «pēr amar+ kaṇ» eṉṉum^tokiyōṭu vēṟṟumi+ poruḷpaṭa+ tokku, attoki ciṟu nucuppueṉṉum^ tokiyōṭu um a+ poruḷpaṭa+ tokku, oru col= āy,
«perum^ tōḷ+ pal yākacāli mutu kuṭumi+ peru vaḻuti»eṉṟāṟpōla mūṉṟām vaḻi+ piṟa col= aṭuttu+ «pēti» eṉṉum mutalkiṭantatu eṉa ē paṭutaliṉ, mayakkam iṉmiyāṉ, avarkku atukaruttu aṉṟu eṉka. aṉṟi+ «perum^ tōḷ» mutal ākiya mūṉṟu*-um pala peyar ummi+ tokipaṭa+ tam= -uḷ tokku oru col= āy+piṉ «pēti» eṉpataṉōṭu vēṟṟumi+ tokipaṭa+ tokkaṉa eṉiṉum,avi ummi+ tokipaṭa+ tokātu-niṉṟu «pēti» eṉpataṉōṭuvēṟṟumi+ tokipaṭa oruṅku tokkaṉa eṉiṉum, tam= uḷ iyiyātu«pēti» eṉpataṉōṭu iyitaliṉ, āṇṭu um mayakkam iṉmi aṟika.aḵtēl, ivvāṟu varutal vaḻakkiṟku um urittu ō eṉiṉ:--aṭukkiya aṭi -~um ciṉi -~um potumi nīkkutaṟku* aṉṟi aṇikuṟittu-niṟṟaliṉ ceyyuṭku* -ē ~urittu* eṉpatu.
«ciṟu pim^ tūvi»
{{[akam:57]}} eṉa+ ciṉiyoṭu kuṇam iraṇṭu aṭukki varutal ceyyuṭku urittueṉṟu* -um, {iḷam perum^ kūttaṉ} eṉa mutaloṭu kuṇam iraṇṭuaṭukki varutal vaḻakkiṟku urittu eṉṟu* -um, piṟar matamēṟkoṇṭu kūṟiṉār. oṉṟu āka pala āka iṉam^ cuṭṭātaṉa ceyyuṭkuuriya ām, iṉam^ cuṭṭi-niṉṟaṉa vaḻakkiṟku uriya ām eṉpatu ēuriyāciriyar karuttu eṉka. aṉṟi+ `piṟa nūl% muṭintatu tāṉuṭaṉpaṭutal' eṉpataṉāl ciṉiyoṭu kuṇam iraṇṭu* aṭukkalceyyuḷ -āru eṉṟu koḷḷiṉum amiyum;
`mutaloṭu kuṇam iraṇṭu aṭukkal vaḻakku iyal
ciṉiyōṭu aṭukkal ceyyuḷ āṟu ē'
{{[ANON]}} eṉṟār ākaliṉ.

{{Par. 6}} vaṇṇa+ ciṉi+ col eṉṟataṉāl vaṇṇam um ciṉi um mutal um^kūṟutal+ kaṇṇatu iv= ārāycci eṉpatu ām. eṉa ē {iḷam perum^kūttaṉ, perum palā+ kōṭu} eṉṉum^ toṭakkattaṉa vēṇṭiya āṟuvara+ peṟum eṉṟa āṟu.

{{Par. 7}} mayaṅkātu varuka eṉa marapu-vaḻu+ kāttavāṟu.

((26))

((pāl vaḻuvamiti))

TC 27 c

[TRANSLATION]

oruvari+ kūṟum paṉmi+ kiḷavi um
oṉṟaṉi+ kūṟum paṉmi+ kiḷavi um
vaḻakkiṉ ākiya uyar col+ kiḷavi
ilakkaṇa maruṅkiṉ col= āṟu alla

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ. oruvaṉi um oruttiyi um^ collum paṉmi+ col= um,oṉṟaṉi+ collum paṉmi+ col= um, vaḻakkiṉkaṇ uyarttu+collum^ col= ām, ilakkaṇa muṟimiyāṉ+ collum neṟi alla;e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu. {yām vantēm, nīyir vantīr, ivar vantār} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 3}} uyar col uyarkkum^ col.

{{Par. 4}} uyar col+ kiḷavi eṉpuḻi+ kūṟiyatu kūṟal aṉmi paṇpu+ tokiārāyccikkaṇ collutum.

{{Par. 5}} {tām vantār toṇṭaṉār} eṉa+ paṉmi+ kiḷavi iḻittaṟkaṇ= umvantatu āl eṉiṉ:-- āṇṭu uyar col tāṉ ē kuṟippu nili āyiḻipu viḷakkiṟṟu eṉpatu.

{{Par. 6}} {{"ilakkaṇa maruṅkiṉ col= āṟu alla"}} eṉṟataṉāṉ ilakkaṇamaṉmi um, {{"vaḻakkiṉ ākiya uyar col+ kiḷavi"}} eṉṟataṉāṉvaḻu aṉmi um^ kūṟiṉār; kūṟa ē, vaḻu amiti eṉṟa āṟu ām.aḵtēl, {{"vaḻakkiṉ ākiya uyar col+ kiḷavi"}} eṉa ē vaḻuamiti eṉpatu peṟutum; {{"ilakkaṇa maruṅkiṉ col= āṟualla"}} eṉal vēṇṭā eṉiṉ: -- aṅṅaṉam^ kūṟiṉ+ ceyyuḷ varinta āṟu ō vaḻu amitta āṟu ōeṉa aiyam ākkaliṉ, avvāṟu kūṟal vēṇṭum eṉpatu.

{{Par. 7}} {{"paṉmi+ kiḷavi um"}} eṉa+ potu vakiyāl kūṟiṉār ēṉum,uyarttaṟkaṇ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varum uyartiṇi+ paṉmi um viravu+paṉmi um ē koḷḷappaṭum eṉṟaṟku {{"vaḻakkiṉ ākiya"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 8}} taṉ= iṉam muṭittal eṉpataṉāṉ {erutti um āvi um}, {entivantāṉ, emmaṉṉi vantāḷ} eṉa uyartiṇi ā ~uyarttu vaḻaṅkalum, oṉṟu eṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāl
«kaṉṉi ñāḻal»,
{{[// cilappatikāram:7_((9))_2]}}
«kaṉṉi eyil» eṉa aḵṟiṇi āy-niṉṟu uyartiṇi vāypāṭṭāl kūṟa+-paṭutal -um,paṇpu koḷ peyar+ koṭi, vaḻakkiṉ akattu um, {perum^ koṟṟaṉ,perum^ cāttaṉ} eṉa il kuṇam aṭuttu uyar col= āy varutal um^koḷka.((27))

celaviṉ um varaviṉ um^ taraviṉ um^ koṭiyiṉ um

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ. {celavu} mutal ākiya nāṉku toḻiṟkaṇ= um nili peṟa+pulappaṭāniṉṟa a+ nāṉku col= um, taṉmi muṉṉili paṭarkkieṉṉum a+ mū iṭattiṟku um uriya āy varum; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 5}} i+ cūttirattu uḷ {īṅku} mutal āyiṉa taṉmikkaṇ= um, {āṅku}mutal āyiṉa paṭarkkikkaṇ= um aṭakkappaṭṭaṉa.

{{Par. 6}} viṉiccol mūṉṟu iṭattiṟku um uriya ātal viṉi iyal uḷpeṟappaṭutaliṉ īṇṭu+ kūṟal vēṇṭā eṉiṉ:-- āṇṭu+ pāl uṇarttumīṟṟāṉ iṭattiṟku* urimi kūṟiṉār, īṇṭu īṟṟāṉ aṉṟi+ {celavu}mutal āyiṉa mutaṉili tām -ē iṭam^ kuṟittu-niṟṟal uṭimiyāṉiv vēṟupāṭu āṇṭu+ peṟappaṭāmiyiṉ īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉār eṉpatu.aḵtēl, {{"taru col varu col āy iru kiḷavi -~um^ taṉmimuṉṉili āy īr iṭatta"}}, `paṭarkki iṭatta koṭi+ col= um^celavu+ col= um' eṉa ōtuka, i+ cūttiram vēṇṭā eṉiṉ: -- aṅṅaṉam ōtiṉ, iṭam^ cuṭṭum mutaṉili i+ nāṉku* -um -ēeṉṉum variyaṟi peṟappaṭāmiyiṉ, itu vēṇṭum eṉpatu. aḵtēl, {pōtal pukutal} eṉṉum^ toṭakkattaṉa -~um iṭam^cuṭṭutaliṉ nāṉku* eṉṉum variyaṟi amiyātu* eṉiṉ: -- avi ikkālattu+ cila iṭattu+ payiṉṟu-varum āyiṉum mūṉṟuiṭattiṟku* -um potu ~ākaliṉ aṉṟē, āciriyar {{"a+ nāl+ col=um"}} eṉa ivaṟṟi -~ē varintu* ōtuvār āyiṟṟu eṉpa. {{Par. 3}} {tarappaṭum poruḷi ēṟpāṉ} tāṉ um muṉṉiṉṟāṉ um ākalāṉ um,{varavu} toḻil taṉkaṇ= um muṉṉiṉṟāṉkaṇ= um ceṉṟu muṭitalāṉum, īṟṟāṉ aṉṟi iv= iru col= um taṉmi muṉṉilikku* uriyaāyiṉa āṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.

((avaṟṟuḷ yātu eṉṉum col))

TC 32 c

[TRANSLATION]

avaṟṟu uḷ
yātu eṉa varūum viṉāviṉ kiḷavi

{{Par. 2}} e-ṭu. {i+ maraṅkaḷ uḷ karuṅkāli yātu, nam= erutu* aintaṉ uḷkeṭṭa erutu yātu} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 3}} {namar uḷ yāvar pōyiṉār, avaṟṟu* -uḷ ev= erutu keṭṭatu} eṉa+piṟa -~um aṟinta poruḷ vayiṉ aiyam^ tīrtaṟku+ {{"terintakiḷavi"}} āy varutaliṉ, avi -~um īṇṭu amikkal+-pālaeṉiṉ:-- avi aṟinta poruḷ vayiṉ aiyam^ tīrtaṟku allatuyāṇṭu* -um vārāmiyiṉ, īṇṭu amikka+-paṭā eṉpatu.

{{Par. 4}} ataṉāl viṉā-vaḻu amittār. ((32))

((ciṉi mutal+ col amiyum vaki))

TC 33 c

[TRANSLATION]

iṉittu eṉa aṟinta ciṉi mutal+ kiḷavikku
viṉi+ paṭu tokutiyiṉ ummi vēṇṭum.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ. kēṭpōrāṉ ittuṇi eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭṭa ciṉi+ kiḷavikku ummutal+ kiḷavikku um viṉi+ paṭu tokutikkaṇ ummi koṭuttu+colluka; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}} e-ṭu.
«paṉṉiru ki um pāṟpaṭa iyaṟṟi»
{{[tirumuru:118]}} eṉa -~um,
«muracu muḻaṅku tāṉi mūvar um^ kūṭi»
{{[porunar:54]}} eṉa -~um varum.

{{Par. 3}} aṟinta ciṉi mutal+ kiḷavi eṉa ē, muṉ= aṟiyappaṭākkāl,{murukaṟku+ ki paṉṉiraṇṭu, tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟku vēntar mūvar} eṉa-~ummi peṟātu varum eṉpatu ām.

{{Par. 4}} {aim^ tali nākam uṭaṉṟatu, nāṉ maṟi mutalvar vantār}eṉpuḻi, iṉittu eṉa aṟinta ciṉi mutal+ kiḷaviyāṉ um, viṉi+paṭu tokuti aṉmiyiṉ, ummi peṟā āyiṉa. aḵtēl, {paṉṉiru kium} eṉpuḻi um tokuti+ peyar viṉiyoṭu toṭarātu {ki}eṉpataṉōṭu oṭṭi niṟṟaliṉ, viṉi+ paṭu tokuti aṉṟu ām piṟaeṉiṉ:-- oṭṭi-niṉṟatu* āyiṉum, {aim^ tali nākam, nāṉ maṟimutalvar} eṉpaṉa pōlātu iru col= um oru poruḷ% mēlvarutaliṉ, «ki» eṉpataṉōṭu iyinta «iyaṟṟi» eṉṉum viṉitokuti+ peyarōṭu um iyintatu ām; ataṉāṉ atu {{"viṉi+ paṭutokuti"}} ām eṉpatu. aḵtēl, {kaṇ= iraṇṭu um kuruṭu; erutuiraṇṭu um mūri} eṉa+ peyar koṇṭavaḻi ummi peṟum āṟu eṉṉieṉiṉ:-- peyar āka viṉi āka muṭikkum^ colloṭu paṭutali īṇṭuviṉi+ paṭutal eṉṟār ākaliṉ, avi um viṉi+ paṭu tokuti āmeṉka. {aim^ tali, nāṉ maṟi} eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku {nākam,mutalvar} eṉpaṉa muṭikkum^ col aṉmiyiṉ, viṉi+ paṭu tokutiākāmi uṇarka.

{{Par. 5}} `ciṟappu uṭi+ poruḷi+ tāṉ iṉitu kiḷattal' eṉpataṉāl ciṉimutal+ kiḷavi eṉṟār āyiṉum, ciṟavāta paṇpu mutal āyiṉa umkoḷḷappaṭum.

{{Par. 6}} e-ṭu. {cuvi āṟu um uṭittu iv= aṭicil, kati aintu umuṭittu i+ kutiri} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 7}}
«iru tōḷ tōḻar paṟṟa» eṉa ~um,
«oṇ kuḻi oṉṟu olki eruttu alippa» eṉa -~um ummi iṉṟi vantaṉa āl eṉiṉ:-- āṇṭu ummi ceyyuḷvikārattāl tokku-niṉṟaṉa eṉpatu. ((33))

{{Par. 2}}

{{Par. 3}} allatil+ eṉiṉ eṉa+ poruḷ paṟṟi ōtiṉar ākaliṉ, allatil+ eṉṉumvāypāṭu* -ē ~aṉṟi, {uḻuntu* aṉṟi ~illi, uḻuntu* -ē ~uḷḷatu}eṉa a+ poruḷ paṭuvaṉa ellām koḷka. {uḻuntu allatilli} eṉa+piṟitu poruḷ kūṟātu {payaṟu allatilli} eṉa a+ poruḷ kūṟiṉ,{payaṟu uḷḷa allaṉa illi} eṉa maṟutali+ poruḷ paṭṭu+ ceppuvaḻu ām āṟu aṟika.

{{Par. 4}} yātāṉ um āka allatil+ eṉiṉ piṟitu poruḷ kūṟuka eṉa eñcāmaltaḻīi yāppuṟuttaṟku {{"e+ poruḷ āyiṉum"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 5}} allatil eṉpataṟku+ taṉ= uḻi uḷḷatu allatu eṉṟu* -um, a+poruḷ allā+ piṟitu poruḷ kūṟal eṉpataṟku iṉa+ poruḷ kūṟukaeṉṟu* -um, urittār āl uriyāciriyar eṉiṉ:-- {payaṟu uḷa ō}eṉṟu viṉāya vaḻi+ {payaṟu illi} eṉṟāl+ paṭum vaḻu iṉmiyāṉum, uḷḷatu allatu eṉṟal karuttu āyiṉ āciriyar allatu eṉa+kuṟitta poruḷ viḷaṅkāmiyiṉ akappaṭa+ cūttiriyār ākalāṉ um,{pāmpuṇi+ karuṅkal+ um payaṟu um viṟpāṉ oruvaṉ uḻi+ ceṉṟu}{payaṟu uḷa ō} eṉṟavaḻi+ {pāmpuṇi+ karuṅkal allatu illi}eṉṟāl iṉa+ poruḷ kūṟāmiyāṉ+ paṭṭa iḻukku iṉmiyāṉ um, avipōliyuri eṉka. allatūum iṉa+ poruḷ kūṟuka eṉpatu ē karuttuāyiṉ, a+ poruḷ allā iṉa+ poruḷ kūṟal eṉṉātu piṟitu poruḷkūṟal eṉṉār āciriyar, ataṉāṉ um aḵtu uri aṉmi uṇarka.((35))

TC 38 c

[TRANSLATION]

iyaṟpeyar+ kiḷavi um cuṭṭu+ peyar+ kiḷavi um

{{Par. 4}} {{"oruṅku iyalum"}} eṉṟataṉāl avi oru poruḷ mēl varutalkoḷka.

{{Par. 5}} e-ṭu. {cāttaṉ avaṉ vantāṉ, cāttaṉ vantāṉ avaṉ pōyiṉāṉ} eṉa-~um, {cātti vantāḷ avaṭku+ pū+ koṭukka} eṉa -~um varum.

{{Par. 7}} piṟitu viṉi kōṭaṟkaṇ eṉa ē, {avaṉ cāttaṉ, cāttaṉ avaṉ} eṉaoṉṟaṟkoṉṟu payaṉili ātaṟkaṇ= um, oru poruḷ% mēl nikaḻum eṉaē, {avaṉ um^ cāttaṉ um vantār, cāttaṉ um avaṉ um vantār} eṉavēṟu poruḷa āy varutaṟkaṇ= um, yātu muṟpaṭa+ kūṟiṉum amiyumeṉpatu ām.

{{Par. 8}} {{"viṉi"}} eṉṟatu muṭikkum^ colli.

{{Par. 9}} cuṭṭuppeyar yāṇṭum iyaṟpeyar vaḻi+ kiḷakkappaṭum eṉayāppuṟuttaṟku, {{"muṟpaṭa+ kiḷavār"}} eṉṟu* -um,{{"iyaṟpeyar vaḻiya"}} eṉṟu* -um^ kūṟiṉār.

{{Par. 10}} cuṭṭuppeyar eṉṟār āyiṉum, akara ikara+ cuṭṭu+ peyar ē koḷka.

{{Par. 5}} itaṉāṉ vaḻakkiṉkaṇ marapu vaḻā nili um^ ceyyuṭkaṇ marapuvaḻu amiti um uṇarttiṉār. ((40))

{{WFW}} i-ḷ. oru poruḷi+ kuṟittu-vanta pala peyar+-coṟkaḷ, orutoḻil -ē muṭipu* -āka+ kūṟātu peyar -tōṟum vēṟu* ākiyatoḻilkaḷi+ koṭuttu muṭippiṉ, oru poruḷa ~āy oṉṟā; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} {āciriyaṉ pērūr kiḻāṉ ceyiṟṟiyaṉ iḷam^ kaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉ vantāṉ}eṉṉātu {āciriyaṉ vantāṉ pērūr kiḻāṉ uṇṭāṉ ceyiṟṟiyaṉ ceṉṟāṉ}eṉa vēṟu vēṟu toḻil kiḷanta vaḻi, {vantāṉ um uṇṭāṉ um ceṉṟāṉum} oruvaṉ ākātu vēṟu āy+ tōṉṟiya āṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.

{{Par. 3}} «enti varuka em perumāṉ varuka mintaṉ varuka maṇāḷaṉvaruka» eṉpuḻi+ kātal mutal āyiṉa paṟṟi oru toḻil pala kālvantami allatu vēṟṟu+ toḻil aṉmiyāṉ, oru toḻil kiḷattal ēām eṉpatu.

{{Par. 4}} īṇṭu+ {{"toḻil"}} eṉṟatu muṭikkum^ colli.

{{Par. 5}} {āciriyaṉ vantāṉ} eṉṟu oru-kāl+ kūṟi iṭi-~iṭṭu avaṉi -~ēpiṉ= oru-kāl {pērūr kiḻāṉ ceṉṟāṉ} eṉṟu kūṟiyavaḻi, oru toṭaraṉmiyāṉ āṇṭu ārāycci illi eṉpatu. aḵtēl, {āciriyaṉ vantāṉpērūr kiḻāṉ uṇṭāṉ ceyiṟṟiyaṉ ceṉṟāṉ} eṉa iṭi-~īṭuiṉṟi-niṉṟaṉa -~um oru-toṭar aṉmiyiṉ, ārāycci iṉṟu*-ām piṟa~eṉiṉ:-- aṟṟu aṉṟu; uḷ+^toṭar pala ~āyiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ tokutiāy avaṉ toḻil pala um^ kūṟutal+ poruṇmittu ākiya perum^toṭar oṉṟu eṉa ē paṭum eṉpatu.

{{Par. 6}} {āciriyaṉ pērūr kiḻāṉ ceyiṟṟiyaṉ iḷam^ kaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉ vantuuṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ} eṉṉātu, {āciriyaṉ vantāṉ pērūr kiḻāṉ uṇṭāṉceyiṟṟiyaṉ ceṉṟāṉ} eṉa+ peyar tōṟum vēṟu toḻil kiḷattalmarapu aṉmiyiṉ, marapu-vaḻu+ kātta āṟu āyiṟṟu.

((iṭa vaḻu vamiti))

[TRANSLATION]

taṉmi+ col= ē aḵṟiṇi+ kiḷavi eṉṟu

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ. taṉmi+ col= um aḵṟiṇi+ col= um eṇṇutaṟkaṇ virāyvarappeṟum; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}} {yāṉ -um eṉ= eḵkam -um cāṟum} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 3}} paṉmi+ taṉmi viṉi kōṭal eṟṟāṉ+ peṟutum ō eṉiṉ:-- taṉmi+col= ē eṉṟataṉāṉ um,
{{"paṉmi urikkum^ taṉmi+ kiḷavi
eṇ= iyal% maruṅkil tiripavi uḷa ē."}}
{{[col:201]}} eṉpataṉāṉ um peṟutum eṉpatu. aḵtēl, {{"eṇ= iyal% maruṅkiltiripavi uḷa ē"}} eṉpataṉāṉ virāy vantu uyartiṇi muṭipukōṭal um peṟappaṭutaliṉ, i+ cūttiram vēṇṭā eṉiṉ:-- uyttu*uṇarntu* iṭarppaṭuvatu eṭuttōttu ilvaḻi eṉa maṟukka. ((43))

 [TRANSLATION]

orumi eṇṇiṉ potu+ piri pāl+ col

{{Par. 2}} potu+ piri pāl+ col eṉṟār ēṉum, {oruvaṉ orutti}eṉpaṉavaṟṟatu pāl uṇarttum īṟu ē koḷḷappaṭum. eṉṉi? eṇṇumuṟi nillā eṉṟu viḷakkappaṭuvaṉa avi ē ākaliṉ eṉpatu.

{{Par. 6}} {oruvaṉ, orutti} eṉa orumikkaṇ niṟṟal um, {iruvaṉ mūvaṉirutti mutti} eṉa eṇṇu muṟimikkaṇ nillāmi um kaṇṭu koḷka.

`eṇ= eṉṟā uṟaḻ eṉṟā āy iraṇṭu um

«neṭu nal yāṉi -~um^ tēr -um mā -~um
paṭi ami maṟavar -um uṭiyam yām»
{{[puṟam:72_4/5]}} eṉa -~um,
«iru maṉa+ peṇṭir -um^ kaḷ= -um^ kavaṟu* -um»
{{[kuṟaḷ:920]}} eṉa -~um, paṭarkki+ col= -um aḵṟiṇi+ kiḷavi -~um virāyeṇṇutal vaḻakku+ payiṟci uṭimiyāṉ avi -~um aṭaṅkauyartiṇi+ col= ē aḵṟiṇi+ kiḷavi eṉa+ potu+ paṭa ōtātu{{"taṉmi+ col= ē aḵṟiṇi+ kiḷavi"}} {{[col:43]}} eṉa+taṉmi+ colli ē vitantu ōtal kuṉṟa+ kūṟal ām ākalāṉ um,avarkku atu karuttu aṉmi aṟika.

{{Par. 5}}
«ā um āṉ iyal+ pārppaṉa mākkaḷ um
peṇṭir um piṇi uṭiyīr um pēṇi+

poṉ pōl+ putalvar+ peṟātīr um

{{[puṟam:9_1/2/3/4/5]}} eṉa+ tiṇi virāy vantu muṉṉili viṉi kōṭal eṟṟāṉ+ peṟutumeṉiṉ:-- a+ nikaraṉa ceyyuḷ muṭipu eṉappaṭum, avaṟṟiatikāra+ puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ+ koḷka. tiṇi virāy eṇṇappaṭum peyarviyaṅkōḷ allā viravu viṉiyoṭu toṭarntu varuvaṉa vaḻakkiṉ uḷuḷa ēl oṉṟu eṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāṉ+ koḷka.

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu. {curucuruttatu, moṭumoṭuttatu} eṉa ici paṟṟi -~um,{koṟukoṟuttār, moṟumoṟuttār} eṉa+ kuṟippu+ paṟṟi -~um,{kuṟukuṟuttatu, kaṟukaṟuttatu} eṉa+ paṇpu paṟṟi -~umiraṭṭittu-niṉṟaṉa pirintu-nillāmi kaṇṭukoḷka. aḵtēl,{kuṟuttatu kuṟuttatu} eṉa+ pirintu um vantaṉa āl eṉiṉ:--aṟṟu aṉṟu, {kuṟuttatu} eṉpatu ōr col {kuṟu} eṉpatu ōr col=aṭuttu+ {kuṟukuṟuttatu} eṉa-niṉṟu {kuṟumi mikuti}uṇarttiṟṟu āyiṉ {kuṟuttatu} eṉpatu {kuṟumi} uṇartta+ {kuṟu}eṉpatu {mikuti} uṇarttiṟṟu ātal vēṇṭum. {kuṟu} eṉpatu{mikuti} uṇarttāmiyiṉ {kuṟukuṟuttatu} eṉpatu oru-col=āy-niṉṟu a+ poruḷ uṇarttiṟṟu eṉa -~ē paṭum. ataṉāṉ+ tāṉ a+{kuṟumi} māttiram uṇartti-niṟpatu ataṉiṉ vēṟu ām eṉpatu.{kaṟukaṟuttatu} eṉpatukku* -um ītu okkum. {kaṟuttatukaṟuttatu}; {kuṟuttatu kuṟuttatu} eṉa+ col% muḻuvatum irumuṟi vārāmiyiṉ, aṭukku aṉmi aṟika.

((paṉmikkuriya vaḻakkukaḷ))

TC 49 c

[TRANSLATION]

oru peyar+ potu+ col uḷ poruḷ oḻiya+
teripu vēṟu kiḷattal talimi um paṉmi um
uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ um aḵṟiṇi maruṅkiṉ um.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ. uyartiṇikkaṇ= um aḵṟiṇikkaṇ= um oru-peyar āy+pala-poruṭku+ potu-~ākiya colli+ piṟa uḷ-poruḷ oḻiya+terintu-koṇṭu potumiyiṉ vēṟu* -āka+ colluka talimiyāṉ -umpaṉmiyāṉ -um; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} teripu eṉpataṟku+ ceyappaṭuporuḷ talimi um paṉmi um ē ām.

{{Par. 3}} {piṟar um vāḻvār uḷar} ēṉum {pārppaṉa+ cēri} eṉṟaluyartiṇikkaṇ talimi paṟṟiya vaḻakku. {eyiṉar nāṭu} eṉpatua+ tiṇikkaṇ paṉmi paṟṟiya vaḻakku. {piṟa pul= um maraṉ umuḷa} ēṉum {kamukam tōṭṭam} eṉṟal aḵṟiṇikkaṇ talimipaṟṟiya vaḻakku. {oṭuvam^ kāṭu} eṉpatu a+ tiṇikkaṇ paṉmipaṟṟiya vaḻakku. {pārppār palar} āyiṉ um {kamuku pala}āyiṉum avi tām ē paṉmi paṟṟiya vaḻakku ām.

{{Par. 4}} pala poruḷ oru colliṉ nīkkutaṟku {{"oru peyar"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 5}} `oṉṟu eṉa muṭittal' eṉpataṉāṉ, {aracar perum^ teru, vayirakaṭakam, ā tīṇṭu kuṟṟi, āṉ atar, eruttu il} eṉa+ potu+ col=iṉṟi-varuvaṉa -~um^ koḷka.

{{Par. 6}} uḷ-poruḷ ellām^ kūṟātu oṉṟaṉi eṭuttu+-kūṟutal marapu* aṉṟu*ākaliṉ, potu+-col_mēl+ col nikaḻum -āṟu uṇarttiya mukattāṉmarapu-vaḻu+ kātta -~āṟu.((49))

TC 50 c

[TRANSLATION]

peyariṉ um toḻiliṉ um piripavi ellām
mayaṅkal kūṭā vaḻakku vaḻippaṭṭaṉa.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ. uyartiṇikkaṇ= um aḵṟiṇikkaṇ= um peyariṉ -āṉumviṉiyiṉ -āṉum potumiyiṉ+ pirintu āṇmikku* -um peṇmikku*-um uriya āy-varuvaṉa ~ellām vaḻu ~ākā, vaḻakku vaḻippaṭṭaṉa~ākalāṉ; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} uyartiṇikkaṇ= um aḵṟiṇikkaṇ= um eṉpatu atikārattāṉvantatu.

{{Par. 3}}
«toṭiyōr koy kuḻi arumpiya kumari ñāḻal»
{{[naṟṟiṇi:54_8/9]}} eṉpatu uyartiṇikkaṇ peyariṉ+ pirinta āṇ-oḻi-miku-col.
{vaṭuku aracar āyiravar makkaḷi uṭiyar} eṉpatu peyariṉ+ pirinta peṇ= oḻi miku col.
{ivar vāḻkkippaṭṭār} eṉpatu toḻiliṉ+ pirinta āṇ oḻi miku col.
{ivar kaṭṭil ēṟiṉār} eṉpatu toḻiliṉ+ pirinta peṇ= oḻi miku col.
{nampi nūṟu erumi uṭiyaṉ} eṉpatu aḵṟiṇikkaṇ peyariṉ+ pirinta āṇ oḻi miku col.
{nam+ aracaṉ āyiram yāṉi uṭiyaṉ} eṉpatu peyariṉ+ pirinta peṇ= oḻi miku col. {toṭi-ceṟittal -um, makkaḷ+^taṉmi -um, il-vāḻkki+_paṭutal-um, kaṭṭil-ēṟutal -um, erumi+-taṉmi -~um, yāṉi+-taṉmi-~um} oḻikkappaṭum poruṭku* -um uṇmiyāl potu_~āy-niṟkal+-pālaṉa oru-pāṟku* uriya_āy-varutaliṉ, marapu-vaḻu ~amitta-~āṟu.

{{Par. 4}} piṟa collāṉ+ pirivaṉa {{"vēṟupaṭu viṉiyiṉ -um iṉattiṉ -um^cārpiṉ -um"}} {{[col:53]}} eṉa -~um, {{"terinili uṭiya~aḵṟiṇi iyaṟpeyar"}} {{[col:171]}} eṉa -~um, {{"niṉiyum^-kāli+ tattam marapiṉ viṉiyōṭu* allatu pāl* ^teripu* ila-~ē"}} {{[col:172]}} eṉa -~um muṉ= ōtappaṭutalāṉ -um, āṇṭuvaḻu iṉmiyāṉ -um, īṇṭu+ tām-ē pirivaṉa -~ē koḷka.

{{Par. 5}} tammāṉ+ tām piriyum eṉpār {{"peyariṉ -um^ toḻiliṉ -um"}}eṉṟār. peyarkkaṇ= um viṉikkaṇ= um^ tām-ē pirivaṉa ~eṉiṉumamiyum.

{{Par. 6}} aḵṟiṇikkaṇ toḻiliṉ+ pirintaṉa uḷa ēl kaṇṭu koḷka. {iṉṟu iv=ūr+ peṟṟam -ellām aṟam^ kaṟakkum, uḻa ~oḻintaṉa} eṉa~uriyāciriyar kāṭṭiṉār -āl -ō ~eṉiṉ:-- {peṟṟam} eṉṉumpotu+-peyar {kaṟattal -um uḻutal -um} ākiyaciṟappu-viṉiyāṉ+ potumi-nīṅkutal vaḻu ~aṉmiyāṉ īṇṭikku*eytāmiyiṉ, avarkku* atu karuttu* aṉṟu* eṉpatu. ((50))

[TRANSLATION]

pala vayiṉāṉ um eṇṇu+ tiṇi viravu+ peyar

{{Par. 2}}

{{Par. 4}} e-ṭu.

{{Par. 7}}
«tammuṭiya taṇ= aḷi um^ tām um^ tam māṉ tēr um

{{[cilampu:7_32]}} eṉa -~um,
«yāṉ um^ tōḻi um āyam um āṭum^ tuṟi naṇṇi+

kāṉ um puḷ= um^ kiti um ellām kari aṉṟu ē» eṉa -~um ivi evvāṟu vantaṉa ō eṉiṉ:-- avitalimi+-poruḷi -~um^ talimi-~il_poruḷi -~um virāyeṇṇi+ talimi+-poruṭku viṉi koṭuppa -~ē talimi-~il_poruḷ-um muṭintaṉa -~āvatu* -ōr muṟimi paṟṟi vantaṉa, īṇṭikkueytā ~eṉpatu. {tāṉ -um^ taṉ puravi -~um^ tōṉṟiṉāṉ} eṉpatu*-um atu. ((pala poruḷ oru col amiyum vaki))

{{Par. 3}} ilakkaṇa+ cūttiraṅkaḷ -ē amiyum i+ cūttiram vēṇṭā piṟaeṉiṉ:-- iru vakiya eṉṉum variyaṟi avaṟṟāṉ+peṟappaṭāmiyāṉ -um, vakuttu+ piṉ= um ilakkaṇam^ kūṟiyavaḻi+poruḷ iṉitu viḷaṅkutalāṉ -um, i+ cūttiram vēṇṭum eṉpatu.((52))

TC 53 c

[TRANSLATION]

avaṟṟu* -uḷ
viṉi vēṟupaṭūum pala-poruḷ-oru-col

{{Par. 3}} {mā+ pūttatu; mā -~um marutu* -um ōṅkiṉa} eṉa vēṟupaṭuviṉiyāṉ -um iṉattāṉ -um, {maram} eṉpatu viḷaṅkiṟṟu.{kavacam pukku-niṉṟu} {mā+ koṇā} eṉṟavaḻi+ {kutiri} eṉpatucārpiṉāl viḷaṅkiṟṟu. {kuruku} mutal āyiṉa -~um aṉṉa.

{{Par. 4}} {{"vēṟupaṭu viṉiyiṉ -um"}} eṉṟār -ēṉum, `oṉṟu* eṉamuṭittal' eṉpataṉāṉ {i+ mā vayiram, veḷiṟu} eṉa vēṟupaṭukkumpeyar -um^ koḷḷappaṭum.

{{Par. 5}} iṉattoṭu cārpu iṭi vēṟṟumi eṉṉi eṉiṉ:-- oru cātikkaṇaṇinta cāti iṉam eṉappaṭum. aṇinta cāti ~aṉṟi ~oruvāṟṟāṉiyipu*-uṭiyatu cārpu* eṉa+-paṭum eṉpatu. ((53))

TC 54 c

{{Par. 3}} {{"viṉi vēṟu-paṭā+ pala-poruḷ-oru-col"}} eṉa vēṟu-niṟpaṉa~illi, vēṟu-paṭu_viṉi mutal āyiṉavaṟṟāṉ vēṟu-paṭuvaṉa tām-ē potu-viṉi koṇṭa-vaḻi viṉi_vēṟu-paṭā+pala-poruḷ-oru-col= ām eṉpatu aṟivittaṟku{{"oṉṟu-viṉi_maruṅkiṉ oṉṟi+-tōṉṟum"}} eṉṟār. {{"oṉṟu-viṉi_maruṅkiṉ oṉṟi+-tōṉṟum"}} eṉa oru cūttiram-āka uriyāciriyar pirittār -āl eṉiṉ:-- aṅṅaṉam pirippiṉ{{"oṉṟu-viṉi_maruṅkiṉ oṉṟi+-tōṉṟutal"}} -umviṉi_vēṟu-paṭūum pala-poruḷ-oru-coṟku* -ē ~ilakkaṇam āymāṟu-kōṭalāṉ -um viṉi_vēṟu-paṭuvaṉa tām -ē potu-viṉikoṇṭa-vaḻi viṉi_vēṟu-paṭātaṉa ~ām eṉpatu ataṉāṉ+peṟa+-paṭāmiyāṉ -um, atu pōli-~uri eṉka.

{{Par. 4}} muṉ= -um piṉ= -um varum^ cārpu mutal āyiṉavaṟṟāṉ+ kuṟittaporuḷ viḷaṅkā+-kāl kiḷantu* -ē colluka eṉa yāppuṟuttaṟku{{"niṉiyum^ kāli"}} eṉṟār.

((itaṟku vaḻuvamiti))

TC 55 c

[TRANSLATION]

kuṟittōṉ kūṟṟam terittu moḻi kiḷavi.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ. oru poruḷ vēṟupāṭu kuṟittōṉ, aḵtu āṟṟal mutalāyiṉavaṟṟāl viḷaṅkātu āyiṉ, ataṉi+ terittu+ colluka; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu.
«aritāra+ cāntam kalantatu pōla
uru keḻa+ tōṉṟi varum -ē -- muruku* uṟaḻum
aṉpaṉ mali+ peyta nīr». eṉa -~um,
«vāru matu+ cōli vaṇṭu utirtta nāṇ malarāl
nāṟum aruvi naḷi mali nal% ^nāṭa.» eṉa -~um varum.
«kalantatu pōla varum -ē ilaṅku aruvi
aṉpaṉ mali+ peyta nīr» eṉa -um,
«nāṟum aruvi naḷi mali nal% ^nāṭa» eṉa -um terittu moḻiyāta-vaḻi+ kuṟittatu viḷaṅkātuvaḻu+-paṭutaliṉ marapu-vaḻu+ kātta -~āṟu. vaṭa-nūlār itaṉinēyam eṉpa.

{{Par. 3}}
«ūṭṭiyaṉṉa ~oṇ ^taḷir+ ceyali»
{{[akam:68_5]}} eṉpuḻi iṉṉataṉi eṉṟu terittu moḻiyāmiyiṉ vaḻu~ām piṟa ~eṉiṉ: -- uvami eṉṉum alaṅkāram āyiṉ aṉṟē iṉṉatuoṉṟaṉi eṉal vēṇṭuvatu. {ceyaliyam^ taḷiriṉatu ceyyātaniṟatti+} ceytatu pōla+ kūṟum^ karuttiṉaṉ ākaliṉ+ piṟitu*-ōr alaṅkāram ām ataṉāṉ atu kaṭā ~aṉṟu eṉpatu.
«paṭuttu vittaṉṉa pāṟi maruṅkiṉ
eṭuttu niṟuttaṉṉa iṭṭu arum^ ciṟu neṟi.»
{{[mali:15]}} eṉa iṉṉōraṉṉa -~ellām av= alaṅkāram paṟṟi-vantaṉa.

{{Par. 4}}
«ollēm^ kuvaḷi+ pulāaṉ makaṉ mārpiṟ
pul= erukkam^ kaṇṇi naṟitu» eṉpuḻi+ {kuvaḷi pulāl nāṟutaṟku um erukkam kaṇṇi naṟituātaṟku* -um} kāraṇam^ kūṟāmiyiṉ vaḻu ām piṟa eṉiṉ:--{putalvaṉ+ payanta pūm^ kuḻal% maṭanti parattiyiṉ+ pirintuvanta kiḻavaṉoṭu pulantu urikkiṉṟāḷ} ākaliṉ {kuvaḷi pulālnāṟutaṟku avaṉ tavaṟṟōṭu kūṭiya avaḷ kātal} kāraṇameṉpatūum, {erukkam^ kaṇṇi naṟitu ātaṟku makiḻnaṉ ceyta tuṉikūr veppam mukiḻ naki mukattāl taṇikkum putalvaṉ mēloru-kālikku*-oru-kāl perukum aṉpu} kāraṇam eṉpatūumpeṟappaṭutaliṉ, vaḻu ākātu eṉpatu.

{{Par. 5}} `mī+ kūṟṟam' eṉpuḻi+ pōla+ kūṟṟu+ kūṟṟam eṉa-niṉṟatu.

((55))

((5.puṟaṉaṭi))

((tiṇikku+ puṟaṉaṭi.))

TC 56 c

[TRANSLATION]

kuṭimi āṇmi iḷami mūppu ē
aṭimi vaṉmi viruntu ē kuḻu ē
peṇmi aracu ē maka ē kuḻavi
taṉmi tiri peyar uṟuppiṉ kiḷavi
kātal ciṟappu ē ceṟal+ col viṟal+ col eṉṟu
āv aṟu mūṉṟu um uḷappaṭa+ tokii
aṉṉa piṟa um avaṟṟoṭu civaṇi
muṉṉattiṉ uṇarum^ kiḷavi ellām
uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ niliyiṉa āyiṉum
aḵṟiṇi maruṅkiṉ kiḷantāṅku iyalum.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ. {{"kuṭimi"}} mutal āka {{"viṟal+ col"}} īṟu āka+colla+-paṭṭa patiṉeṭṭu -um uḷappaṭa aṉṉa piṟa -um avaṟṟōṭuporunti+ tokku muṉṉattiṉ uṇarum^ kiḷavikaḷ ellām, uyartiṇi+poruḷ% mēl niṉṟaṉa āyiṉum, aḵṟiṇi+ poruḷiuṇartti-niṉṟavaḻi+ pōla, aḵṟiṇi muṭipu ē koḷḷum; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu. {kuṭimi naṉṟu, kuṭimi tītu, āṇmi naṉṟu, āṇmi tītu}eṉa varum. oḻintaṉavaṟṟiṟku um ivvāṟu poruntum viṉitalippeyka.

{{Par. 3}} {{"taṉmi tiri peyar"}} {ali}. itaṉōṭu oru-poruḷ+-kiḷavi-~āy-varuvaṉa -~um^ koḷka.

{{Par. 4}} {{"uṟuppiṉ kiḷavi"}} {kuruṭu muṭam} eṉṉum^ toṭakkattaṉa.{{"kātal+-col"}} {pāvi yāṉi} eṉa oppumi karutātu kātalpaṟṟi-varuvaṉa. {{"ciṟappu+ col"}} {kaṇ} pōla+ ciṟantāṉi+{kaṇ=} eṉṟal um {uyir} pōla+ ciṟantāṉi {uyir} eṉṟal um eṉaivi mutal āyiṉa. {{"ceṟal+ col"}} ceṟutali+pulappaṭukkum {poṟiyaṟi keḻīiyili} eṉṉum^ toṭakkattaṉa.{{"viṟal+ col"}} viṟali uṇarttum {peru viṟal arum^ tiṟal}eṉṉum^ toṭakkattaṉa. {{"taṉmi tiri peyar"}} mutal āyiṉaporuḷ vakiyāṉ āṟu* -āka aṭakkappaṭṭaṉa.

{{Par. 5}} {{"kuṭimi, āṇmi, iḷami, mūppu, aṭimi, vaṉmi, peṇmi,uṟuppiṉ kiḷavi, ciṟappu+ col, viṟal+ col"}} eṉpaṉauyartiṇikkaṇ ākupeyar āy -allatu vārā; allaṉa irutiṇikkaṇ= -um otta urimiya eṉa+ koḷka. kātal paṟṟi+ {ciṟuvaṉi} {yāṉi} eṉṟal -um ākupeyar ām aṉṟōeṉiṉ:-- yātāṉum -ōr iyipu paṟṟi oṉṟaṉ peyar oṉṟaṟku* āyatuāku-peyar ām; iyipu karutātu kātal mutal āyiṉavaṟṟāṉ{yāṉi} eṉṟavaḻi ākupeyar -uḷ aṭaṅkā ~eṉpatu. {ciṟuvaṉi} {yāṉi} eṉṟal -um oṉṟaṉ peyar oṉṟaṟku* ātaloppumiyāṉ āku-peyar eṉpār -um uḷar.

{{Par. 6}} collāṉ aṉṟi+ tiṇi-vēṟupāṭu colluvāṉ-kuṟippoṭu paṭuttuuṇarappaṭutaliṉ, {{"muṉṉattiṉ uṇarum^ kiḷavi"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 7}} `aḵṟiṇi muṭipiṉa' eṉṉātu {{"aḵṟiṇi maruṅkiṉ+ kiḷantāṅkuiyalum"}} eṉa+ potuppaṭa+ kūṟiyavataṉāl, {kuṭimi naṉṟu,kuṭimi nalla, aṭimi naṉṟu, aṭimi nalla} eṉa ēṟpuḻiorumikku* -um paṉmikku* -um potu ~āy-niṟṟal -um^koḷḷappaṭum.

{{Par. 8}} {{"aṉṉa piṟa -~um"}} eṉṟataṉāṉ {vēntu vēḷ kuricil amiccupurōcu} eṉṉum^ toṭakkattaṉa -~um^ koḷka.

[TRANSLATION]

vēṟṟumi tām ē ēḻ eṉa moḻipa.

{{Par. 1}}

{{C CENV}} nāṉku coṟku um potu ilakkaṇam uṇarttiṉār, ataṟku* iṭiyīṭuiṉṟi avaṟṟatu ciṟappu ilakkaṇam uṇarttutal% muṟimi āyiṉumvēṟṟumi ilakkaṇam uṇarttutaṟku vēṟu iṭam iṉmiyāṉ um, potuilakkaṇam ātal oppumiyāṉ um, urupu ēṟṟal peyarkku ilakkaṇamākaliṉ vēṟṟumi uṇartti+ peyar uṇarttal muṟi ākalāṉ um,kiḷavi ākkattiṟku um peyar iyaṟku um iṭi vēṟṟumi ilakkaṇamuṇarttiya eṭuttukkoṇṭār. vēṟṟumi āvaṉa peyar um ōriṭiccol= um ākaliṉ, avaṟṟatu ilakkaṇam um potu ilakkaṇam ēām.

{{Par. 2}} vēṟṟumi ilakkaṇam eṉa oṉṟu āyiṉ um ciṟappu uṭiya eḻuvakivēṟṟumi um avaṟṟatu mayakkam um viḷivēṟṟumi um^ taṉittaṉiuṇartta+ takum vēṟupāṭu yāppuṭimiyāṉ mūṉṟu ōttāṉuṇarttiṉār.

{{Par. 3}} potu ilakkaṇam uṇartti+ ciṟappu ilakkaṇam uṇarttutal muṟiākaliṉ, mutaṟkaṇṇatu ākiya peyarccoṟku+ payaṉili kōṭal umurupu ēṟṟal um kālam^ tōṉṟāmi um ākiya ilakkaṇam uṇarttuvāriyipu paṭṭamiyāṉ vēṟṟumi ilakkaṇam uṇarttiṉār eṉa mēlōttiṉōṭu iv= ōttu iṭi iyipu kūṟiṉār āl uriyāciriyareṉiṉ:-- aṟṟu aṉṟu; iv= ōttu+ peyar ilakkaṇam nutali eṭuttu+koḷḷappaṭṭatu āyiṉ, urupu ēṟṟal um payaṉili kōṭal um kālam^tōṉṟāmi um ākiya peyar ilakkaṇam muṉ= ōti, iyipupaṭutalāṉvēṟṟumi uṇarttum karuttiṉar āyiṉ avaṟṟi um `iṉṉailakkaṇatta' ~eṉa ~uṇartti+ piṉ= -um eṭuttukkoṇṭapeyar-ilakkaṇam -ē paṟṟi ~ōti+ peyar-iyal eṉa ōr ōttāṉmuṭiyal+-pāṟṟu aṉṟē; avvāṟu aṉṟi, vēṟṟumi ilakkaṇam ē muṉkūṟi {{"aṉṟi aṉittum peyar+ payaṉili ē"}} {{[col:66]}} eṉa-~um, {{"īṟu peyarkku ākum iyaṟkiya eṉpa"}} {{[col:69]}}eṉa -~um vēṟṟumi ilakkaṇam kūṟi a+ cūttirattāṉ+ payaṉilikōṭal um urupu ēṟṟal um peyarkku ilakkaṇam eṉpatu uyttuṇaravittu+ piṉ= um vēṟṟumi ilakkaṇam ē uṇartti, itaṉivēṟṟumi ōttu eṉṟu* -um, avaṟṟatu mayakkam uṇarttiya ōttivēṟṟumi mayaṅku iyal eṉṟu* -um, ciṟappu illā viḷi vēṟṟumiuṇarttiya ōtti viḷi marapu eṉṟu* -um, nutaliyataṉāṉ+ peyarkoṭuttu mūṉṟu ōttu āka vittu, peyar iyal eṉa vēṟu ōrōttiṟku+ peyar koṭuttamiyāṉ um, {{"ellā+ col= um poruḷkuṟittaṉa ē"}} {{[col:155]}} eṉpatu mutal ākiya aintucūttiram um peyar ilakkaṇam uṇarttum ōttiṉ muṉ viyātu iṭivittal poruntāmiyāṉ um, iv= ōttu+ peyar ilakkaṇam nutalieṭuttukkoḷḷappaṭṭatu aṉṟu, vēṟṟumi ilakkaṇam ē nutalieḻuntatu eṉa ē paṭum; ataṉāṉ avarkku atu karuttu aṉṟu eṉka.aḵtēl, {{"peyarnili+ kiḷavi kālam^ tōṉṟā"}} {{[col:70]}}eṉa+ peyar ilakkaṇam īṇṭu+ kūṟiyatu eṉṉi eṉiṉ:-- payaṉilikōṭal um urupu ēṟṟal um peyar ilakkaṇam eṉpatu īṇṭu+peṟappaṭṭamiyāṉ, avaṟṟōṭu iyiya+ kālam^ tōṉṟāmi um īṇṭu ēkūṟiṉār. peyar ilakkaṇam ātal oppumiyāṉ eṉpatu.

{{WFW}}

{{Par. 4}} i-ḷ: vēṟṟumi āvaṉa ēḻu eṉṟu colluvar tol= āciriyar; e-ṟu.

{{C CENV}}

{{Par. 5}} ceyappaṭuporuḷ% mutal āyiṉa āka+ peyar+ poruḷi vēṟupaṭuttuuṇarttaliṉ, vēṟṟumi āyiṉa. ceyappaṭuporuḷ% mutalāyiṉavaṟṟiṉ vēṟupaṭuttu+ poruḷ% māttiram uṇarttaliṉ, eḻuvāyum vēṟṟumi āyiṟṟu. allatūum, vēṟṟumi eṉpatu paṉmi paṟṟiyavaḻakku eṉiṉ um amiyum.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} muṭikkum^ col+ poruḷ a+ toṭarmoḻikku+ payaṉ ākaliṉ payaṉilieṉṟār.

{{Par. 4}} viyaṅkoḷa varutal viṉi nili urittaṟkaṇ= um, viṉāviṟkuēṟṟal peyar koḷa varutaṟkaṇ= um, viṉikkuṟippu āyavaḻi+paṇpu koḷa varutaṟkaṇ= um aṭaṅkum āyiṉum viṉi um peyar umpaṇpu um muṭikkum^ col= ātal ē aṉṟi muṭikkappaṭum^ col= ātalum uṭiya, viyaṅkōḷ um viṉā um muṭikkum^ col āy allatunillāmiyiṉ, av vēṟupāṭu aṟivittaṟku vēṟu kūṟiṉār eṉpatu.

{{Par. 6}} {ā pala} eṉpuḻi+ {pala} eṉpataṟku+ payaṉili yātu eṉiṉ, atupayaṉili āy {ā} eṉpataṉi muṭittaṟku vantatu ākaliṉ, tāṉpiṟitu ōr col% ^nōkkātu {ā} eṉpataṉōṭu toṭarntu ataṉimuṭittu amintumāṟum eṉpatu. aḵtēl, payaṉili koḷḷātatueḻuvāy vēṟṟumi ām āṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ:-- urupu ēṟṟal peyarkkuilakkaṇam āyiṉum urupu ēlāta vaḻi um peyar ām āṟu pōla,eḻuvāy vēṟṟumi payaṉili koḷḷātavaḻi um eḻuvāy vēṟṟumi ēām eṉpatu. ilakkiyam eṅku um^ cellātaṉa ilakkaṇam ām āṟueṉṉi eṉiṉ:-- āṇṭu ilakkaṇam āvaṉa urupu ēṟṟaṟku ēṟṟa taṉmium payaṉili um payaṉili kōṭaṟku ēṟṟa taṉmi um ākaliṉ, a+taṉmi ellāvaṟṟiṟku um uṇmiyiṉ ilakkaṇam ām eṉpatu.

{{Par. 7}} akara+ cuṭṭu aṉṟi eṉa īṟu tirintu-niṉṟatu. {{"aṉṟiyaṉittumpeyar+ payaṉili"}} eṉa ē, payaṉili kōṭal peyarkkuilakkaṇam eṉpatūum peṟṟām. ((5))

((toki+ peyarum payaṉili koḷḷal))

{{Par. 3}} peyariṉ ākiya toki um eṉṉum ummiyāṉ+, peyaroṭu peyartokkaṉa ē aṉṟi {nilam^ kaṭantāṉ, mā+ koṇarntāṉ} eṉa+peyaroṭu viṉi vantu tokka viṉiyiṉ ākiya toki -~um uḷaeṉpatu ām. āka ē peyarōṭu peyar um peyaroṭu toḻil um^tokkaṉa toki eṉpatu peṟṟām.

{{Par. 6}} peyariṉ ākiya toki um eṉṟa ummiyāṉ viṉiyiṉ ākiya viṉi+toki taḻuvappaṭṭatu eṉṟum, {{"ellā+ toki um oru col%naṭiya"}} {{[col:420]}} eṉpataṉāl toki+ col= ellām eḻuvāyvēṟṟumi ātal peṟappaṭutaliṉ, īṇṭu {{"av= um uriya a+pālāṉa"}} eṉṟatu toki+ coṟku+ payaṉili kōṭal% māttirameytuvittaṟku eṉṟu* -um, uriyāciriyar kūṟiṉār āl eṉiṉ:--aṟṟu aṉṟu; viṉi+ tokikku nilimoḻi viṉi eṉpatuuriyāciriyarkku+ karuttu aṉmi {{"viṉiyiṉ tokuti kālattuiyalum"}} {{[col:415]}} eṉṉum^ cūttirattiṉ+ collutum. iṉi{{"ellā+ toki um oru col% naṭiya"}} {{[col:420]}}eṉpataṟku oru col% naṭiya ām eṉpatu allatu eḻuvāy vēṟṟumiām eṉṉum^ karuttu iṉmiyāṉ um, a+ karuttu uṇṭāyiṉ avi umeḻuvāy vēṟṟumi āy niṉṟu {{"aṉṟiyaṉittum peyar+ payaṉiliē"}} {{[col:66]}} eṉṟataṉāl payaṉili eytum ākaliṉ {{"av= umuriya appālāṉa"}} eṉṟal kūṟiyatu kūṟiṟṟu ām ākalāṉ um, atuum uriyāciriyar karuttu aṉṟu eṉka.

{{Par. 7}} aḵtēl, toki um eḻuvāy vēṟṟumi ām eṉpatu ē i+cūttirattiṟku+ karuttu āyiṉ {{"eḻuvāy vēṟṟumi peyar tōṉṟunili ē"}} {{[col:65]}} eṉṉum^ cūttirattiṉ piṉ vikkaeṉiṉ:-- peyar tōṉṟiya tuṇi āy niṟṟal um payaṉili kōṭal umākiya eḻuvāy vēṟṟumi ilakkaṇam iraṇṭu um^ toki+ coṟku umeytuvittaṟku av= iraṇṭu cūttirattiṟku+ piṉ vittār eṉpatu.āṇṭu vippiṉ, {{"aṉṟiyaṉittum peyar+ payaṉili ē"}}{{[col:66]}} eṉṟataṉāṉ+ payaṉili kōṭal peyarkku ē āy+tokikku eytātu ām eṉpatu. ((eḻuvāyum, tōṉṟā eḻuvāyum))

{{Par. 3}} {karuvūrkku+ cellāy ō cāttā} eṉṟavaḻi+ {celval} eṉa -~um,{yāṉ yātu ceyval} eṉṟavaḻi {itu cey} eṉa -~um, {ivaṉ yār}eṉṟavaḻi+ {paṭittalivaṉ} eṉa -~um ceppiyavaḻi, {yāṉ nīivaṉ} eṉṉum eḻuvāy veḷippaṭātu niṉṟu, {celval, itu cey,paṭittalivaṉ} eṉṉum payaṉili koṇṭa āṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.

((peyaril urupu niṟkum iṭam))

TC 69 c

[TRANSLATION]

kūṟiya muṟiyiṉ urupu nili tiriyātu
īṟu peyarkku ākum iyaṟkiya eṉpa.

{{Par. 1}}

{{C CENV}} eḻuvāy vēṟṟumi uṇartti ēṉi aṟu vaki vēṟṟumi ilakkaṇam umuṇarttiya eṭuttukkoṇṭār.

{{Par. 2}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ. peyar, ai, oṭu, ku, iṉ, atu, kaṇ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭamuṟimiyi uṭiya urupu tam^ nili tiriyātu peyarkku īṟu āmiyalpi uṭiya eṉṟu colluvar āciriyar; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 3}} e-ṭu: {cāttaṉi, cāttaṉoṭu, cāttaṟku, cāttaṉiṉ, cāttaṉatu,cāttaṉkaṇ} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 4}} a+ muṟi paṟṟi avi eṇṇuppeyarāṉ vaḻaṅkappaṭutaliṉ {{"kūṟiyamuṟiyiṉ urupu"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 5}} viṉiccol iṟuti niṟkum iṭiccol, tām ē vēṟu uṇarappaṭātu a+coṟku uṟuppu āy niṟkum aṉṟu ē, ivi avvāṟu peyarkku uṟuppuākātu tām eṉa vēṟu uṇarappaṭṭu iṟuti niṟkum eṉpār {{"nilitiriyātu"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 6}} urupu peyar cārntu varum eṉa ē, urupu ēṟṟal ākiya peyarilakkaṇam um peṟappaṭṭatu. ((8))

((peyariṉ iyalpu))

TC 70 c

[TRANSLATION]

peyar nili+ kiḷavi kālam^ tōṉṟā
toḻil nili oṭṭum oṉṟu alam^ kaṭi ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: peyarccol+ kālam^ tōṉṟā. viṉiccollōṭu okkum oru kūṟuallāta iṭattu; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} {cāttaṉ, koṟṟaṉ, uṇṭal, tiṉṟal} eṉa+ kālam^ tōṉṟātu niṟṟalum, {uṇṭāṉ, tiṉṟāṉ} eṉa+ toḻiṉili oṭṭuvaṉa kālam^ tōṉṟiniṟṟal um^ kaṇṭu koḷka.

{{Par. 3}} {uṇṭāṉ, tiṉṟāṉ} eṉṉum^ toṭakkattu+ paṭuttu+ collappaṭumtoḻil+ peyar viṉiccol+ pōla+ tiṇi um pāl um^ kālam ummutal āyiṉavaṟṟi viḷakki, {aṉ āṉ} mutal ākiya īṟṟa āyvarutaliṉ, {{"toḻil nili oṭṭum"}} eṉṟār. {{"oṭṭum"}} eṉpatu uvama+-col.

{{Par. 4}} peyar ākiya niliyi uṭiyatu peyarnili eṉa aṉmoḻi+ toki.

{{Par. 5}} peyar -- poruḷ. toḻilnili um atu.

{{Par. 6}} kālam^ tōṉṟā eṉpaṉa oru col= āy+ peyarnili+ kiḷavieṉpataṟku muṭipu āyiṉa.

{{Par. 7}} payaṉili kōṭal um urupu ēṟṟal um ākiya peyar ilakkaṇamīṇṭu+ peṟappaṭutaliṉ, avaṟṟōṭu iyiya+ kālam^ tōṉṟāmi ākiyapeyar ilakkaṇam um īṇṭu ē kūṟiṉār. perumpāṉmi paṟṟi+ kālam^tōṉṟāmi peyar ilakkaṇam āyiṟṟu. ((9))

((3.iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi))

((ai urupiṉ poruḷ))

TC 71 c

[TRANSLATION]

iraṇṭu ākuvatu ē
ai eṉa+ peyariya vēṟṟumi+ kiḷavi
evvaḻi variṉum viṉi ē viṉikkuṟippu
av= iru mutalil tōṉṟum atu ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: {{"peyar ai oṭu ku"}} eṉṉum^ cūttirattu ai eṉa+ peyarpeṟṟa vēṟṟumiccol iraṇṭāvatu ām. aḵtu^ yāṇṭu variṉum viṉium viṉikkuṟippu um ākiya av= iraṇṭu mutaṟkaṇ= um^ tōṉṟumavi poruḷ āka varum; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu: {kuṭatti vaṉintāṉ, kuḻiyi uṭiyaṉ} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 3}} eṇṇu muṟiyāṉ iraṇṭāvatu eṉpatu peṟappaṭum āyiṉum, i+ kuṟitol= āciriyar vaḻakku eṉpatu aṟivittaṟku ai eṉa+ peyariyavēṟṟumi+ kiḷavi iraṇṭāvatu eṉṟār. itu mūṉṟāvatu mutalāyiṉavaṟṟiṟku um okkum.

{{Par. 4}} peyariya eṉpatu peyar eṉpatu mutal āka muṭintatu ōrpeyareccam.

{{Par. 5}} tammi uṇarttuvaṉa um peyar eṉappaṭutaliṉ {{"ai eṉa+peyariya"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 6}} {{"viṉi ē viṉikkuṟippu"}} eṉṟār āyiṉum avaṟṟiṉceyappaṭuporuḷ ē koḷḷappaṭum; av= iru mutaliṉ+ tōṉṟumeṉṟamiyāṉ, mutal ātaṟku* -um vēṟṟumi+ poruḷ ātaṟku* -umēṟpaṉa avi -~ē ākaliṉ. eṉṉi avi mutal ātaṟku ēṟṟa -~āṟueṉiṉ:-- ceyappaṭuporuḷ mutal āyiṉa toḻiṟku+ kāraṇam ākalāṉ-um, {{"āy eṭṭu eṉpa toḻiṉ mutaṉili ē"}} {{[col:112]}} eṉaavaṟṟi mutaṉili eṉṟu kūṟupa ākalāṉ um eṉpatu. aḵtēl, ceyappaṭuporuḷ -āvatu viṉimutal+ ^toḻil+payaṉuṟuvatu ātalāl, {kuḻi} mutal āyiṉavaṟṟiṟku av= ilakkaṇam ēlāmiyiṉ+ceyappaṭuporuḷ aṉmiyāṉ viṉikkuṟippu eṉal vēṇṭā eṉiṉ:--aṟṟu aṉṟu; {{"a+ mu+ kālam um^ kuṟippoṭu um^ koḷḷum*,meynnili uṭiya"}} {{[col:200]}} eṉṟār ākaliṉ, kuṟippu+col+ kālamoṭu tōṉṟi+ toḻil+ col= ātal um kuḻi mutal āyiṉatoḻil+ payaṉ uṟutal um āciriyar tuṇipu ākaliṉ, avi um^ceyappaṭuporuḷ ām eṉpatu. āyiṉ, ceyappaṭuporuṭkaṇ+ ^tōṉṟumeṉa amiyum, viṉi ē viṉikkuṟippu eṉal vēṇṭā eṉiṉ:--aṅṅaṉam^ kūṟiṉ, ceyappāṭu iṉitu viḷaṅkum^ ciṟappu uṭi+teriniliviṉi+ ceyappaṭuporuḷi ē kūṟiṉār ō kuṟippu+ poruḷum aṭaṅka+ potu vakiyāṉ+ kūṟiṉār ō eṉṟu aiyam ām; aiyamnīṅka {{"viṉi ē viṉikkuṟippu"}} eṉṟār eṉpatu. viṉiviṉikkuṟippu eṉpaṉa īṇṭu ākupeyar.

{{Par. 7}} {pukaḻi niṟuttāṉ, pukaḻi niṟuttal, pukaḻi uṭiyāṉ,pukaḻi uṭimi} eṉa iraṇṭāvatu peyaroṭu toṭarntavaḻi umviṉi+ ceyappaṭuporuḷ um kuṟippu+ ceyappaṭuporuḷ um paṟṟi ēniṟkum eṉpār, ev vaḻi variṉum eṉṟu yāppuṟuttār.

{{Par. 8}} iyaṟṟappaṭuvatu um vēṟupaṭukkappaṭuvatu um, eytappaṭuvatu umeṉa+ ceyappaṭuporuḷ mūṉṟu ām. iyaṟṟutal -āvatu muṉ= illataṉi uṇṭākkutal. vēṟupaṭuttal -āvatu muṉ= uḷḷataṉi+ tirittal. eytappaṭutal -āvatu iyaṟṟutal -um vēṟupaṭuttal -umiṉṟi+ toḻil+ payaṉ uṟum^ tuṇi ~āy niṟṟal.

((10))

((apporuḷiṉ pākupāṭukaḷ))

TC 72 c

[TRANSLATION]

kāppiṉ oppiṉ ūrtiyiṉ iḻiyiṉ
ōppiṉ pukaḻiṉ paḻiyiṉ eṉṟā

nōkkaliṉ añcaliṉ citippiṉ eṉṟā
aṉṉa piṟa um a+ mutal+ poruḷa

{{Par. 1}}

{{C CENV}} ceyappaṭu poruḷ mūṉṟaṉi -~um paṟṟi-varum vāypāṭukaḷivirikkiṉṟār.

{{Par. 2}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: {kāppu} mutal āka+ {citippu} īṟu āka+ collappaṭṭairupatteṭṭu+ poruḷ um avi pōlvaṉa piṟa um ākiya a+ mutal+poruḷ mēl varum ellā+ col= um iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi+ pāla eṉṟucolluvar pulavar; e-ṟu.

{{C CENV}} eṉṟā eṉpatu eṇ= iṭiccol.

{{Par. 3}} iṉ= ellām {{"puṇar iyal* ^nili iṭi+ poruṇilikku"}}{{[col:250]}} utavātu eṇṇiṉkaṇ vantaṉa. muṉṉarvaruvaṉavaṟṟiṟku um ītu okkum.

{{Par. 4}} {{"a+ mutal"}} eṉṟatu kūṟappaṭṭa ceyappaṭuporuḷ eṉṟa āṟu.

{{Par. 5}} e-ṭu: {eyili iḻittāṉ} eṉpatu iyaṟṟappaṭuvatu. {kiḷiyiōppum, poruḷi iḻakkum, nāṇi aṟukkum, maratti+ kuṟikkum,nelli+ tokukkum, vēliyi+ pirikkum, aṟatti ākkum, nāṭṭi+citikkum} eṉpaṉa vēṟupaṭukkappaṭuvaṉa. {kuṟittal}{curukkutal um ciṟitu iḻakka+ citittal um} ām. {aṟuttal}{ciṟitu iḻavāmal+} ciṉiyi āyiṉum mutali āyiṉum {iru kūṟu}ceytal, {ūri+ kākkum, tantiyi okkum, tēri ūrum,kuricili+ pukaḻum, nāṭṭi+ paḻikkum, putalvari+ peṟum,maṉiviyi+ kātalikkum, pakivari vekuḷum, ceṟṟāri+ ceṟum,naṭṭāri uvakkum, nūli+ kaṟkum, poṉṉi niṟukkum, ariciyiaḷakkum, aṭikkāyi eṇṇum, ūri+ cārum, neṟiyi+ cellum,cūtiṉi+ kaṉṟum, kaṇiyi nōkkum, kaḷvari añcum} eṉpaṉaeytappaṭuvaṉa. {vekuḷal um^ ceṟal um} {koli+} poruḷa āyavaḻi vēṟupaṭukkappaṭuvaṉa ām. {ceṟal} {vekuḷiyatu} kāriyam.

{{Par. 6}} iyaṟṟappaṭuvatu orutaṉmittu ākaliṉ, ataṟku oru vāypāṭu* -ēkūṟiṉār. ēṉiya pala ilakkaṇatta ākaliṉ pala vāypāṭṭāṉvirittu+ kūṟiṉār.

{{Par. 7}} aṉṉa piṟa um eṉṟataṉāl, {pakivari+ paṇittāṉ, cōṟṟi aṭṭāṉ,kuḻiyi uṭiyāṉ, poruḷi ilaṉ} eṉṉum^ toṭakkattaṉa koḷka.

{{Par. 8}} {kāppu} mutal āyiṉa poruḷ paṟṟi ōtappaṭṭamiyāṉ, {ūri+purakkum, ūri aḷikkum, tantiyi nikarkkum, tantiyioṭṭum, tēri+ celuttum, tēri+ kaṭāvum} eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉaellām^ koḷka.

{{Par. 9}} iyaṟṟappaṭutal mutal ākiya vēṟupāṭu, kuṟippuviṉi+ceyappaṭuporuṭkaṇ ēṟpaṉa koḷka. ((11))

((4.mūṉṟām vēṟṟumi))

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} mēl {{"ai ~oṭu kuv="}} eṉṟu* ōtiyavaḻi āṉ urupu* um^taḻuvappaṭṭamiyāṉ, īṇṭu um av= āṟu ē koḷḷappaṭum; paṭa ēmūṉṟāvatu ātal um viṉimutal karuvi+ poruṭṭu ātal um āṉurupiṟku um eytum.

{{Par. 3}} viṉimutal -āvatu karuvi mutal āyiṉa kāraṇaṅkaḷi+toḻiṟpaṭuttuvatu. karuvi -~āvatu viṉimutal+ ^toḻil+ payaṉi+ ceyappaṭuporuṭkaṇ uyppatu.

{{Par. 4}} aṉi eṉpatu attaṉmiya ākiya mutal eṉa avaṟṟatuilakkaṇattiṟku+ tōṟṟuvāy ceyta āṟu.

{{Par. 5}} mutal eṉpataṟku mēl ē urittām.

{{Par. 6}} e-ṭu: {koṭiyoṭu tuvakkuṇṭāṉ, ūciyoṭu kuyiṉṟa tūcu um paṭṭuum} eṉa -~um, {akattiyaṉāl tamiḻ urikkappaṭṭatu, vēlāṉeṟintāṉ} eṉa -~um varum.

{{Par. 7}} piṟa poruḷ um uḷa ēṉum viṉimutal karuvi ciṟantamiyāṉ{{"viṉimutal karuvi aṉi mutaṟṟu"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 8}} viṉimutal karuvikkaṇ oṭu eṉ urupu ikkālattu aruki allatuvārātu. ((12))

((apporuḷiṉ pākupāṭukaḷ))

TC 74 c

[TRANSLATION]

ataṉiṉ iyaṟal ataṉ+ ^taku kiḷavi
ataṉ viṉi+ paṭutal ataṉiṉ ātal
ataṉiṉ+ kōṭal ataṉoṭu mayaṅkal
ataṉōṭu iyinta oppu al oppuri
iṉ= āṉ ētu īṅku eṉa varūum
aṉṉa piṟa um ataṉ pāla eṉmaṉār

{{Par. 1}}

{{C CENV}} i-ḷ: {{"ataṉiṉ iyaṟal"}} -- {maṇṇāṉ iyaṉṟa kuṭam} eṉpatu.{maṇ} mutaṟkāraṇam. mutaṟ-kāraṇam -āvatu kāriyattōṭu* oṟṟumi uṭiyatu. {{"ataṉ taku kiḷavi"}} -- {vāyāṉ+ ^takkatu vāycci, aṟivāṉaminta cāṉṟōr} eṉpaṉa. ivi karuvi+ pāṟpaṭum. {{"ataṉviṉippaṭutal"}} -- {cāttaṉāṉ muṭiyum i+ karumam} eṉpatu.itu viṉimutal+ pāṟpaṭum. {{"ataṉiṉ ātal"}} -- {vāṇikattāṉāyiṉāṉ} eṉpatu. {{"ataṉiṉ kōṭal"}} -- {kāṇattāṉ+ koṇṭaarici} eṉpatu. ivi um^ karuvi+ pāṟpaṭum. ivi aintu poruḷum oṭu urupiṟku um^ ciṟupāṉmi uriya āyiṉum, perumpāṉmipaṟṟi āṉ urupiṟku ē uriya pōla+ kūṟiṉār. {{"ataṉoṭu mayaṅkal"}} -- {eṇṇoṭu virāya arici} eṉpatu.{{"ataṉōṭu iyinta oru viṉi+ kiḷavi"}} -- {āciriyaṉoṭuvanta māṇākkaṉ} eṉpatu. {varutal+} toḻil iruvarkku umottaliṉ, {{"oru viṉi+ kiḷavi"}} āyiṟṟu. {{"ataṉōṭu iyintavēṟu viṉi+ kiḷavi"}} -- {maliyoṭu poruta māl yāṉi}eṉpatu. {porutal} {yāṉikku} allatu iṉmiyiṉ {{"vēṟu viṉi+kiḷavi"}} āyiṟṟu. {{"ataṉōṭu iyinta oppu al oppuri"}} --{poṉṉōṭu irumpu aṉiyar niṉṉoṭu piṟar ē} eṉpatu. oppuallataṉi oppu āka+ kūṟaliṉ {{"oppu al oppuri"}} āyiṟṟu. immūṉṟaṟku um uṭaṉikaḻtal potu eṉpatūum avi oṭu eṉ urupiṟku ēuriya eṉpatūum uṇarttiya, {{"ataṉōṭu iyital"}} mūṉṟaṟku um^kūṟiṉār. {{"ataṉoṭu mayaṅkaṟku"}} um ītu okkum. ellā+ poruṭkaṇ= um atu eṉṟatu urupu ēṟkum peyar+ poruḷi.{{"ataṟku viṉi uṭimiyiṉ"}} {{[col:76]}} eṉpataṟku um ītuokkum. {{"iṉ= āṉ ētu"}} eṉpatu {muyaṟciyiṉ+ piṟattalāṉ oliniliyātu} eṉpatu. {{"ataṉiṉ ātal"}} eṉa+ kāraka-~ētu muṉ+kūṟappaṭṭamiyāṉ, itu ñāpaka-~ētu ām. i+ poruḷ āṉ urupiṟkuum iṉ= urupiṟku um urittu eṉpatu viḷakkiya ētu eṉṉātu {{"iṉ=āṉ ētu"}} eṉṟār. {{"iṉ= āṉ"}} eṉpatu ummi+ toki. avaṟṟatuētu+ poruṇmi; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}} {{"eṉa"}} eṉpataṉi māṟṟi ētu eṉpataṉ piṉ koṭuttu, `īṅkuvarūum aṉṉa piṟa um' eṉa iyikka. `īṅṅaṉam' eṉṟu pāṭamōtuvār um uḷar.

{{Par. 3}} {{"ataṉiṉ iyaṟal"}} eṉpataṟku+
«taccaṉ ceyta ciṟu mā viyam»
{{[kuṟun:61]}} eṉṟu* -um, `iṉṉāṉ' eṉpataṟku+ {kaṇṇāṉ+ kotti kālāṉ muṭavaṉ}eṉṟu* -um, utāraṇam^ kāṭṭiṉār āl uriyāciriyar eṉiṉ:-- aṟṟuaṉṟu; «taccaṉ ceyta ciṟu mā viyam» eṉpatu {{"viṉimutalkaruvi aṉi mutaṟṟu"}} {{[col:73]}} eṉpuḻi aṭaṅkutalāṉ īṇṭuum pāṟpaṭukka vēṇṭāmiyāṉ um, ciṉi vikāratti mutal% mēlēṟṟi+ kūṟum poruṇmi `iṉṉāṉ' eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟappaṭāmiyāṉ um,atu pōliyuri eṉka.

{{Par. 4}} oṭu ~um āṉ um iraṇṭu vēṟṟumi ākal+ pāla eṉiṉ:-- aṟṟu aṉṟu; vēṟṟumi-mayakkam -āvatu oru vēṟṟumiyatu oru poruṭkaṇ= āka, cila poruṭkaṇ= āka ēṉivēṟṟumi -~um^ cēṟal aṉṟu* ē; avvāṟu aṉṟi+ ciṟitu* oḻittu ellā+ poruṭkaṇ= -um iraṇṭu*urupu* -um^ cēṟalāṉ -um, vaṭanūluḷ poruḷ vēṟṟumi allatuurupu vēṟṟumiyāṉ oru vēṟṟumi āka ōtappaṭāmiyāṉ um, īṇṭuellā āciriyar um oru vēṟṟumi āka ē ōtiṉār eṉka. vaṭanūloṭumāṟukoḷḷāmi+ kūṟal āciriyarkku mēṟkōḷ āyiṉ, viḷivēṟṟumiyi eḻuvāy vēṟṟumikkaṇ aṭakkaṟpālar āvar, avvāṟuaṭakkal ilar eṉiṉ:-- aṟṟu aṉṟu; viḷi vēṟṟumiyi eḻuvāyvēṟṟumikkaṇ aṭakkal āṇṭu ellārkku um oppa muṭintatu aṉṟu;
`ēḻu iyal% muṟiyatu etirmukam* vēṟṟumi
vēṟu eṉa viḷampāṉ peyaratu vikāram eṉṟu
ōtiya pulavaṉ um uḷaṉ oru vakiyāṉ
intiraṉ eṭṭām vēṟṟumi eṉṟaṉaṉ' eṉpa ākaliṉ, aintiranūlār viḷi vēṟṟumiyi eṭṭām vēṟṟumiāka nērntār eṉpatu peṟappaṭum; āciriyar avar matam paṟṟi+kūṟiṉār ākaliṉ ataṉoṭu māṟukoḷḷātu eṉpatu. i+ karuttuviḷakkiya aṉṟē, pāyirattuḷ
{{"aintira niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ"}} eṉa+ kūṟiṟṟu eṉka.

{{Par. 5}} aṉṉa piṟa um eṉṟataṉāl,
{kaṇṇāṉ+ kotti},
«tūṅku kiyāṉ ōṅku naṭiya»
{{[puṟam:22_1]}},
{matiyōṭu okkum* mukam},
{cūloṭu kaḻuti pāram^ cumantatu} eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉa koḷka.

((5.nāṉkām vēṟṟumi))

((ku urupiṉ poruḷ.))

TC 75 c

[TRANSLATION]

nāṉku ākuvatu ē
ku eṉa+ peyariya vēṟṟumi+ kiḷavi
e+ poruḷ āyiṉ um^ koḷḷum atu ē

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: mēl ku eṉa+ peyar koṭuttu ōtappaṭṭa vēṟṟumiccolnāṉkāvatu ām. atu yātāṉ um oru poruḷ āyiṉum ataṉi ēṟṟuniṟkum; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu: {antaṇaṟku āvi+ koṭuttāṉ} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 3}} {māṇākkaṟku nūl+ poruḷ urittāṉ} eṉa+ koṭi+ poruḷa ākiyacollāṉ aṉṟi+ piṟa vāypāṭṭāṉ+ kūṟappaṭuvaṉa um, {māṇākkaṟkuaṟivu koṭuttāṉ} eṉa+ koṭuppāṉ poruḷ āy+ koḷvāṉkaṇ+ cellātuāṇṭu+ tōṉṟum poruḷ um ellām aṭaṅkutaṟku {{"e+ poruḷāyiṉum"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 4}} piṟa poruḷ um uḷa āyiṉum, kōṭal+ poruḷ ciṟantamiyiṉ,{{"epporuḷ āyiṉum^ koḷḷum"}} eṉṟār ((14)).

((apporuḷiṉ pākupāṭukaḷ))

TC 76 c

[TRANSLATION]

ataṟku viṉi uṭimiyiṉ ataṟku uṭampaṭutaliṉ
ataṟku+ paṭu poruḷiṉ atu ~āku kiḷaviyiṉ
ataṟku^ yāppu uṭimiyiṉ ataṉ poruṭṭu* ātaliṉ
naṭpiṉ pakiyiṉ kātaliṉ ciṟappiṉ eṉṟu
a+ poruḷ+ kiḷavi um ataṉ pāla eṉmaṉār.

{{Par. 1}}

{{C CENV}} i-ḷ: {{"ataṟku viṉi uṭimi"}} -- {karumpiṟku vēli} eṉpatu.viṉi -- īṇṭu {upakāram}. {{"ataṟku uṭampaṭutal"}} --{cāttaṟku makaḷ uṭampaṭṭār} eṉpatu. {cāṉṟōr kolikkuuṭampaṭṭār} eṉpatu um atu. {{"ataṟku+ paṭu poruḷ"}} -āvatu{potu ~ākiya poruḷi+ pakukkuṅkāl} {oru paṅkiṉ+ paṭumporuḷ}; atu {cāttaṟku+ kūṟu koṟṟaṉ} eṉpatu. {{"atu ākukiḷavi"}} -- {kaṭicūttirattiṟku+ poṉ} eṉpatu. {poṉkaṭicūttiram āy+ tiriyum} ākaliṉ {{"atu āku kiḷavi"}} eṉṟār.kiḷavi -- poruḷ. {{"ataṟku yāppuṭimi"}} -- {kikku yāppuuṭiyatu kaṭakam} eṉpatu. {{"ataṟporuṭṭu ātal"}} --{kūḻiṟku+ kuṟṟēval ceyyum} eṉpatu. {{"naṭpu"}} -- {avaṟkunaṭṭāṉ, avaṟku+ tamaṉ} eṉpaṉa. {{"paki"}} -- {avaṟku+paki, avaṟku māṟṟāṉ} eṉpaṉa. {{"kātal"}} -- {naṭṭārkku+kātalaṉ, putalvaṟku* aṉpuṟum} eṉpaṉa. {{"ciṟappu"}} --{vaṭukaracarkku+ ciṟantār cōḻiyavaracar, kaṟpārkku+ciṟantatu cevi} eṉpaṉa.

{{Par. 2}} {{"a+ poruḷ"}} eṉṟatu aṉṉa poruḷi; {iv= āṭi um a+ nūlāṉiyaṉṟatu} eṉṟatu pōla. eṉa ē, aṉṉa piṟa um eṉṟa āṟu ām

{{Par. 3}} {{"a+ poruḷ+ kiḷavi um"}} eṉṟataṉāl, {piṇikku maruntu,naṭṭārkku+ tōṟkum, avaṟku+ takkāḷ ivaḷ, uṟṟārkku uriyarpoṟṟoṭi makaḷir} eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉa ellām^ koḷka. ((15))

((6. aintām vēṟṟumi))

aintu ākuvatu ē

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: mēl iṉ= eṉa+ peyar koṭuttu ōtappaṭṭa vēṟṟumiccolaintāvatu ām. aḵtu i+ poruḷiṉ ittaṉmittu i+ poruḷ eṉṉumporuṇmiyi uṇarttum; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} aintāvatu nāṉku poruṇmi uṭittu; poruvu um, elli um,nīkkam um, ētu um eṉa. avaṟṟuḷ+ poruvu iru vaki+ paṭum;uṟaḻ-poruvu* -um, uvama+-poruvu* -um eṉa. ētu -~um iruvaki+ paṭum; ñāpaka-~ētu -~um, kāraka-~ētu -~um eṉa.avaṟṟuḷ ñāpaka-~ētu+ poruṇmi mēlē kūṟappaṭṭatu.kāraka-~ētu+ poruṇmi {{"accam"}} {{"ākkam"}} eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ+peṟappaṭum. nīkkam+ poruṇmi {{"tīrtal"}} {{"paṟṟuviṭutal"}}eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ+ peṟappaṭum. ēṉi iraṇṭu um {{"itaṉiṉ iṟṟuitu"}} eṉpataṉāṉ+ koḷḷa+ paṭum, av= iraṇṭaṉi um aḵtu irumuṟiyāṉ uṇarttum ākalāṉ elli+ poruḷ -- {karuvūriṉ kiḻakku;itaṉiṉ ūṅku} eṉa varum. {kiḻakku ūṅku} eṉpaṉa, viṉikkuṟippualla ēṉum {{"iṟṟu"}} eṉṉum poruḷpaṭa niṟṟaliṉ, {{"iṟṟu"}}eṉṟal ē ām. porūu+ poruṭku utāraṇam muṉṉar+ peṟappaṭum.((16))

((apporuḷiṉ pākupāṭukaḷ))

TC 78 c

[TRANSLATION]

vaṇṇam vaṭivu ē aḷavu ē cuvi ē
taṇmi vemmi accam eṉṟā
naṉmi tīmi ciṟumi perumi
vaṉmi meṉmi kaṭumi eṉṟā
mutumi iḷami ciṟattal iḻittal
putumi paḻami ākkam eṉṟā
iṉmi uṭimi nāṟṟam^ tīrtal
paṉmi ciṉmi paṟṟuviṭutal eṉṟu

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: vaṇṇa mutal āka+ paṟṟuviṭutal īṟu āka+ collappaṭṭaṉa umavi pōlvaṉa piṟa um aintām vēṟṟumi+ tiṟattaṉa ām; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} {{"vaṇṇam"}} {veṇmi karumi} mutal āyiṉa. {{"vaṭivu"}}{vaṭṭam^ catura} mutal āyiṉa. {{"aḷavu"}} {neṭumi kuṟumi}mutalāyiṉa. {{"cuvi"}} {kippu+ puḷippu} mutal āyiṉa.{{"nāṟṟam"}} {naṟunāṟṟam^ tīnāṟṟam*} mutal āyiṉa.

{{Par. 3}} e-ṭu: {kākkiyiṉ+ karitu kaḷampaḻam, itaṉiṉ vaṭṭam itu,itaṉiṉ* ^neṭitu itu, itaṉiṉ+ tīvitu itu, itaṉiṉ+ taṇṇituitu, itaṉiṉ veytu itu, itaṉiṉ* ^naṉṟu itu, itaṉiṉ+ tītu itu,itaṉiṉ+ ciṟitu itu, itaṉiṉ+ peritu itu, itaṉiṉ valitu itu,itaṉiṉ melitu itu, itaṉiṉ+ kaṭitu itu, itaṉiṉ mutitu itu,itaṉiṉ iḷitu itu, itaṉiṉ+ ciṟantu itu, itaṉiṉ iḻintatu itu,itaṉiṉ+ putitu itu, itaṉiṉ+ paḻitu itu, ivaṉiṉ ilaṉ ivaṉ,ivaṉiṉ uṭiyaṉ ivaṉ, itaṉiṉ* ^nāṟum itu, itaṉiṉ+ pala ivi,itaṉiṉ+ cila ivi} eṉa -~um; {{"accam"}} {kaḷḷariṉ añcum}eṉa -~um, {{"ākkam"}} -- {vāṇikattiṉ āyiṉāṉ} eṉa -~um,{{"tīrtal"}} -- {ūriṉ+ tīrntāṉ} eṉa -~um, {{"paṟṟuviṭutal"}}-- {kāmattiṉ+ paṟṟuviṭṭāṉ} eṉa -~um varum.

{{Par. 4}} {{"accam*"}} mutaliya nāṉku* -um oḻittu oḻinta irupattunāṉkuum otta paṇpu vēṟupāṭu paṟṟi+ porūupporuḷ viritta āṟu.

{{Par. 5}} aṉṉa piṟa um eṉṟataṉāl, {avaṉiṉ aḷiyaṉ ivaṉ, ataṉiṉ+ cēyttuitu, ikaḻcciyiṉ+ keṭṭāṉ, makiḻcciyiṉ mintuṟṟāṉ} eṉpaṉapōlvaṉa koḷka.

{{Par. 6}} pala vāypāṭṭōṭu vaḻakkiṉkaṇ+ payiṉṟu varutal uṭimiyāṉ+porūu+ poruḷ itaṟku+ ciṟappu uṭittu ākaliṉ, perumpāṉmi umataṉi ē virittār. ((17))

((7. āṟām vēṟṟumi))

((atu urupiṉ poruḷ))

TC 79 c

[TRANSLATION]

āṟu ākuvatu ē
atu eṉa+ peyariya vēṟṟumi+ kiḷavi
taṉṉiṉ um piṟitiṉ um itaṉatu itu eṉum
aṉṉa kiḷavi+ kiḻamittu atu ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: atu eṉa+ peyar koṭuttu ōtappaṭṭa vēṟṟumiccol āṟāvatuām. atu uṭiyatu āy niṟkum^ taṉṉāṉ um piṟitoṉṟāṉ um `itaṉatuitu' eṉpatu paṭa-niṟkum^ kiḷaviyiṉ+ ^tōṉṟum^ kiḻamiyi+poruḷ āka uṭittu; e-ṟu.

{{C CENV}} eṉa ē, āṟāvatu kiḻami+ poruṭṭu eṉṟu* -um, a+ kiḻami taṉṉāṉvanta taṟkiḻami um piṟitāṉ vanta piṟitiṉkiḻami um eṉairaṇṭu eṉṟu* -um^ kūṟiya āṟu ām.

{{Par. 2}} taṉ= eṉṟatu taṉṉōṭu oṟṟumi uṭiya poruḷi, piṟitu eṉṟatutaṉṉiṉ vēṟu ākiya poruḷi.

{{Par. 3}} aḵtēl, taṉṉiṉ um piṟitiṉ um ākiya kiḻamittu eṉṉātu{{"itaṉatu itu eṉum aṉṉa kiḷavi+ kiḻamittu"}} eṉṟatu eṉṉieṉiṉ:-- poruḷ+-kiḻami -~um, paṇpu+-kiḻami -~um,toḻil+-kiḻami -~um avi pōlvaṉa -~um eṉa+ kiḻami tām pala,avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu cuṭṭātu {{"itaṉatu itu"}} eṉṉum^ collāṉ+^tōṉṟum kiḻami māttiram cuṭṭum eṉṟaṟku {{"itaṉatu itu eṉumaṉṉa kiḷavi+ kiḻamittu"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 4}} itaṉa ivi eṉṉum paṉmi urupu+ toṭar um aṭaṅkutaṟku {{"aṉṉakiḷavi"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 5}} oṉṟu pala kuḻīiya taṟkiḻami um, vēṟu pala kuḻīiyataṟkiḻami um oṉṟiyaṟkiḻami um, uṟuppiṉ kiḻami um, meytirintu āya taṟkiḻami ~um eṉa+ taṟkiḻami aintuvakippaṭum.

{{Par. 6}} poruḷiṉ kiḻami um, nilattiṉ kiḻami um, kālattiṉ kiḻami umeṉa+ piṟitiṉ kiḻami mū vaki+ paṭum. avaṟṟiṟku utāraṇammuṉṉar+ kāṭṭutum. ((18))

((apporuḷiṉ pākupāṭukaḷ))

TC 80 c

[TRANSLATION]

iyaṟkiyiṉ uṭimiyiṉ muṟimiyiṉ kiḻamiyiṉ
ceyaṟkiyiṉ mutumiyiṉ viṉiyiṉ eṉṟā
karuviyiṉ tuṇiyiṉ kalattiṉ mutaliṉ
oruvaḻi uṟuppiṉ kuḻuviṉ eṉṟā
terintu moḻi+ ceytiyiṉ niliyiṉ vāḻcciyiṉ
tirintu vēṟupaṭūum piṟa um aṉṉa
kūṟiya maruṅkiṉ+ tōṉṟum^ kiḷavi
āṟaṉ pāla eṉmaṉār pulavar.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: {{"iyaṟki"}} mutal āka {{"vāḻcci"}} īṟu āka+collappaṭṭaṉa um tirintu vēṟupaṭūum aṉṉa piṟa um ākiya mēl+kūṟappaṭṭa kiḻami+ poruṭkaṇ tōṉṟum^ col= ellām āṟāmvēṟṟumi+ tiṟattaṉa eṉṟu colluvar pulavar; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} tirintu vēṟupaṭūum aṉṉa piṟa um eṉṟār āyiṉum, tirintuvēṟupaṭutal aṉṉa piṟa um eṉṟataṉāl taḻuva+ paṭuvaṉavaṟṟuḷorucāraṉavaṟṟiṟku ē koḷka.

{{Par. 3}} e-ṭu: {eḷḷatu kuppi, paṭiyatu kuḻām} eṉpaṉa kuḻūu+kiḻami. avi muṟiyāṉ ē oṉṟu pala kuḻīiyatu um vēṟu palakuḻīiyatu um ām. {cāttaṉatu iyaṟki, nilattatu akalam}eṉpaṉa iyaṟki+ kiḻami. {cāttaṉatu nilimi, cāttaṉatuillāmi} eṉpaṉa nili+ kiḻami. ivi oṉṟiyaṟkiḻami.{yāṉiyatu kōṭu, puliyatu ukir} eṉpaṉa uṟuppiṉ kiḻami.uṟuppu* -āvatu poruḷiṉ ēkatēcam eṉpatu aṟivittaṟku oruvaḻiuṟuppu eṉṟār. {cāttaṉatu ceyaṟki, cāttaṉatu kaṟṟaṟivu}eṉpaṉa ceyaṟki+ kiḻami. {aracaṉatu mutumi, aracaṉatumutirvu} eṉpaṉa mutumi+ kiḻami. mutumi eṉpatu piṟitu ōrkāraṇam paṟṟātu kālam paṟṟi orutali āka a+ poruṭkaṇ+^tōṉṟum paruvam ākaliṉ ceyaṟki -~uḷ aṭakkātu vēṟu kūṟiṉār.{cāttaṉatu toḻil, cāttaṉatu celavu} eṉpaṉa viṉi+ kiḻami.ivi mey tirintu āya taṟkiḻami. {cāttaṉatu uṭimi,cāttaṉatu tōṭṭam} eṉpaṉa uṭimi+ kiḻami. {maṟiyatu tāy,maṟiyatu tanti} eṉpaṉa muṟimi+ kiḻami. {iciyatu karuvi,vaṉikalattatu tikiri} eṉpaṉa karuvi+ kiḻami. {avaṉatutuṇi, avaṉatu iṇaṅku} eṉpaṉa tuṇi+ kiḻami. {nilattatuoṟṟikkalam, cāttaṉatu vilittīṭṭu} eṉpaṉa kala+ kiḻami.{oṟṟiyatu mutal, oṟṟiyatu poruḷ} eṉpaṉa mutal+ kiḻami.{kapilaratu pāṭṭu, paraṇaratu pāṭṭiyal} eṉpaṉa ceyyuḷ+kiḻami. terintu moḻiyāṉ+ ceyyappaṭutaliṉ {{"terintu moḻi+ceyti"}} āyiṉa. ivi poruḷ+ piṟitiṉkiḻami. {murukaṉatukuṟiñci nilam, veḷḷiyatu āṭci} eṉpaṉa kiḻami+ kiḻami.{kāṭṭatu^ yāṉi, yāṉiyatu kāṭu} eṉpaṉa vāḻcci+ kiḻami.avaṟṟuḷ {murukaṉatu kuṟiñci nilam, yāṉiyatu kāṭu} eṉpaṉanila+ piṟitiṉkiḻami. {kāṭṭatu^ yāṉi} eṉpatu poruḷ+piṟitiṉkiḻami. eṉiyatu kāla+ piṟitiṉkiḻami.

{{Par. 4}} tirintu vēṟupaṭūum aṉṉa piṟa um eṉṟataṉāl, {eḷ+ cāntu, kōṭṭunūṟu, cāttaṉatu oppu, tokiyatu viri, poruḷatu kēṭu, collatuporuḷ} eṉṉum^ toṭakkattaṉa koḷka. avaṟṟuḷ {eḷ+ cāntu, kōṭṭunūṟu} eṉpaṉa mutal āyiṉa muḻuvatūum^ tiritaliṉ, vēṟukūṟiṉār.

{{Par. 5}} mēl+ collappaṭṭa taṟkiḻami piṟitiṉkiḻami eṉpaṉavaṟṟiviritta āṟu. ((19))

((8. ēḻām vēṟṟumi))

((kaṇ urupiṉ poruḷ))

[TRANSLATION]

{{Par. 2}} kuṟippiṉ tōṉṟuvatu eṉṟatu avaṟṟi iṭam āka+ kuṟittavaḻi avvēṟṟumi tōṉṟum eṉṟa āṟu. eṉa ē, iṭam āka+kuṟikkappaṭātavaḻi a+ peyarkkaṇ vēṟṟumi tōṉṟātu eṉpatu ām.

{{Par. 3}} taṉ= iṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāṉ ēṉi vēṟṟumi+ coṟkaḷ um avva+poruṭkaṇ kuṟippu ilvaḻi avva+ peyarkkaṇ vārāmi koḷka.

{{Par. 4}} e-ṭu: {taṭṭuppuṭikkaṇ vantāṉ, māṭattiṉkaṇ iruntāṉ,kūtirkkaṇ vantāṉ} eṉa varum. ((20))
