{{[kali:37_8]}} eṉa -~um,
«kaṇ= akal* ñālam»
{{[puṟam:35_29]}} eṉa -~um {{"kaṇ="}} eṉṉum iṭiccollāṉ uṇarttappaṭumiṭapporuṇmi. {{"tēvaki"}} eṉpatu tici+ kūṟu. {{"kaṇ kālpuṟam akam uḷ="}} eṉpaṉa mutal āyiṉavaṟṟatu poruḷ vēṟṟumivaḻakku nōkki uṇarntu koḷka.

{{Par. 3}} {kaṇ niṉṟu colliyāṉi} {eṉkaṇ niṉṟu ivi collal+ pālialli} eṉṟu* -um, {ūr+ kāl+ ceyyi} {ūrkkaṇ+ cey} eṉṟum,{ūr+ puṟattu niṉṟa maratti} {ūrkkaṇ maram} eṉṟu* -um, {eyilakattu+ pukkāṉi} {eyiṟkaṇ+ pukkāṉ} eṉṟu* -um, {illuḷiruntāṉi} {iṟkaṇ iruntāṉ} eṉṟu* -um, {aracaṉ uḻiiruntāṉi} {aracaṉkaṇ iruntāṉ} eṉṟu* -um, {āliṉ kīḻ+ kiṭantaāvi} {āliṉkaṇ+ kiṭantatu} eṉṟu* -um, {marattiṉ mēl iruntakuraṅki} {marattiṉkaṇ iruntatu} eṉṟu* -um, {ēr+ piṉceṉṟāṉi} {ērkkaṇ+ ceṉṟāṉ} eṉṟu* -um, {kāṭṭu+ cārōṭuvataṉi+} {kāṭṭiṉkaṇ ōṭum} eṉṟu* -um, {uṟiyūrkku ayal*^niṉṟa cirāppaḷḷi+ kuṉṟi} {uṟiyūrkkaṇ+ kuṉṟu} eṉṟu* -um,{eyil+ puṭi niṉṟāri} {eyiṟkaṇ niṉṟār} eṉṟu* -um, {vaṭa pālvēṅkaṭam teṉ pāl+ kumari} eṉpaṉavaṟṟi {vaṭakkaṇ vēṅkaṭamteṟkaṇ+ kumari} eṉṟu* -um, {puli muṉ paṭṭāṉi+} {pulikkaṇ+paṭṭāṉ} eṉṟu* -um, {nūliṉ iṭi um^ kaṭi um^ tali um* niṉṟamaṅkalatti} {nūṟkaṇ maṅkalam} eṉṟu* -um, {ki valattuuḷḷataṉi+} {kikkaṇ uḷḷatu} eṉṟu* -um, {taṉ= iṭattunikaḻvataṉi+} {taṉkaṇ nikaḻvatu} eṉṟu* -um av= iṭa+ poruḷpaṟṟi ēḻām vēṟṟumi vanta āṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.

{{Par. 4}} {{"eṉāa"}} eṉpatu eṇ= iṭiccol.

{{Par. 5}} {{"kaṇ"}} mutal āyiṉa ellām urupu eṉṟār āl uriyāciriyareṉiṉ:-- urupu āyiṉ, ēḻāvataṟku+ {{"kaṇ="}} eṉpatu urupu ātalmēl ē peṟappaṭṭamiyāṉ+ peyarttu+ {{"kaṇ kāl"}} eṉṟalkūṟiyatu kūṟiṟṟu ām ākalāṉ um {ūr+ puṟattu iruntāṉ, ūrakattu iruntāṉ, ki valattu uḷḷatu koṭukkum} eṉa+ {puṟamakam valam} eṉpaṉavaṟṟuvaḻi attu+ cāriyi koṭuttu utāraṇam^kāṭṭiṉamiyāṉ um, avarkku atu karuttu aṉṟu eṉka. urupu* allaēl, {eṉ= uḻi, eṉ muṉ} eṉa nili moḻi urupiṟku ōtiya ceykipeṟṟa āṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ:-- {{"ataṉ+ poruṭṭu ākaliṉ"}}{{[col:76]}} eṉa -~um, «tam+ uṭiya taṇ= aḷi um^ tām um» eṉa-~um urupiṉ poruḷ paṭa varum piṟa moḻi vantuḻi um nili moḻia+ ceyki peṟṟu niṟṟaliṉ, a+ ceyki urupu puṇarccikku ēeṉṉum yāppuṟavu iṉṟu eṉka.

{{Par. 6}} aṉṉa piṟa um eṉṟataṉāl {poruṭkaṇ uṇarvu, eṉkaṇ aṉpu uṭiyaṉ,malarkkaṇ nāṟṟam, ākāyattukkaṇ+ paruntu} eṉṉum^ toṭakkattaṉakoḷka. ((21))

((9. vēṟṟumiyiṉ tokiviriiyalpu))

TC 83 c

 [TRANSLATION]

vēṟṟumi+ poruḷi virikkum^ kāli
īṟṟu niṉṟu iyalum^ toki vayiṉ pirintu
pal= āṟu āka+ poruḷ puṇarntu icikkum
ellā+ col= um uriya eṉpa.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: vēṟṟumi+ tokiyi virikkum iṭattu, vēṟṟumi ē aṉṟi,aṉmoḻi+ tokiyi virippuḻi+ pal= āṟṟāṉ aṉmoḻi+ poruḷoṭupuṇarntu varum ellā+ collum, virikkappaṭum; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} urupu toka+ poruḷ niṟṟaliṉ, vēṟṟumi+ tokiyi {{"vēṟṟumi+poruḷ"}} eṉṟār. tokiyi {{"vēṟṟumi+ poruḷ"}}{{[E302n,E332n]}} eṉṟu* -um {{"poruḷ"}} {{[col:95,100]}}eṉṟu* -um yāṇṭu* -um āḷpa.

{{Par. 3}} aṉmoḻi+ toki paṇpu+ toki mutal ākiya toki iṟuti niṉṟuiyaṟaliṉ, {{"īṟṟu niṉṟu iyalum^ toki"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 4}} {tāḻ kuḻal, poṉ+ ^toṭi, maṇ+ kāraṇam} eṉṉum aṉmoḻi+tokikaḷi virippuḻi, {tāḻ kuḻali uṭiyāḷ, poṟṟoṭiyiaṇintāḷ, maṇ= ākiya kāraṇattāṉ iyaṉṟatu} eṉa varum {uṭimium, aṇital um, iyaṟal um}, {karum^ kuḻal+ pēti, poṉ+ ^toṭiarivi, maṇ+ kuṭam} eṉṉum vēṟṟumi+-tokikaḷi virippuḻi-~um {karum^ kuḻali uṭiya pēti, poṉ+ ^toṭiyi aṇintaarivi, maṇṇāṉ iyaṉṟa kuṭam} eṉa vanta -~āṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.

{{Par. 5}} {{"poruḷ puṇarntu icikkum"}} eṉa ē, iru tokikkaṇ= um oruvāypāṭṭāṉ aṉṟi+ poruḷ citiyāmal uṇarttutaṟku ēṟṟa vāypāṭuellāvaṟṟāṉ -um varum eṉpatu* ām. avvāṟu ātal mēl+kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟuḷ kaṇṭukoḷka.

{{Par. 6}} aṉmoḻi+ tokiyiṉ+ col+ peytu virittal yāṇṭu+ peṟṟām eṉiṉ:--atu um anuvāta mukattāṉ īṇṭu ē peṟṟām eṉka. peṟa ē, iraṇṭutoki um virippuḻi varum vēṟupāṭu kūṟiya āṟu ām.

{{Par. 7}} itaṉi {{"vēṟṟumi+ toki -~ē uvami+-toki -~ē"}}{{[col:412]}} eṉṉum^ cūttirattiṉ piṉ vikka ~eṉiṉ:-- atu-~um* muṟi āyiṉum, iṉi varum^ cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ vēṟṟumi+-tokiviri paṟṟiya mayakkam uṇarttutalāṉ, āṇṭu+ paṭum* muṟimiuṇarttutal īṇṭu* -um iyipu*-uṭittu* eṉka.

{{Par. 8}} uriyāciriyar iraṇṭu cūttiram -āka aṟuttu āciriyarmata-vikaṟpam^ kūṟi+ `tam matam itu' eṉpatu pōtara oṉṟu*-āka urippār -um uḷar eṉṟār. iraṇṭu* āy oṉṟu āya-vaḻi+piṟitu* uri iṉmiyiṉ, uriyāciriyar karuttu itu -~ē ~ām.((22))

((vēṟṟumiyiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.))

((3. vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyal))

((vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ tammuḷ mayaṅki+ poruḷ vēṟupāṭukoḷḷum iyalpuṇarttuvatu.))

((1. vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷmayaṅkutal))

((cārpu poruḷil))

TC 84 c

[TRANSLATION]

karumam allā+ cārpu eṉ kiḷavikku
urimi um uṭittu ē kaṇ= eṉ vēṟṟumi.

{{Par. 1}}

{{C CENV}} mēl+ kūṟappaṭṭa vēṟṟumi tammuḷ mayaṅkum āṟu uṇarttiyaeṭuttukkoṇṭār. a+ mayakkam iruvakippaṭum; poruḷ mayakkamum, urupu mayakkam um eṉa. poruḷ-mayakkam -āvatu taṉ poruḷiṉ+ ^tīrātu piṟitoṉṟaṉporuṭkaṇ cēṟal. urupu-mayakkam -āvatu taṉ poruḷiṉ+ tīrntu cēṟal. {{"yātaṉ urupiṉ+ kūṟiṟṟu āyiṉum"}} {{[col:106]}} eṉpataṉāṉurupu-mayakkam uṇarttiṉār; allaṉavaṟṟāṉ ellāmporuḷ%-mayakkam uṇarttiṉār. iv= iruvaki mayakkam -umuṇarttutalāṉ, iv= ōttu vēṟṟumimayaṅkiyal eṉṉum peyarttuāyiṟṟu. mayakkam ē aṉṟi vēṟṟumikku ōtiya ilakkaṇattiṉ+piṟaḻntu vaḻīi amivaṉa um piṟa um iyipu uṭiyaṉa īṇṭuuṇarttutalāṉ, i+ kuṟi paṉmi nōkki+ ceṉṟa kuṟi eṉa uṇarka.

{{Par. 2}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: iraṇṭu āvataṟku ōtappaṭṭa cārpu poruṇmi karuma+ cārpuum^ {{"karumam allā+ cārpu"}} um eṉa iraṇṭu vaki+ paṭum.avaṟṟuḷ, karuma+ cārpu {tūṇi+ cārntāṉ} eṉa oṉṟaṉi oṉṟumeyyuṟutal ām. {{"karumam allā+ cārpu"}} meyyuṟutal iṉṟi{aracari+ cārntāṉ} eṉa varuvatu ām. {{"karumam allātacārpu"}} poruṇmikku urittu āy varutal um uṭittu ēḻāvatu;e-ṟu.

{{Par. 3}}

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu: {aracarkaṇ+ cārntāṉ} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 4}} karumam -āvatu īṇṭu meyyuṟutal āyiṉ, {aracarkaṇ+ cārntāṉ}eṉpuḻi uṟutal iṉmiyiṉ+ {{"cārpu"}} ām āṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ:--{tūṇ paṟṟu āka oru cāttaṉ cārntāṟpōla aracar paṟṟu āka a+cārntāṉ oḻukutaliṉ} {{"cārpu"}} āyiṟṟu eṉpatu.

{{Par. 5}} ummi etirmaṟi.

{{Par. 6}} {tūṇiṉkaṇ+ cārntāṉ} eṉa+ {karuma+ cārccikkaṇ} ēḻāvatuceṉṟāl+ paṭum iḻukku eṉṉi eṉiṉ:-- aṉṉatu ōr vaḻakku iṉmi ēpiṟitu illi eṉka. i+ kālattu av= āṟu vaḻaṅkupa āl eṉiṉ:--{karumam allā+ cārccikkaṇ} varum urupi+ {cārtal}oppumiyāṉ+ {karuma+ cārccikkaṇ=} um^ koṭuttu ulakattāriṭitterivu iṉṟi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟār eṉa ē paṭum; āciriyar{{"karumam allā+ cārpu eṉ kiḷavi"}} eṉa vitantu kūṟiṉamiyāṉeṉpatu. ((1))

((ciṉi+ peyaril))

TC 85 c

[TRANSLATION]

ciṉi nili+ kiḷavikku ai um^ kaṇ= um
viṉi nili okkum eṉmaṉār pulavar.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: ciṉi mēl niṟkum^ colliṟku iraṇṭāvatu* -um ēḻāvatu* -umviṉi-nilikkaṇ okkum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu: {kōṭṭi+ kuṟittāṉ, kōṭṭiṉkaṇ+ kuṟittāṉ} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 3}} viṉi+ kuṟippi nīkkutaṟku {{"viṉi"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 4}} {{"viṉi nili okkum"}} eṉṟār ēṉum, {pukaḻtal paḻittal}eṉṉum^ toṭakkattaṉa oḻittu mayaṅkutaṟku ēṟkum {aṟuttalkuṟittal} mutalāyiṉa viṉi ē koḷḷappaṭum.

{{Par. 5}} iraṇṭāvataṉ poruṭkaṇ ēḻāvatu ceṉṟatu āyiṉum, ciṟuvaraviṟṟuaṉṟi iraṇṭāvatu pōla ēḻāvatu vaḻakkiṉkaṇ+ payiṉṟu varutaliṉ,okkum eṉṟār; atu kāraṇattāl, {{"karumam allā+"}} {cārccikku}um ciṉi+ kiḷavikku um ēḻāvatu urimi uṭittu eṉauṭaṉkūṟātu, vēṟu kūṟiṉār eṉpatu. ((2))

((kaṉṟal cellal poruḷil))

TC 86 c

[TRANSLATION]

kaṉṟal um^ celavu* um oṉṟum ār viṉi ~ē.

{{WFW}}

{{Par. 1}} i-ḷ: {{"kaṉṟal"}} poruḷ% mēl varum^ col= um {{"celavu"}}poruḷ% mēl varum^ col= um iraṇṭāvataṟku um ēḻāvataṟku um orutoḻila; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu: {cūtiṉi+ kaṉṟiṉāṉ, cūtiṉkaṇ+ kaṉṟiṉāṉ; neṟiyi+ceṉṟāṉ, neṟikkaṇ+ ceṉṟāṉ} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 3}} poruḷ paṟṟi ōtiṉamiyāl, {cūtiṉi ivaṟiṉāṉ, cūtiṉkaṇivaṟiṉāṉ; neṟiyi naṭantāṉ, neṟikkaṇ* ^naṭantāṉ} eṉavaruvaṉa um^ koḷka.

{{Par. 4}} iraṇṭāvataṟku i+ poruḷ mēl ē kūṟappaṭṭamiyāl, {kaṉṟalcelavu} eṉṉum poruṭku ēḻāvatu um urittu eṉṟu ōta amiyumeṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉam ōtiṉ ##ēḻāvataṉ varavu ciṟupāṉmi# eṉpatupaṭum; ākaliṉ uṭaṉ-ōtiṉār eṉpatu.

{{Par. 5}} {{"ciṉi nili+ kiḷavi"}} nilimoḻi variyaṟi ākalāṉ um,itu varumoḻi variyaṟi ākalāṉ um, vēṟu kūṟiṉār eṉpatu.((3))

((mutal+ peyar ciṉi+ peyarkaḷil))

TC 87 c

[TRANSLATION]

mutal+ ciṉi+ kiḷavikku atu eṉ vēṟṟumi
mutal+ kaṇ variṉ ē ciṉikku ai varum ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: mutal+ collōṭu toṭarnta ciṉi+ colliṟku āṟām vēṟṟumimutaṟkaṇ varum āyiṉ, ciṉi+ colliṉkaṇ iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi ēvarum; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu: {yāṉiyatu kōṭṭi+ kuṟittāṉ} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 3}} {{"ciṉi nili+ kiḷavikku ai um^ kaṇ= um"}} {{[col:85]}}eṉṟataṉāṉ mutal+ ciṉi+ kiḷavikku um iraṇṭu vēṟṟumi umeytuvataṉi niyamittavāṟu. itu varum^ cūttirattiṟku umokkum. ((4))

TC 88 c

[TRANSLATION]

mutal muṉ ai variṉ kaṇ= eṉ vēṟṟumi
ciṉi muṉ varutal teḷḷitu eṉpa.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: a+ {{"mutal+ ciṉi+ kiḷavikkaṇ,"}} mutaṟcol muṉ aikāravēṟṟumi variṉ, ciṉiccol muṉ kaṇ= eṉṉum vēṟṟumi varutalteḷḷitu; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu: {yāṉiyi+ kōṭṭiṉkaṇ+ kuṟittāṉ} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 3}} teḷḷitu eṉṟataṉāṉ, {yāṉiyi+ kōṭṭi+ kuṟittāṉ} eṉa+ciṟupāṉmi aikāra vēṟṟumi um varum eṉpatu ām.

{{Par. 4}} ciṉi+ kiḷavikkaṇ mayakkam uṇarttukiṉṟa cūttirattu iṭi+{{"kaṉṟal um^ celavu um"}} eṉṉum^ cūttiram vittatu eṉṉieṉiṉ:-- {{"ai um^ kaṇ um"}} eṉpatu atikārattāṉ vara+cūttiram^ curuṅkum ākaliṉ, {{"ciṉi nili+ kiḷavikku"}}eṉṉum^ cūttirattiṉ piṉ ataṉi vittār eṉpatu. ((5))

TC 89 c

[TRANSLATION]

mutal um^ ciṉi um poruḷ vēṟupaṭāa
nuvalum^ kāli+ col+ kuṟippiṉa ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: mutal um^ ciṉi um mutal āyatu mutal ē āy+ ciṉi āyatuciṉi ē āy+ tammuḷ vēṟu poruḷ ākā; colluṅkāl, colluvāṉatucollutal+ kuṟippiṉāṉ mutal eṉṟu* -um^ ciṉi eṉṟu* -umvaḻaṅkappaṭum; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} col+ kuṟippiṉa eṉṟatu mutal eṉappaṭṭatu tāṉ ē taṉṉi+piṟitoṉṟaṟku ēkatēcam āka+ kuṟittavaḻi+ ciṉi um ām; ciṉieṉa+ paṭṭatu tāṉ ē taṉkaṇ ēkatēcatti nōkki mutal eṉa+kuṟittavaḻi mutal um ām e-ṟu.

{{Par. 3}} {kōṭṭatu nuṉiyi+ kuṟittāṉ, kōṭṭi nuṉikkaṇ+ kuṟittāṉ,kōṭṭi nuṉiyi+ kuṟittāṉ} eṉa mutaṟku ōtappaṭṭa urupuciṉikkaṇ= um vantatu* -āl eṉṟu aiyuṟṟārkku, karuttuvakiyāṉ+ {kōṭu} eṉpatu āṇṭu mutal āy-niṟṟaliṉ, mutaliṟkuōtiya urupu* -ē mutaṟkaṇ vantatu* eṉa, aiyam akaṟṟiyavāṟu.((6))

((piṇṭa+ peyaril))

TC 90 c

[TRANSLATION]

piṇṭa+ peyar um āy iyal tiriyā
paṇṭu iyal maruṅkiṉ marīiya marapu ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{C CENV}} i-ḷ: piṇṭatti uṇarttum peyar -um mutal+ ciṉi+ peyariyalpiṉ+ tiriyā; avvāṟu avaṟṟi mutal -um^ ciṉi -~um ākavaḻaṅkutal mēṟṟoṭṭu vaḻaṅki_vārā-niṉṟa kūṟṟāṉ maruviyamuṟi; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}}
piṇṭam pala poruḷ+ tokuti.

{{Par. 3}} īṇṭu {{"āy iyal tiriyā"}} eṉa māṭṭeṟintatu, {{"mutal+ ciṉi+kiḷavikku+"}} pōla+ piṇṭa+ peyarkku mutaṟkaṇ āṟāvatu variṉciṉikku iraṇṭāvatu varutal um, mutaṟku iraṇṭāvatu variṉciṉikku ēḻāvatu varutal um, ciṟupāṉmi iraṇṭāvatu varutalum ām.

{{Par. 4}} e-ṭu: {kuppiyatu taliyi+ citaṟiṉāṉ, kuppiyi+ talikkaṇ+citaṟiṉāṉ, kuppiyi+ taliyi+ citaṟiṉāṉ} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 5}} piṇṭam um mutal um^ ciṉi um ē ākaliṉ vēṟu kūṟal vēṇṭāeṉiṉ:-- {kuppi} eṉpuḻi, tokka pala poruḷ allatu, avaṟṟiṉvēṟu āy avaṟṟāṉ iyaṉṟu tāṉ oṉṟu eṉappaṭum poruḷ iṉmiyiṉ,tollāciriyar ataṉi mutal eṉṟu vēṇṭār; ataṉāṉ vēṟu kūṟiṉāreṉpatu. {paṭi kāṭu kā} mutalāyiṉa um aṉṉa. ((7))

((uyarpu poruḷil))

TC 91 c

[TRANSLATION]

oru viṉi oṭu+ col uyarpiṉ vaḻittu ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: {{"ataṉōṭu iyinta oru viṉi+ kiḷavi"}} eṉamūṉṟāvataṟku ōtiya {{"oru viṉi oṭu+ col"}} uyar-poruḷiuṇarttum peyar vaḻi+ tōṉṟum; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu: {aracaṉōṭu iḷiyar vantār, āciriyaṉoṭu māṇākkarvantār} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 3}} uyar poruḷ+ peyar vaḻi oṭu+ koṭukka eṉa ē, uyarpu il vaḻi+{cāttaṉ um^ koṟṟaṉ um vantār} eṉa iru peyar um eḻuvāy āyniṟkum eṉpatu ām. {nāyoṭu nampi vantāṉ} eṉa iḻipeyarkkaṇ= um{{"oru viṉi oṭu+ col"}} vantatu āl eṉiṉ:-- yātāṉ um ōrāṟṟāṉ ataṟku uyarpu uṇṭāyiṉ allatu avvāṟu kūṟār; kūṟupaāyiṉ, aḵtu {{"oru viṉi oṭu+ col"}} eṉappaṭātu,{kipporuḷoṭu vantāṉ} eṉpatu pōla, {atu taṉakku uṇṭākavantāṉ} eṉa+ piṟitu poruḷ paṭuvatu ōr oṭu urupu ām eṉka.

{{Par. 4}} itaṉi vēṟṟumi ōttiṉkaṇ {{"ataṉiṉ iyaṟal"}} {{[col:74]}}eṉṉum^ cūttirattiṉ piṉ viyātu īṇṭu vittatu eṉṉi eṉiṉ:--vēṟṟumiyatu peyar um muṟi um^ toki um potu vakiyāṉavaṟṟatu ilakkaṇam um uṇarttiyatu allatu, `iṉṉa vēṟṟumiiṉṉa poruṭkaṇ iṉṉa āṟu ām' eṉṉum vicēṭa ilakkaṇam atikārampaṭṭu iṉṟu ākalāṉ, āṇṭu viyātu, mūṉṟāvatu ipporuṭkaṇ ivvāṟuām eṉa vicēṭa ilakkaṇam {{"mūṉṟaṉ um aintaṉ um"}}{{[col:92]}} eṉa+ kūṟukiṉṟār ākaliṉ, ataṉōṭu iyiya īṇṭuvittār; itu um oru poruḷ paṟṟi ōtukiṉṟa vicēṭa ilakkaṇamākalāṉ eṉpatu. {{C CENV}} potu-vakiyāṉ {{"iru vayiṉ* ^niliyum"}} eṉṟār -ēṉum,{vaḻi pōyiṉār -ellām^ kūṟi kōṭpaṭṭār} eṉṟavaḻi+ {kūṟikōṭpaṭutal} {kaṭavuḷari} oḻittu {ēṉiyōrkku*} -ē āyiṉa-~āṟu pōla ētu+-poruṭkaṇ varum mūṉṟāvatu* -um aintāvatu* -umoru poruḷ -ē paṟṟi-niṟṟaliṉ {{"iru vayiṉ* ^niliyaṟku"}}ēlāmiyāṉ, avaṟṟi oḻittu aḵtu ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟku* -ē ~ām.eṉṉi? ēṉiya taṉ poruḷiṉ+ ^tīrātu piṟitu oṉṟaṉ poruṭkaṇ+cēṟal uṭimiyāṉ {{"iru vayiṉ* ^niliyaṟku"}} ēṟṟa āṟuaṟika.

{{Par. 3}} aṉṉa piṟa āvaṉa -- {nōyiṉ* ^nīṅkiṉāṉ nōyi nīṅkiṉāṉ} eṉa-~um, {cāttaṉi vekuṇṭāṉ cāttaṉoṭu vekuṇṭāṉ} eṉa -~um,{muṟiyāṉ+ kuttum^ kuttu muṟiyiṉ+ kuttum^ kuttu} eṉa -~um,{kaṭaloṭu kāṭu oṭṭātu kaṭali+ kāṭu oṭṭātu} eṉa -~umvaruvaṉa -~um piṟa -~um ām.

{{Par. 4}} ikkālattu+ citintu vaḻaṅkum mayakkam -um uṇmiyāl, {{"tol%neṟi piḻiyātaṉa"}} ārāyntu uṇara+-paṭum eṉpār{{"teriyumōrkku"}} eṉṟār. ((18))

TC 110 c

[TRANSLATION]

`itaṉatu itu iṟṟu' eṉṉum^ kiḷavi -~um
ataṉi+ koḷḷum poruḷ vayiṉāṉ -um
ataṉāṉ+ ceyaṟ paṭaṟku otta kiḷavi um
muṟikkoṇṭu eḻunta peyar+ col+ kiḷavi um
pāl vari kiḷavi um paṇpiṉ ākkam um
kālattiṉ aṟiyum vēṟṟumi+ kiḷavi um
paṟṟuviṭu kiḷavi um tīrntu moḻi+ kiḷavi um
aṉṉa piṟa um nāṉkaṉ urupiṉ+
^tol% neṟi marapiṉa tōṉṟal āṟu ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: `itaṉatu itu i+-taṉmittu' eṉṉum āṟāvataṉ poruṇmi-~um, oṉṟaṉi oṉṟu koḷḷum eṉṉum iraṇṭāvataṉ poruṇmi -~um,oṉṟaṉāṉ oṉṟu toḻiṟpaṭaṟku okkum eṉṉum mūṉṟāvataṉ poruṇmi-~um, muṟi+-poruṇmiyi+ koṇṭu-niṉṟa peyar+ colliṉatu āṟāmvēṟṟumi+ poruṇmi -~um, nilatti varintu kūṟum poruṇmi-~um, paṇpiṉkaṇ ām poruvu* -um ākiya aintāvataṉ poruṇmi-~um, kālattiṉkaṇ= aṟiyappaṭum ēḻām vēṟṟumi+ poruṇmi -~um,paṟṟuviṭu poruṇmi -~um, tīrntu moḻi+ poruṇmi -~um ākiyaaintāvataṉ poruṇmi -~um, avi pōlvaṉa piṟa -~um, nāṉkaṉurupāṉ+ ^tōṉṟutaṟkaṇ tol% neṟi marapiṉa; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu: {yāṉikku+ kōṭu kūritu} eṉa -~um, {ivaṭku+ koḷḷum iv=aṇi} eṉa -~um, {avaṟku+ ceyya+ takum a+ kāriyam} eṉa -~um,{āviṟku+ kaṉṟu} eṉa -~um, {karuvūrkku+ kiḻakku} eṉa -~um,{cāttaṟku neṭiyaṉ} eṉa -~um, {kālikku varum} eṉa -~um,{maṉi vāḻkkikku+ paṟṟuviṭṭāṉ} eṉa -~um, {ūrkku+ tīrntāṉ}eṉa -~um, a+ poruḷ ellāvaṟṟiṉkaṇ= um nāṉku ām urupu vantaāṟu kaṇṭu koḷka.

{{Par. 3}} {val% pāl mel% pāl} eṉpa ākaliṉ nilatti+ {{"pāl"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 4}}
«ciṟappu ē nalaṉ ē kātal valiyōṭu
a+ nāl+ paṇpu* -um nili+-kaḷam eṉpa»
{{[poruḷ_uvamaviyal:4]}} eṉa uvami nili+ kaḷatti+ paṇpu eṉpa ākaliṉ, {{"paṇpiṉākkam"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 5}} itu um vēṟṟumi mayakkam ākaliṉ mēl+ kūṟappaṭṭavaṟṟōṭuviyātu ittuṇi um pōtantu vittatu eṉṉi eṉiṉ:-- atu tokivirippa mayaṅkum atikāram; itu toki al vaḻi {yāṉiyatu kōṭukūritu} eṉṉum^ toṭarmoḻi+ poruḷ citiyāmal {yāṉikku+ kōṭukūritu} eṉa nāṉkāvatu āṇṭu+ ceṉṟu-niṉṟatu ākalāṉ, avaṟṟōṭuviyār āyiṉār eṉpatu.

{{Par. 6}} aḵtēl, {āviṉ kaṉṟu} eṉa -~um {karuvūr+ kiḻakku} eṉa -~umtoki āy um varutaliṉ, apporuṭkaṇ nāṉkāvatu varutal īṇṭu+kūṟal+ pāṟṟu aṉṟu eṉiṉ:-- aḵtu okkum* maṉ= āyiṉum, piṟavēṟṟumi+ poruṭkaṇ nāṉkāvatu cēṟal oppumiyāṉ avaṟṟiṟku vēṟuōr cūttiram^ ceyyātu ivaṟṟōṭu oruṅku ōtiṉār eṉpatu.

{{Par. 7}} aṉṉa piṟa um eṉṟataṉāṉ, {ūrkkaṇ+ ceṉṟāṉ, ūrkkaṇ uṟṟatuceyvāṉ} eṉṉum ēḻāvataṉ poruṭkaṇ= um, {ūriṉ+ cēyaṉ} eṉṉumaintāvataṉ poruṭkaṇ= um, {ūrkku+ ceṉṟāṉ, ūrkku uṟṟatuceyvāṉ, ūrkku+ cēyaṉ} eṉa nāṉkāvatu varutal koḷka. piṟa umaṉṉa. ((27))

((ARUMUGA NAVALAR))

(( oru peyarppaṭṭateṉa mēlum vantavāṟu kaṇṭu koḷka. aṉmoḻittoki eccaviyaluḷuṇarttap paṭum; ataṉāl avvāku peyarātaluṭimi paṟṟi īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār;)) eccaviyaluḷ+ kūṟappaṭṭa āyiṉum viṉiyecca mutal āyiṉaviṉiccol ātal um iṭiccol= ātal um uṭimiyāṉ avaṟṟiviṉiyiyal uḷ= um iṭiyiyal uḷ= um^ kūṟiya āṟu pōla eṉpatu.

{{Par. 4}} {tolkāppiyaṉāṉ -um^ kapilaṉāṉ -um^ ceyyappaṭṭa nūli+tolkāppiyam^ kapilam eṉṟal} {{"viṉimutal urikkum^kiḷavi"}} eṉṟār āl uriyāciriyar eṉiṉ:-- aṟṟu aṉṟu; oru moḻiilakkaṇam īṇṭu um^ kūṟār āyiṉum {veṟpu+ cērppu} eṉṉum peyariṟuti {itaṉi uṭiyāṉ} eṉṉum poruḷ tōṉṟa aṉ= eṉpatu ōriṭiccol vantu {veṟpaṉ cērppaṉ} eṉa niṉṟāṟpōla, {tolkāppiyaṉkapilaṉ} eṉṉum peyar iṟuti ivaṉāṉ+ ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṉum poruḷtōṉṟa am= eṉpatu ōr iṭiccol vantu aṉ keṭa+ {tolkāppiyamkapilam} eṉa niṉṟaṉa eṉpatu āciriyar karuttu ām; ataṉāṉ aviutāraṇam ātal uriyāciriyar karuttu aṉṟu eṉka.

{{Par. 5}} {{"aṉiya marapiṉa"}} eṉṟatu avvāṟu^ yātu* -āṉum -ōr iyipupaṟṟi oṉṟaṉ peyar oṉṟaṟku ātal eṉa ākupeyar ilakkaṇattiṟku+tōṟṟuvāy ceyta -~āṟu. oṉṟaṉ poruṭkaṇ oṉṟu cēṟal eṉṉumoppumiyāṉ ivaṟṟi īṇṭu* -um^ kūṟiṉār. aḵtēl āku-peyareḻuvāy-vēṟṟumi mayakkam ātalāṉ īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉār eṉṟār -āluri-~āciriyar eṉiṉ:-- āku-peyar ēṉi vēṟṟumi -~umēṟṟu-niṟṟalāṉ -um, eḻuvāy-vēṟṟumi āy-niṉṟa -vaḻi -~um atupiṟitu* -ōr vēṟṟumi+ poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟu mayaṅkāmiyiṉ vēṟṟumimayakkam eṉa+-paṭāmiyāṉ -um atu pōli-~uri eṉka. ((4. viḷimarapu))

{{C CENV}} {{"nila+ peyar"}} -- {aruvāḷaṉ, cōḻiyaṉ} eṉpaṉa. {{"kuṭi+peyar"}} -- {maliyamāṉ, cēramāṉ} eṉpaṉa. {{"kuḻuviṉpeyar"}} -- {aviyattār, attikōcattār} eṉpaṉa. {{"viṉi+peyar"}} -- {varuvār, celvār} eṉpaṉa; {taccaṉ, kollaṉ}eṉpaṉa um avi. {{"uṭi+ peyar"}} -- {amparkiḻāṉ,pērūrkiḻāṉ} eṉpaṉa; {veṟpaṉ, cērppaṉ} eṉpaṉa um avi.{{"paṇpu koḷ peyar"}} -- {kariyāṉ, ceyyāṉ} eṉpaṉa.{{"pallōr+ kuṟitta muṟi nili+ peyar"}} -- {tantiyar,tāyar} eṉpaṉa. {{"pallōr+ kuṟitta ciṉi nili+ peyar"}} --{peruṅkālar, peruntōḷar} eṉpaṉa. {{"pallōr+ kuṟitta tiṇinili+ peyar"}} -- {pārppār, aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar, āyar,vēṭṭuvar} eṉpaṉa. {{"pallōr+ kuṟitta"}} eṉṟu vicēṭittalāṉ,i+ mū vaki+ peyar uḷ orumi+ peyar iraṇṭu tiṇikku um uriyaām. {{"kūṭi varu vaḻakkiṉ āṭu iyaṟpeyar"}} --{paṭṭiputtirar, kaṅkimāttirar} eṉpaṉa. ivi āṭal kuṟittuiḷiyār pakuti paṭa+ kūṭiyavaḻi allatu vaḻaṅka+ paṭāmiyiṉ+{{"kuḻuviṉ peyariṉ"}} vēṟu āyiṉa. a+ {{"kuḻuviṉ peyar"}} orutuṟikkaṇ urimi pūṇṭa pallōr mēl ekkālattum nikaḻvaṉa.{{"iṉṟivar eṉṉum eṇ= iyal+ peyar"}} āvaṉa {oruvar, iruvar,muppattumūvar} eṉpaṉa. {{"iṉṟivar"}} eṉpatu ittuṇiyar eṉṉumporuṭṭu+ pōlum. eṇ= ākiya iyalpu paṟṟi+ poruḷ uṇarttutalāṉ{{"eṇ= iyal+ peyar"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 3}} oru nimittam paṟṟi+ cēṟaliṉ+ pal peyar oru peyar ākaaṭakkappaṭṭamiyāṉ, {{"nila+ peyar"}} mutalāyiṉavaṟṟi vēṟukūṟiṉār. aḵtēl, {{"oppoṭu varūum^ kiḷavi"}} um aṉṉatu ākaliṉivaṟṟoṭu vikkal+ pāṟṟu eṉiṉ:-- aṟṟu ēṉum, vaḻakku+ payiṟciiṉmiyāṉ avaṟṟoṭu vittār eṉka. ((11))

((viravuppeyariṉ toki, vaki))

{{Par. 4}} {{"ciṉippeyar"}} āvaṉa {perum^ kālaṉ, muṭavaṉ} eṉa a+ ciṉi~uṭimi ākiya nimittam paṟṟi mutal% mēl varum peyar.{{"ciṉimutaṟpeyar"}} āvaṉa {cīttali+ cāttaṉ, koṭum puṟamaruti} eṉa+ ciṉi+ peyaroṭu toṭarntu varum mutaṟpeyar.{cāttaṉ, maruti} eṉṉum* mutaṟpeyar ciṉippeyaroṭu toṭarntuallatu poruḷ uṇarttāmiyiṉ+ {{"ciṉimutaṟpeyar"}} āyiṉa.{{"muṟippeyar"}} āvaṉa {tanti tāy} eṉa muṟi paṟṟi muṟiuṭi+ poruḷ% mēl varuvaṉa. muṟi -~āvatu piṟaviyāṉoruvaṉōṭu* oruvaṟku varum iyipu. allaṉa aintu um^tammi uṇartti niṉṟa ākalāṉ, {tām} eṉpatu mutal ākiya col= ēām.

{{Par. 5}} {{"piṟa um"}} eṉṟataṉāṉ, {maka, kuḻavi} pōlvaṉa koḷka.ivaṟṟi uyartiṇi+ peyar eṉṟār āl uriyāciriyar eṉiṉ:--marapiyal uḷ,
«maka um piḷḷi um paṟaḻ um pārppu um
avi um aṉṉa a+ pālāṉa.»
{{[marapiyal-14]}} eṉa -~um,
«kuñcaram peṟum ē kuḻavi+ peyar+ koṭi.»
{{[marapiyal-19]}} eṉa -~um, avi aḵṟiṇikku ātal kūṟi,
«kuḻavi um maka um āy iraṇṭu allavi
kiḻava alla makkaṭkaṇ= ē.»
{{[marapiyal-23]}} eṉa uyartiṇikku um ōti vittār ākaliṉ, avi viravu+ peyar ēām; ataṉāṉ atu pōliyuri eṉka.

{{Par. 6}} oru nimittattāṉ iraṇṭu tiṇi+ poruḷ um uṇarttutaliṉ viravu+peyar poruḷ* ^tōṟum* nimitta vēṟupāṭu uṭiya {{"pala poruḷoru col"}} aṉmi aṟika. ((20))

«veṇ kuṭi+ peru viṟal» eṉṟa vaḻi+ {cem^ kuṭi} mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu iyipu nīkkātu {veṇkuṭiyōṭu} iyipu iṉmi māttiri nīkki {veṇ kuṭiyāṉ} eṉpatupaṭa niṟṟaliṉ aḵtu iyipu iṉmi nīkkal ām. {karum^ kuvaḷi}eṉṟavaḻi+ {cemmi} mutal āyiṉavaṟṟōṭu iyipu nīkkaliṉ, itupiṟitiṉ iyipu nīkkal ām. {{"paṉmi cuṭṭiya peyar"}} eṉpatu,«veṇ kuṭi+ peru viṟal» eṉpatu pōla, orumi iyipu nīkkātupaṉmi cuṭṭutalōṭu iyipu iṉmi māttiri nīkki+ ##paṉmicuṭṭum# eṉpatu paṭa-niṉṟatu. ataṉāṉ vicēṭikkuṅkāl+ piṟitiṉiyipu nīkkal oru-tali ~aṉṟu* eṉka. aḵṟiṇi orumi um a+tiṇi+ paṉmi um uyartiṇi orumi um ākiya palavaṟṟi umuṇarttalāṉ+ {{"paṉmi cuṭṭiya peyar"}} eṉpār um uḷar. aḵtuuriyāciriyar karuttu aṉmi av= uriyāṉ viḷaṅkum.

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: mēl% mū vakiya eṉappaṭṭa viṉiccol+ ^tām {{"am ām emēm"}} eṉṉum īṟṟa ākiya col= um, {{"ummoṭu varūum^ ka ṭa taṟa"}} ākiya kum= um ṭum= um tum= um ṟum= um eṉṉum īṟṟa ākiyacol= um eṉa, av= eṭṭu um paṉmi uṇarttum^ taṉmi+ col= ām;e-ṟu.

{{Par. 3}}

{{C CENV}} taṉakku orumi allatu iṉmiyiṉ taṉmippaṉmi -āvatu taṉṉoṭu piṟari uḷappaṭuttal ē ām. av= uḷappaṭuttal mū vakippaṭum; muṉṉiṉṟāri uḷappaṭuttalum, paṭarkkiyāri uḷappaṭuttal um, av= iruvari um oruṅkuuḷappaṭuttal um eṉa.

{{Par. 4}} am ām eṉpaṉa muṉṉiṉṟāri uḷappaṭukkum; tamar āya vaḻi+paṭarkkiyāri um uḷappaṭukkum. em ēm eṉpaṉa paṭarkkiyāriuḷappaṭukkum. ummoṭu varūum^ ka ṭa ta ṟa av= iruvari umoruṅku uḷappaṭuttal um^ taṉittaṉi uḷappaṭuttal um uṭiya.

{{Par. 11}} iṉi avi nikaḻkālam paṟṟi varuṅkāl, nil, kiṉṟu eṉpaṉavaṟṟōṭuvarum. nil= eṉpatu lakāram ṉakāram āy ṟakāram peṟṟu niṟkum.

{{Par. 18}} kum= īṟu, viṉi koṇṭu muṭitaliṉ, oḻinta um= īṟṟiṉ vēṟu eṉa ēpaṭum. ṭa ta ṟa eṉpaṉa, etirkālattiṟku uriya eḻuttuaṉmiyāl, pāl uṇarttum iṭiccoṟku uṟuppu āy vantaṉa eṉa ēpaṭum. paṭa ē, avaṟṟi uṟuppu āka uṭiya īṟu mūṉṟu ām;ataṉāṉ {{"um="}} eṉa ōr īṟu āka aṭakkal ākāmiyiṉ, {{"a+nāl+ kiḷaviyoṭu"}} eṉṟār. [TRANSLATION]

avaṟṟu uḷ
paṉmi urikkum taṉmi+ kiḷavi
eṇ= iyal% maruṅkiṉ tiripavi uḷa ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: kūṟappaṭṭa irupattu mūṉṟu coṟkaḷ uḷ, paṉmi uṇarttum^taṉmiccol eṇ= iyalum vaḻi aḵṟiṇiyi uḷappaṭuttu+ tirivaṉauḷa; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu: {yāṉ um eṉ eḵkam um^ cāṟum} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 3}} taṉmi+ paṉmi viṉiccol, uyartiṇi viṉi ākaliṉ, uyartiṇiē uḷappaṭuttal+ pālaṉa; aḵṟiṇiyi uḷappaṭuttal vaḻu āyiṉumamikka eṉpār, {{"tiripavi uḷa"}} eṉṟār. ataṉāṉ i+cūttiratti {{"muṉṉuṟa+ kiḷanta uyartiṇiyav= ē"}}{{[col:208]}} eṉṉum cūttirattiṉ piṉ vittār.

{{Par. 4}} tiriyum eṉṉātu {{"tiripavi uḷa"}} eṉṟataṉāṉ, ellām^ tiriyācila ē tirivaṉa eṉpatu ām. ((12))

((yār eṉṉum kuṟippu muṟṟu))

TC 210 c

[TRANSLATION]

yāar eṉṉum viṉāviṉ kiḷavi
attiṇi maruṅkiṉ mu+ pāṟku um urittu ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: {yār} eṉṉum viṉā+ poruḷi uṇarttum^ col uyartiṇimaruṅkiṉ mu+ pāṟku um urittu; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu: {avaṉ yār, avaḷ yār, avar yār} eṉa varum.
«ūti kūṭṭu uṇṇum uku paṉi yāmattu em^
kōti kūṭṭu uṇṇiya tāṉ yār maṉ -- pōtu ellām^
tātoṭu tāḻum^ tār+ kacci vaḷa nāṭaṉ
tūtoṭu vārāta vaṇṭu.» eṉpuḻi, {vaṇṭu tāṉ yār} eṉa, {yār} eṉpatu aḵṟiṇikkaṇ= umvantatu āl eṉiṉ:-- atu tiṇi vaḻuvamiti eṉappaṭum.

{{Par. 3}} itu viṉikkuṟippu āyiṉum, pallōr paṭarkki uṇarttum ār īṟṟiṉmūṉṟu pāl uṇarttum vēṟupāṭu uṭimiyāṉ, avaṟṟoṭu viyātuīṇṭu vittār.

{{Par. 4}} ceyyuḷ iṉpa nōkki aḷapu eḻuntu niṉṟatu.

{{Par. 5}} {{"viṉāviṉ kiḷavi"}} eṉa ataṉ poruḷ uṇarttiya āṟu. ((13))

TC 213 c

[TRANSLATION]

atu+ col vēṟṟumi uṭimiyāṉ um^
kaṇ= eṉ vēṟṟumi nilattiṉāṉ um
oppiṉāṉ um paṇpiṉāṉ um eṉṟu
a+ pāl+ kālam^ kuṟippoṭu tōṉṟum.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: āṟām vēṟṟumiyatu uṭimi+ poruṭkaṇ= um, ēḻāmvēṟṟumiyatu nila+ poruṭkaṇ= um, oppiṉkaṇ= um, paṇpiṉkaṇ= umeṉa a+ pakuti+ kālam^ kuṟippāṉ+ ^tōṉṟum.

{{Par. 2}} a+ pakuti+ kālam -āvatu a+ poruḷ+ pakuti paṟṟi varum^col= akattu+ kālam ām.

{{Par. 3}} a+ pakuti+ kālam^ kuṟippāṉ+ ^tōṉṟum eṉa ē, a+ poruḷ paṟṟiviṉikkuṟippu varum eṉṟa āṟu ām.

{{Par. 4}} uṭiyāṉatu uṭimittaṉmi ē aṉṟi uṭipporuḷ um uṭimieṉappaṭutaliṉ {{"uṭimiyāṉ um"}} eṉpataṟku uṭipporuṭkaṇ=um eṉa -~um urikka. urikka ē, uṭipporuṭkaṇ varuṅkāl,uṭipporuḷ+ col= ākiya mutaṉili paṟṟi-varutal -umpeṟappaṭum. {karumiyaṉ} eṉpatu uṭimi+ poruḷ āy aṭaṅkaliṉ,{{"paṇpiṉāṉ -um"}} eṉpuḻi+ {kariyaṉ} eṉa `iṉṉaṉ' eṉpatupaṭa-varutal ē koḷka. vāḷātu uṭimiyāṉ um eṉṟavaḻi{{"aṉmiyiṉ"}} {{[col:214]}} eṉpatu pōla av= oru vāypāṭu* -ēpaṟṟi-niṟkum. `itaṉi iḵtu uṭittu' eṉpatu paṭa-varum ellāvāypāṭu -um^ taḻuvutaṟku {{"atu+ col vēṟṟumi uṭimiyāṉ-um"}} eṉṟār. {{"kaṇ= eṉ vēṟṟumi nilattiṉāṉ um"}} eṉpataṟkuum ītu okkum. āyiṉ iḵtu iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi+ poruḷ ām eṉiṉ:--āṇṭu uṭimi urupu nōkkiya col= āy varuvatu allatu iraṇṭāmvēṟṟumi+ poruḷ eṉappaṭātu. eṉṉi? atu ceyappaṭu poruṇmittuākaliṉ. ataṉāṉ uṭimi āṟāvataṉ poruḷ eṉa ē paṭum eṉpatu.

{{Par. 5}} e-ṭu:
«kacciṉaṉ kaḻaliṉaṉ»
{{[akam:76_7]}} eṉa -~um, {illattaṉ, puṟattaṉ} eṉa -~um, {poṉ+ aṉṉaṉ, pulipōlvaṉ} eṉa -~um, {kariyaṉ, ceyyaṉ} eṉa -~um varum.

{{Par. 6}} {kacciṉāṉ, illattāṉ} eṉa+ peyar -um^ kuṟippāṉ+ kālamviḷakkaliṉ {{"a+ pāl+ kālam^ kuṟippoṭu tōṉṟum"}} eṉṟataṉāṉviṉi+-kuṟippu* eṉpatu peṟum -āṟu* eṉṉi ~eṉiṉ -- {{"toḻil* ^nili oṭṭum oṉṟu alam^ kaṭi"}} {{[col:70]}}eṉa+ toḻil+-peyar allaṉa {{"kālam^ tōṉṟā"}} eṉṟamiyāl,{kacciṉāṉ illattāṉ} eṉpaṉa kālam viḷakkāmiyiṉ, kuṟippāṉ+kālam viḷakkuvaṉa viṉi+-kuṟippu* ātal peṟutum. allatūum, viṉi+-kuṟippu* -um kālam^ tōṉṟutali ilakkaṇam-āka ~uṭiya viṉi+-col= ē ātaliṉ, teṟṟeṉa viḷakkā āyiṉum^kālam uṭiya ~eṉa -~ē paṭum. peyarkku* aṉṉatu* -ōr ilakkaṇamiṉmiyiṉ, kālam^ teṟṟu*-eṉa viḷakkuvaṉa uḷa -~ēl+ koḷvatu*allatu, kālam viḷakkāta peyar -um^ kālam uṭiya ~eṉauyttu*-uṇarum -āṟu illi. ataṉāṉ -um^ kuṟippāṉ+ kālamuṇarttuvaṉa viṉi+-kuṟippu* -ē eṉpatu peṟappaṭum eṉka.

{{Par. 7}} `taṉ= iṉa muṭittal' eṉpataṉāṉ, {aiyāṭṭiyaṉ, tuṇaṅkiyaṉ}eṉa+ ciṟupāṉmi kālam um viṉiceyyiṭam um paṟṟi varuvaṉa um^koḷka. ((16))

TC 214 c

[TRANSLATION]

aṉmiyiṉ iṉmiyiṉ uṇmiyiṉ vaṉmiyiṉ
aṉṉa piṟa um^ kuṟippoṭu koḷḷum
eṉṉa kiḷavi um^ kuṟippu ē kālam.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: {aṉmi iṉmi uṇmi vaṉmi} eṉṉum poruḷ paṟṟi varuvaṉaum, avi pōlvaṉa piṟa um, kuṟippu+ poruṇmiyōṭu poruntumellā+ col= um^ kālam^ kuṟippāṉ uṇarappaṭum; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} kālam^ kuṟippāṉ uṇarappaṭum eṉa ē, ivi um viṉikkuṟippu āmeṉṟa āṟu ām.

{{Par. 3}} e-ṭu: {allaṉ, allaḷ, allar} eṉa -~um; {ilaṉ, ilaḷ, ilar} eṉa-~um; {uḷaṉ, uḷaḷ, uḷar} eṉa -~um; {vallaṉ, vallaḷ, vallar}eṉa -~um varum.

{{Par. 4}} potuppaṭa+ kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, {poruḷ ilaṉ, poruḷ ilaḷ, poruḷilar} eṉa {uṭimikku} maṟutali ākiya {iṉmi} um^koḷḷappaṭum.

{{Par. 5}} ivi oru vāypāṭu* -ē paṟṟi+ piṟattaliṉ, vēṟu kūṟiṉār.

{{Par. 6}} paṇpōṭu ivaṟṟu iṭi vēṟṟumi eṉṉi eṉiṉ:-- {iṉmi}, poruṭkumaṟutali ākaliṉ, poruḷiṉkaṇ+ kiṭakkum paṇpu eṉappaṭātu.{aṉmi} ~um {uṇmi} ~um paṇpiṟku* um ottaliṉ+ paṇpu*eṉappaṭā. eṉṉi? kuṇattiṟku+ kuṇam iṉmiyiṉ. {vaṉmi} --{āṟṟal}; atu um^ kuṇattiṟku um uṇṭākaliṉ+ kuṇam eṉappaṭātu,{ūṟu} eṉiṉ, atu paṇpu āy aṭaṅkum. ataṉāṉ+ poruṭkaṇ+ kiṭantutaṉakku ōr kuṇam iṉṟi+ toḻiliṉ vēṟu āya kuṇattiṉ {aṉmi}mutal āyiṉa vēṟu eṉappaṭum. paṇpu eṉiṉum^ kuṇam eṉiṉumokkum. i+ karuttu ē paṟṟi aṉṟē āciriyar iṉmi -~um{{[E372n]}} uṇmi -~um {{[E420n]}} uṇarttum^ coṟkaḷimuṭippār-āyiṟṟu* eṉpatu.

{{Par. 7}} {{"eṉṉa kiḷavi um"}} eṉṟatu {{"aṉṉa piṟa um"}}eṉappaṭṭavaṟṟi ē āka+ koḷka.

{{Par. 8}} {{"kuṟippu ē kālam"}} eṉṟavaḻi+ kuṟippu eṉṟatukuṟikkappaṭuvataṉi.

{{Par. 9}} {{"aṉṉa piṟa um"}} eṉṟataṉāṉ, {nallaṉ, nallaḷ, nallar;tīyaṉ, tīyaḷ, tīyar; uṭiyaṉ, uṭiyaḷ, uṭiyar} eṉa i+toṭakkattaṉa ellām koḷka. ((17))

TC 215 c

[TRANSLATION]

paṉmi um orumi um pāl aṟi vanta
aṉṉa marapiṉ+ kuṟippoṭu varūum^
kāla+ kiḷavi uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ
mēli+ kiḷaviyoṭu vēṟupāṭu ila ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: paṉmi um orumi um ākiya pāl viḷakki+ kuṟippu+poruṇmi uṭiya āy varum viṉiccol, mēl varum uyartiṇikkaṇ+kūṟiya terinili viṉiyōṭu okkum e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} teri-nili_viṉiyōṭu* ottal -āvatu, uyartiṇi+ teri-nili_viṉikku* ōtiya īṟṟu* -uḷ tamakkuēṟpaṉavaṟṟōṭu viṉikkuṟippu vantavaḻi, avvav+ īṟṟāṉ avva+pāl -um iṭam -um viḷakkal ām.

{{Par. 3}} mēl, viṉikkuṟippu iṉṉa poruḷ paṟṟi varum eṉṟatu allatu iṉṉaīṟṟāṉ iṉṉa pāl viḷakkum eṉṟilar; ataṉāṉ aḵtu īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉār.

{{Par. 4}} kūṟappaṭṭa poruṭkaṇ vantaṉa āyiṉum, {illi, il, iṉṟi} eṉpaṉapāl viḷakkāmiyiṉ, avaṟṟi nīkkutaṟku+ {{"paṉmi um orumium pāl aṟi vanta"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 5}} oru poruṭkaṇ+ pala vāypāṭu* -um oru poruṭkaṇ oru vāypāṭu*-um paṟṟi-varum iru tiṟam -um eñcāmal+ ^taḻuvutaṟku, {{"aṉṉamarapiṉ"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 6}} taṉmi um paṭarkki um uṇarttum^ terinili viṉi īṟṟu uḷ+kuṟippuviṉikku ēṟpaṉa:-- am, ām, em, ēm, eṉ, ēṉ eṉṉum^taṉmi īṟu āṟu um, aṉ, āṉ, aḷ, āḷ, ar, ār eṉṉum paṭarkkiīṟu āṟu um eṉa+ paṉṉiraṇṭu ām.

{{Par. 7}} e-ṭu: {kariyam, kariyām, kariyem, kariyēm, kariyeṉ, kariyēṉ}eṉa -~um; {kariyaṉ, kariyāṉ, kariyaḷ, kariyāḷ, kariyar,kariyār} eṉa -~um avvav= īṟu avvav= iṭam um pāl um viḷakkiyaāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka. oḻinta poruṭkaṇ= um oṭṭikkoḷka.

{{Par. 8}} āṉ, āḷ, ār eṉpaṉa nila+ poruṇmikkaṇ allatu piṟa poruṭkaṇpayiṉṟu vārā.

{{Par. 9}} iṉṉum, {{"mēli+ kiḷaviyoṭu vēṟupāṭu ila"}} eṉṟataṉāṉ,{vantaṉaṉ} eṉa+ terinili viṉi toḻiṉmi mēṟpaṭa+ toḻiluṭi+ poruḷ kīḻppaṭa muṟṟu āy niṉṟu uṇarttiya āṟu pōla{uṭiyaṉ} eṉa+ kuṟippu viṉi um {uṭimi} mēṟpaṭa {uṭiyāṉ}kīḻppaṭa muṟṟu āy niṉṟu uṇarttutal um^ koḷka. {vantāṉ,uṭiyāṉ} eṉa+ peyar āyavaḻi+ toḻil uṭi+ poruḷ um {uṭiyāṉ}um mēṟpaṭṭu+ tōṉṟum āṟu aṟika. iḵtu {{"aḵṟiṇi maruṅkiṉ,mēli+ kiḷaviyoṭu vēṟupāṭu ila ē"}} {{[col:221]}} eṉpataṟkuum okkum. ((18))

((3. aḵṟiṇi viṉi))

((paṉmi viṉimuṟṟu))

TC 216 c

[TRANSLATION]

a ā va eṉa varūum iṟuti
a+ pāl% mūṉṟu ē palavaṟṟu+ paṭarkki.

{{Par. 1}}

{{C CENV}} uyartiṇi viṉi uṇartti, iṉi aḵṟiṇiviṉi uṇarttukiṉṟār.

{{Par. 2}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: akaram um ākāram um vakara uyirmey+ um ākiya īṟṟiuṭiya a+ kūṟṟu mūṉṟu um aḵṟiṇi+ paṉmi+ paṭarkki ām;e-ṟu.

{{Par. 3}}

{{C CENV}} akaram mūṉṟu kālam um paṟṟi varum. ākāram etirmaṟi viṉi āymūṉṟu kālattiṟku um urittu āyiṉum, etirkālattu+ payiṉṟuvarum. akaram, iṟanta kālam paṟṟi varuṅkāl, ka ṭa ta ṟa eṉṉunāṉkaṉ muṉ, aṉ peṟṟu um, peṟātu um varum. ēṉi eḻuttiṉ muṉṅakāra ḻakāram oḻittu iṉ peṟṟu varum. akarattiṉ muṉciṟupāṉmi iṉ= ē aṉṟi aṉ peṟṟu um peṟātu um varum. nikaḻkālattiṉkaṇ nil, kiṉṟu eṉpaṉavaṟṟōṭu aṉ peṟṟu um peṟātu umvarum. etirkālattiṉkaṇ pakara vakarattōṭu aṉ peṟṟu um peṟātuum varum.

{{Par. 4}} e-ṭu: {tokkaṉa tokka, uṇṭaṉa uṇṭa, vantaṉa vanta, ceṉṟaṉaceṉṟa} eṉa -~um; {añciṉa} eṉa -~um; {pōyiṉa pōyaṉa pōya} eṉa-~um; {uṇṇā niṉṟaṉa uṇṇā niṉṟa, uṇkiṉṟaṉa uṇkiṉṟa} eṉa -~um;{uṇpaṉa uṇpa, varuvaṉa varuva} eṉa -~um varum, {uriñuvaṉauriñuva} eṉa ukarattōṭu ēṉi eḻuttu+ pēṟu um ēṟṟavaḻi+koḷka.

{{Par. 5}} {varuva, celva} eṉṉum^ toṭakkattaṉa akara īṟu ātal um,vakara īṟu ātal um uṭiya eṉpatu kiḷaviyākkattu uḷ+ kūṟiṉām.

{{Par. 6}} ākāram kālaveḻuttu+ peṟātu, {uṇṇā, tiṉṉā} eṉa varum.vakaram, {uṇkuva tiṉkuva} eṉa etirkālattiṟku urittu āy+kukaram aṭuttu um, {ōṭuva, pāṭuva} eṉa+ kukaram aṭātu umvarum. {uriñuva, tirumuva} eṉa ukaram peṟutal um ēṟṟavaḻi+koḷka. oḻinta eḻuttōṭu um oṭṭikkoḷka. ((19))

((orumi viṉimuṟṟu))

TC 217 c

[TRANSLATION]

oṉṟaṉ paṭarkki ta ṟa ṭa ūrnta
kuṉṟu iyal ukarattu iṟuti ākum.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: oṉṟaṉi uṇarttum paṭarkki viṉi -~āvatu, ta ṟa ṭaeṉpaṉavaṟṟi ūrntu-niṉṟa kuṟṟu*-iyal_ukaratti ~īṟu* -āka~uṭiya col= ām; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{Par. 8}} {ceymmaṉa} mutal ākiya mūṉṟu vāypāṭṭāṉ um, av= īṟṟa āy+kālam uṇarttum {uṇmaṉa, uṇṇum, uṇṭa} eṉṉum^ toṭakkattaṉaellām^ taḻuvappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟāṉ avi taḻuvappaṭṭa āṟu eṉṉieṉiṉ:-- ellā+ toḻil -um ceytal vēṟupāṭu ākaliṉ, potu ~ākiyaceytal ellā+ toḻili -~um akappaṭuttu-niṟkum; ataṉāṉ avaṟṟāṉavi taḻuvappaṭum eṉka. avi potu um^ ciṟappu um alla ēl,{eṉ ceyyāniṉṟāṉ} eṉṟu viṉāya vaḻi, {uṇṇāniṉṟāṉ} eṉa+cepputal iyiyātu ām eṉka. itu ceytu ceypu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku umokkum.

{{Par. 9}} aḵtēl, cilavaṟṟi īṟṟāṉ uṇartti+ cilavaṟṟi vāypāṭṭāṉuṇarttiyatu eṉṉi eṉiṉ:-- am= īṟu um aṉ= īṟu um ai īṟu ummutal ākiya coṟkaḷ kāla+ paṉmiyāṉ varum vāypāṭṭu+paṉmiyāṉ+ cūttiram perukum eṉṟu añci, avaṟṟi īṟṟāṉuṇartti+ kāla vēṟupāṭu ilēcāṉ+ koḷḷa vittār. kāla+ paṉmiillaṉavaṟṟi vāypāṭṭāṉ um uṇarttupa, īṟṟāṉ um uṇarttupa.

{{Par. 10}} muṟṟu ātal um pala vāypāṭṭāṉ+ payiṉṟu varutal um uṭimiyāṉ,muṉṉili viṉiyi muṉ vittār. ēval+ poruṇmi muṉṉiliviṉikkaṇ= um uṇmiyiṉ+ poruḷ iyipu* uṭittu ākalāṉ um,iṭam^ kuṟittu muṟṟu āy varutal oppumiyāṉ um, ataṉ piṉviyaṅkōḷ vittār. ataṉ piṉ, muṟṟu ātal oppumiyāṉ {{"iṉmiceppal vēṟu eṉ kiḷavi ceymmaṉa"}} eṉpaṉavaṟṟi vittal%muṟimi āyiṉum, muṟṟiṉkaṇ viṉiyeccam uṇmiyāṉ um, īṟṟu+paṉmiyoṭu payiṉṟu varutalāṉ um, viṉiyeccam vittār. iṉmipaṟṟi varum viṉiyeccam um uṇmiyāṉ ataṉōṭu iyiya {{"iṉmiceppal"}} vittār. viṉikkuṟippu ātal oppumiyāṉ um,ceymmaṉaviṉ+ payiṟci uṭimiyāṉ um, ataṉ piṉ {{"vēṟu eṉkiḷavi"}} vittār. muṟṟu ātal oppumiyāṉ, ataṉ piṉ,{{"ceymmaṉa"}} vittār. muṟṟā nilimi -~um uṭittu* ākaliṉ,ataṉ piṉ {{"ceyyum"}} eṉpatu vittār. peyareccam ātaloppumiyāṉ, ataṉ piṉ {{"ceyta"}} eṉpatu vittār. ivvāṟuiyipu paṟṟi vittamiyāṉ, {{"a+ muṟi niṉṟa"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 11}} {{"tiripu vēṟupaṭūum^ ceytiya ākiya"}} eṉa ē, vēṟuvēṟuuṇarttiṉ allatu oru col+ collutaṟkaṇ iru tiṇi um uṇarttāmipeṟutum. ((25))

{{Par. 4}} muṉṉili_viṉi+-kuṟippu, uyartiṇi_viṉi+-kuṟippiṟku* ōtiyaporuḷ paṟṟi aikāram -um āy -um iru -~um īr -um eṉṉum* nāṉku*īṟṟa ~ām. {kaḻaliṉi, nāṭṭi, poṉ= aṉṉi, kariyi} eṉa-~um; {kaḻaliṉāy, nāṭṭāy, poṉ= aṉṉāy, kariyāy} eṉa -~um;{kaḻaliṉir, nāṭṭiṉir, poṉ= aṉṉir, kariyir} eṉa -~um;{kaḻaliṉīr, nāṭṭiṉīr, poṉ= aṉṉīr, kariyīr} eṉa -~um varum.oḻinta poruḷoṭu -~um oṭṭikkoḷka. {pōṟi} eṉa ikara īṟṟuviṉi+-kuṟippu* -um uṇṭu* -āl eṉiṉ:-- {pōṉṟaṉaṉ, pōṉṟāṉ}eṉpaṉa pōla vantu teri-nili_viṉi ~āy-niṉṟatu eṉa maṟukka.

{{Par. 5}} aḵṟiṇi_viṉi+-kuṟippu* -um uyartiṇi_viṉi+-kuṟippiṟku*ōtiya poruḷ paṟṟi-varutaliṉ, avaṟṟi eṭuttu* ōtiṟṟu* eṉṉi~eṉiṉ: -- aṉmi mutal āyiṉa poruḷ paṟṟi-vantaṉa eṉa+ kiḷantu*-ōtalām^ curukkattaṉa vākiyāṉ um, ciṉi-mutal+_kiḷavi paṇpu* -um aṭuttu-varutal uyartiṇiatikārattu+ peṟappaṭāmiyāṉ -um avaṟṟi ōtuvār ēṉi+ poruḷ -um uṭaṉ-ōtiṉār.

{{Par. 6}} muṉṉili viṉikkuṟippu+ pala ātalāṉ -um, eṭuttu*-ōtā_vaḻi+paṭuvatu* -ōr kuṟi-pāṭu* iṉmiyāṉ -um, ivaṟṟi uyttuṇaravittār eṉpatu. allatu, `eṭuttōttu ilvaḻi uyttuṇarvatu' eṉiṉum amiyum. ((27))

{{Par. 7}} muṉṉili viṉi īṟṟāṉ etirkālam paṟṟi-varum ikaratti -~ummiṉṉi -~um mutal -um iṟuti -~um vittu mūṉṟu kālam -umpaṟṟi-varum nāṉku īṟṟi -~um^ tam= uḷ iyiya iṭi vittār. allatu poruṇmi karutātu cūttira-yāppiṟku* ēṟpa vittāreṉiṉum amiyum.

((peyarecca vāypāṭukaḷ))

{{Par. 1}}

{{Par. 3}}

{{C CENV}} ivaṟṟiṟku otta urimiya eṉa ē, oḻinta iraṇṭaṟku um ivaṟṟōṭuoppa uriya ākā, ciṟupāṉmi uriya eṉṟa āṟu ām.

{{Par. 4}} e-ṭu: {vāḻum il, kaṟku nūl, tuyilum^ kālam, vaṉiyum^ kōl,ōtum pārppāṉ, uṇṇum ūṇ} eṉa+ ceyyum eṉṉum peyareccattu um=īṟu kāla eḻuttu+ peṟātu āṟu poruṭku um urittu āy vanta āṟu.{pukka il, uṇṭa cōṟu, vanta nāḷ, veṉṟa vēl, āṭiya kūttaṉ,pōyiṉa pōkku} eṉa+ ceyta eṉṉum peyareccattu iṟuti akaram kaṭa ta ṟa um yakara ṉakaram um ūrntu a+ poruṭku urittu āyvanta āṟu. {nōy tīru maruntu, nōy tīrnta maruntu} eṉṉum ētu+poruṇmi karuvikkaṇ aṭaṅkum; {aracaṉ ā koṭukkum pārppāṉ, ākoṭutta pārppāṉ} eṉa -~um; {āṭi olikkum^ kūli, āṭi olittakūli} eṉa -~um; ēṉi iraṇṭaṟku um^ ciṟupāṉmi uriya āy vantaāṟu.

{{Par. 6}}
«niṉ mukam^ kāṇu maruntiṉēṉ eṉṉum āl»
{{[kali:60_19]}} eṉpuḻi+ {kāṭciyi maruntu} eṉṟāṉ ātaliṉ, «kāṇu maruntu»eṉpatu viṉippeyar koṇṭatu ām.
«poccāvā+ karuviyāṉ+ pōṟṟi+ ceyiṉ»
{{[kuṟaḷ:527]}} eṉpuḻi+ «poccāvā+ karuvi» eṉpatu um atu. {āṟu ceṉṟa viyar}eṉpuḻi {viyar āṟu cēṟalāṉ} vanta kāriyam ākaliṉ,ceyappaṭuporuṭkaṇ aṭaṅkum. {nūṟṟa nūlāṉ iyaṉṟa kaliṅkam} umoṟṟumi nayattāl {nūṟṟa kaliṅkam} eṉappaṭum.
«nūlā+ kaliṅkam vāl ari+ koḷīi»
{{[patiṟṟu:12_21]}} eṉa+ peyareccattiṉ etirmaṟi poruḷ+ peyar koṇṭu niṉṟatu ām.piṟa um^ kūṟappaṭṭa poruṭkaṇ aṭaṅkum āṟu aṟintu aṭakki+koḷka. {uṇṭāṉ cāttaṉ,} {meḻukiṟṟu+ tiṇṇi} eṉpuḻi {uṇṭāṉmeḻukiṟṟu} eṉṉu muṟṟuccol viṉimutal um^ ceyyappaṭuporuḷ umākiya poruṭku uriya ām āṟu pōla. iv= iru vaki+ peyareccamum nila mutal ākiya poruṭku uriya ām eṉa avaṟṟatu aṟuporuṭku um urimi uṇarttiya āṟu. muṭipu eccaviyal uḷ+peṟappaṭum.

{{Par. 7}} poruḷi+ {{"kiḷavi"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 8}} nila mutal āyiṉavaṟṟi+ peyarecca+ poruḷ eṉṉātu muṭikkum^col= eṉiṉ, av= aṟu poruṭku um eṉṉātu av= aṟu peyarkku umeṉṟu ōtuvār. ōta ē, {{"peyar eñcu kiḷavi peyaroṭu muṭim ē"}}{{[col:433]}} eṉṉum^ cūttiram vēṇṭāvām; ataṉāṉ ivi poruḷeṉṟal ē uri.

{{Par. 9}} am ām eṉpaṉa mutal āka akaram īṟu āka+ kiṭanta iṟutiiṭiccoṟku+ kūṟappaṭṭa kāla eḻuttu+ ciṟiya citintu variṉum,ciṟupāṉmi piṟa eḻuttu+ peṟiṉum, nuṇ= uṇarvu uṭiyōr vaḻakkunōkki uṇarntu koḷka. ((37))

{{Par. 3}} {{"avaṟṟoṭu varuvaḻi"}} eṉa+ ceyyum eṉṉum^ colapporuṇmikku* urittu* āy -um urittu* aṉṟi -~um varum irunilimi -~um uṭittu* eṉpatu peṟutum. avaṟṟoṭu vārānilimi muṟṟuccol= ām. aḵtēl, atu muṟṟu+ col= ātaṟkaṇ= um peyar-eñcu_kiḷavi~ātaṟkaṇ= um vēṟṟumi eṉṉi eṉiṉ: -- ēṉi muṟṟiṟku* -um ēṉi ~eccattiṟku* -um vēṟṟumi -~āvatuē īṇṭu um vēṟṟumi ām eṉka. muṟṟu+-colliṟku* -um eccattiṟku*-um vēṟṟumi yātu* eṉiṉ:-- piṟitu ōr collōṭu iyiyātu tām ētoṭar ātaṟku ēṟkum viṉiccol muṟṟu ām, piṟitu ōr col+ paṟṟiallatu niṟṟal āṟṟā viṉiccol eccam ām; itu tam= uḷ vēṟṟumieṉka. aḵtēl, uṇṭāṉ eṉpatu cāttaṉ eṉṉum peyar avāvi aṉṟu* ēniṟpatu; tām ē toṭar ām eṉṟatu eṉṉi eṉiṉ:-- aṟṟu aṉṟu;{uṇṭāṉ cāttaṉ} eṉṟavaḻi, {etti} eṉṉum avāynilikkaṇ{cōṟṟi} eṉpatu vantu iyintāṟpōla, {uṇṭāṉ} eṉṟavaḻi {yār}eṉṉum avāynilikkaṇ {cāttaṉ} eṉpatu vantu iyivatu allatu,a+ peyar paṟṟi allatu niṟṟal āṟṟā nilimittu aṉṟu a+ col=eṉpatu. iv vēṟṟumi viḷaṅka āciriyar muṟṟuccol= eṉṟu* -um,eccam eṉṟu* -um, avaṟṟiṟku+ peyar koṭuttār. ((peyareccam um viṉiyeccam um))

TC 236 c

[TRANSLATION]
peyar eñcu kiḷavi um viṉi eñcu kiḷavi um
etirmaṟuttu moḻiyiṉum poruṇili tiriyā.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: peyareccam um viṉiyeccam um, ceytal+ poruḷa aṉṟi a+ceytal+ poruṇmi etirmaṟuttu+ colliṉum, av= ecca+poruṇmiyiṉ+ ^tiriyā; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} {{"poruḷ*^nili"}} -~āvatu tam= eccam ākiya peyari -~um viṉiyi -~um^ koṇṭu allatuamiyāta nilimi. eṉ colliya āṟu ō eṉiṉ:-- {{"ceyyum ceyta"}} eṉa -~um,{{"ceytu ceyyū ceypu"}} eṉa -~um peyareccam um viṉiyeccamum viti vāypāṭṭāṉ ōtappaṭṭamiyāṉ, āṇṭu+ ceyyā, ceyyātueṉṉum etirmaṟi-vāypāṭu aṭaṅkāmiyiṉ, avi eccam ātalpeṟappaṭṭiṉṟu. ataṉāl, avi um av= ecca+ poruṇmiyiṉ+tiriyātu peyar um viṉi um^ koḷḷum eṉa eytātatu eytuvittaāṟu.

{{Par. 3}} e-ṭu: {uṇṇā illam, uṇṇā+ cōṟu, uṇṇā+ kālam, vaṉiyā+ kōl,ōtā+ pārppāṉ, uṇṇā ūṇ eṉa -~um; uṇṇātu vantāṉ, uṇṇāmikku+pōyiṉāṉ} eṉa -~um varum.

{{Par. 4}} {uṇṇā} eṉpatu {uṇṇum, uṇṭa} eṉṉum iraṇṭaṟku* -um etirmaṟi~ām. {uṇṇāta} eṉpatu* -um atu. {uṇṇātu} eṉpatu {uṇṭu, uṇṇū,uṇkupu} eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku etirmaṟi. {uṇṇāmikku} eṉpatu,{uṇṇiyar, uṇṇiya, uṇaṟku} eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku* -um {uṇṇa} eṉa+`ceyaṟku' eṉpatu paṭa-varum ceya ~eṉ eccattiṟku* -umetirmaṟi -~ām. {uṇṇāmi, uṇṇāmal} eṉpaṉa um^ tām ataṟkuetirmaṟi ām. piṟa -~um etirmaṟi-vāypāṭu uḷa -~ēl+ koḷka.

{{Par. 5}} {uṇṭilaṉ, uṇṇāniṉṟilaṉ, uṇṇalaṉ, uṇṇāṉ} eṉa muṟṟu+ col= umetirmaṟuttu niṟkum ākaliṉ, poruṇili tiriyātu eṉa ataṟkuōtārāyiṟṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ:-- viti viṉikku um etirmaṟi viṉikkuum potu āka īṟu paṟṟi ōti allatu viti+ poruḷa ākiya vāypāṭupaṟṟi ōtāmiyiṉ, āṇṭu+ kaṭṭuri illi eṉka. ((39))

«koṉ muṉi iravu ūr pōla+
cila ākuka nī tuñcu nāḷ ē»

((8.uri iyal))

((uriccol ilakkaṇam uṇarttuvatu))

((1. potu ilakkaṇam))

((uriccolliṉ iyalpu))

TC 297 c

[TRANSLATION]

uriccol+ kiḷavi virikkum^ kāli
iciyiṉ um^ kuṟippiṉ um paṇpiṉ um^ tōṉṟi+
peyariṉ um viṉiyiṉ um* meytaṭumāṟi
oru col pala poruṭku urimi tōṉṟiṉum
pala col oru poruṭku urimi tōṉṟiṉum
payilātavaṟṟi+ payiṉṟavi cārtti+
tatta marapiṉ+ ceṉṟu nili maruṅkiṉ
e+ col= āyiṉum poruḷ vēṟu kiḷattal.

{{Par. 1}}

{{C CENV}} niṟutta muṟiyāṉ ē uriccol= uṇarttiya eṭuttu+ koṇṭār; ataṉāṉiv= ōttu uriyiyal eṉṉum peyarttu āyiṟṟu. {{"tamakku iyalpuillā"}} iṭiccol+ pōlātu ici kuṟippu+ paṇpu eṉṉum poruṭku+tām ē uriya ākaliṉ, uriccol= āyiṟṟu. perumpāṉmi um^ceyyuṭku uriya āy varutaliṉ uriccol= āyiṟṟu eṉpār um uḷar.

{{Par. 2}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: uriccolli virittu* urikkum-iṭattu, ici, kuṟippu+paṇpu eṉṉum poruḷ% mēl+ tōṉṟi, peyarkkaṇ= um viṉikkaṇ= umtam= urupu taṭumāṟi, oru col+ pala poruṭku urittu* āyvariṉum pala col oru poruṭku urittu* āy variṉum kēṭpāṉāṉ+payilappaṭāta colli+ payiṉṟavaṟṟōṭu cārtti+ peyar um viṉium ākiya tattamakku uriya nilikkaḷattiṉkaṇ yāt-āṉum orucol= āyiṉum vēṟu vēṟu poruḷ uṇarttappaṭum eṉṟa āṟu.

{{C CENV}} eṉṟatu ici kuṟippu+ paṇpu eṉṉum poruḷa āy+ peyar viṉipōṉṟu um avaṟṟiṟku mutaṉili āy um^ taṭumāṟi oru col oruporuṭku urittu ātal ē aṉṟi, oru col+ pala poruṭku um palacol oru poruṭku um uriya āy varuvaṉa uriccol= eṉṟu* -um,avi peyar um viṉi um pōla īṟu paṟṟi+ poruḷ uṇarttalākāmiyiṉ, veḷippaṭātavaṟṟi veḷippaṭṭavaṟṟōṭu cārtti+tammi eṭuttu*-ōti -~ē a+ poruḷ uṇarttappaṭum eṉṟu* -um,uriccoṟku+ potu-~ilakkaṇam -um avaṟṟiṟku+ poruḷ uṇarttum*muṟimi ~um uṇarttiya -~āṟu.

{{Par. 3}} kuṟippu -- maṉattāṉ+ kuṟittu uṇarappaṭuvatu. paṇpu --poṟiyāṉ uṇarappaṭum^ kuṇam.

 [TRANSLATION]

{{Par. 2}} kuṟippu+ poruṇmi pala vakittu ākalāṉ um, peyariṉ umviṉiyiṉ um meytaṭumāṟi um^ taṭumāṟātu um oru col+ palaporuṭku urittu āy um varutalāṉ um, īṟu paṟṟi+ tokuttuuṇarttaṟku aṉṉa īṟu uṭiya aṉmiyāṉ um, {{"pal% muṟiyāṉ umparantaṉa varūum"}} eṉṟār.

«viṉi+ kuṟi tīrntāriṉ+ tīrntaṉṟu ulaku»

«paya+ kuṟi illi+ tām vāḻu nāḷ ē»

((9. eccaviyal))

((1. coṟkaḷiṉ vaki))

((nālvaki+ coṟkaḷ))

TC 397 c

[TRANSLATION]

iyaṟcol tiricol ticiccol vaṭacol= eṉṟu
aṉittu* -ē ceyyuḷ īṭṭa+ col= ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{C CENV}} kiḷaviyākkam* mutal -āka uriyiyal iṟuti -~āka+ kiṭantaōttukkaḷ -uḷ uṇarttutaṟku* iṭam iṉmiyāṉ uṇarttappaṭātueñci-niṉṟa col= ilakkaṇam ellām^ tokuttu uṇarttiyaeṭuttukkoṇṭār. ataṉāṉ iv= ōttu eccaviyal eṉṉum peyarttu*āyiṟṟu.

{{Par. 2}} {{"kaṇṭīr eṉṟā"}} {{[col:425]}} eṉa -~um, {{"ceyyāy eṉṉumuṉṉili viṉiccol"}} {{[col:450]}} eṉa -~um, {{"uriccol%maruṅkiṉ um"}} {{[col:456]}} eṉa -~um, {{"orumi cuṭṭiyapeyar nili+ kiḷavi"}} {{[col:461]}} eṉa -~um, ivi mutalākiya cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ uṇarttappaṭṭa acinili um viṉiccol=ilakkaṇam um vaḻuvamiti um av= ōttukkaḷ uḷ uṇarttātu īṇṭuuṇarttiyatu eṉṉi ō eṉiṉ:-- ataṟku+ kāraṇam avva+ cūttiramurikkumvaḻi+ collutum.

{{Par. 3}} pala poruḷ+ tokutikku* oṉṟaṉāṉ+ peyar koṭukkum^-kāl{{"talimi um paṉmi um"}} paṟṟi+ koṭuppiṉ allatu piṟituāṟu iṉmiyāṉ um, talimi um paṉmi um eccattiṟku iṉmiyāṉum, pattu vaki eccam īṇṭu uṇarttalāṉ eccaviyal āyiṟṟu eṉṟalporuntāmi uṇarka.

{{Par. 4}} ceyyuṭku* uriya col= um, avaṟṟatu* ilakkaṇam -um, avaṟṟāṉ+ceyyuḷ-ceyvuḻi+ paṭum vikāram -um, ceyyuḷ+ poruḷkōḷ -um,eṭuttu+-kōṭaṟkaṇ uṇarttukiṉṟār.

{{Par. 5}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: iyaṟcol= -um, tiri-col= -um, tici+-col= -um vaṭa-col=-um eṉa attuṇi -~ē ceyyuḷ īṭṭutaṟku* uriya col= -āvaṉa;e-ṟu.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: cuṇṇam -āvatu iyalpu* -āka aminta īr aṭikkaṇ uḷavākiya eṇ cīri+ tuṇittuiyiyum -vaḻi aṟintu kūṭṭi iyaṟṟappaṭuvatu ām; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} aḷav-aṭi allātaṉa vikāra-v-aṭi ākaliṉ+ {{"paṭṭāṅku amintu*"}}ila, ātaliṉ+ {{"paṭṭāṅku aminta īr aṭi"}} eṉa ē, aḷav-aṭi ātalpeṟappaṭum. {{"īr aṭi eṇ cīr"}} vikāra aṭiyāṉ -umpeṟappaṭutaliṉ, avaṟṟi nīkkutaṟku+ {{"paṭṭāṅku aminta īraṭi"}} eṉṟār eṉa ē, cuṇṇam aḷav-aṭi iraṇṭaṉ uḷ allatupiṟāṇṭu vārātu eṉpatu.

{{Par. 3}} e-ṭu:
«curi āḻa ammi mitappa vari aṉiya
yāṉikku nīttu muyaṟku nili eṉpa
kāṉaka nāṭaṉ cuṉi.»
{{[ANON]}} eṉpuḻi, {āḻa, mitappa, nīttu, nili} eṉpaṉa -~um, {curi,ammi, yāṉikku, muyaṟku} eṉpaṉa -~um, niṉṟuḻi niṟpaiyiyāmiyiṉ, `curi mitappa, ammi āḻa, yāṉikku nili,muyaṟku nīttu' eṉa+ tuṇittu+ kūṭṭa, iyinta āṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.

{{Par. 4}} cuṇṇam pōla+ citar-āy+ parantu kiṭattaliṉ+ {{"cuṇṇam"}}eṉṟār. ((10))

((aṭimaṟi+ poruḷkōḷ))

TC 407 c

[TRANSLATION]

aṭimaṟi+ ceyti aṭi nili tirintu
cīr nili tiriyātu taṭumāṟum= ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: aṭimaṟi+ ceyyuḷ -āvatu, cīr niṉṟāṅku niṟpa aṭikaḷ tattam* niliyiṉ+ tirintu oṉṟaṉ*^nilikkaḷattu oṉṟu ceṉṟu-niṟkum; e-ṟu.

{{C CENV}} eṉa ē, ellā aṭi um yāṇṭum^ cellum eṉpatu ām.

{{Par. 2}} e-ṭu:
«māṟā+ kātalar mali maṟantaṉar -ē
āṟā+ kaṭpaṉi varal āṉā -~ē
ēṟā mel% ^tōḷ vaḷi nekiḻum= ē
kūṟāy tōḻi yāṉ vāḻum -āṟu* -ē»
{{[ANON]}} eṉa varum. itaṉ -uḷ+ cīr niṉṟāṅku niṟpa+ poruḷ-citiyāmalellā ~aṭi -~um^ taṭumāṟiya -~āṟu kaṇṭu-koḷka.

{{Par. 3}} perumpāṉmi -~um nāl-aṭi+_ceyyuṭkaṇ allatu i+ poruḷkōḷvārātu eṉka.

{{Par. 4}} {{"niraṉiṟi tāṉ ē"}} {{"cuṇṇam^ tāṉ ē"}} {{"moḻimāṟṟuiyaṟki"}} eṉpaṉa pōla īṇṭu um {{"aṭimaṟi+ ceyti"}}eṉpataṉi+ kuṟaḷaṭi ākki, `aṭi nili tirintu cīr nilitiriyātu taṭumāṟum= ē poruḷ* ^teri maruṅkiṉ' eṉṟa cūttiram-āka aṟuppār -um uḷar. ((11))

((ataṟku, mēlum oru muṭipu))

TC 408 c

[TRANSLATION]

poruḷ teri maruṅkiṉ

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: poruḷ ārāyuṅkāl, aṭi-maṟi+_ceyyuṭkaṇ īṟṟu*-aṭiyatuiṟuti+-cīr eruttu*-aṭiyiṉ+ ceṉṟu tirital -um variyār; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} {{"cīr nili-tiriyātu taṭumāṟum"}} {{[407]}} eṉṟār ākaliṉ cīrnili-tirital -um oruvaḻi+ kaṇṭu eytiyatu ikantupaṭāmal+kātta āṟu.

{{Par. 3}} ilakkiyam vantavaḻi+ kaṇṭukoḷka.

{{Par. 4}} {{"eruttu vayiṉ"}} eṉpataṟku īṟṟayal+ cīr vayiṉ eṉṟu poruḷurittu,
«cūral pampiya ciṟukāṉ yāṟu ē
cūr aramakaḷir ār aṇaṅkiṉar ē
cāral* ^nāṭa nī varutī ē
vāral eṉiṉ ē yāṉ añcuval ē»
{{[ANON]}} eṉpuḻi {añcuval yāṉ} eṉa iṟuti+ cīr īṟṟayal+ cīr vayiṉ+ceṉṟu tirintatu eṉṟu utāraṇam^ kāṭṭiṉār -āl uriyāciriyareṉiṉ:-- «yāṉ añcuval» eṉa niṉṟāṅku niṟpa -~um poruḷ cellumākaliṉ ivvāṟu tirital poruntāmiyiṉ, avarkku atu karuttuaṉṟu eṉka.

{{Par. 5}} ellā aṭi -~um yāṇṭum^ cellum āyiṉum, urippōr kuṟippāṉeruttu eṉṟu* -um īṟṟu aṭi eṉṟu* -um^ kūṟiṉār.
`urippōr kuṟippiṉ uṇar vaki aṉṟi
iṭippāl% mutal īṟu eṉṟivi tam= uḷ%
matikka+ paṭātaṉa maṇṭala yāppu ē' eṉa urippōr kuṟippāṉ mutal -um iṭi -~um īṟu* -um^ kōṭalpiṟar um^ kūṟiṉār eṉpatu. ((12))

((3. tokiccol vaki tokiyāvaṉa))

TC 410 c

 [TRANSLATION]

ta na nu e eṉum avi mutal ākiya
kiḷi nutal peyar um pirippa+ piriyā.

{{Par. 1}}

{{C CENV}} ceyyuṭku* uriya col= -um, col+ ^toṭukkum^-kāl+ paṭum vikāram-um avi ceyyuḷ ākkum^-kāl+ tammuḷ+ puṇarntu-niṟkum āṟu* -umākiya ceyyuḷ-oḻipu uṇartti, iṉi vaḻakku*-ilakkaṇattu*_oḻipukūṟukiṉṟār.

{{Par. 2}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: ta na nu e eṉpaṉavaṟṟi mutal āka uṭiya āy+ kiḷiminutali varum peyar um pirikkappaṭā; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 3}}

{{C CENV}} avi āvaṉa {tamaṉ, tamaḷ, tamar; namaṉ, namaḷ, namar; numaṉ,numaḷ, numar; emaṉ, emaḷ, emar; tammāṉ, tammāḷ, tammār;nammāṉ, nammāḷ, nammār; nummāṉ, nummāḷ, nummār; emmāṉ,emmāḷ, emmār} eṉa varum.

{{Par. 4}} ummiyāṉ+ piṟa {{"kiḷi nutal+ peyar"}} um pirikka+ piriyāeṉpatu ām: avi {tāy, ñāy, tanti, taṉṉi} eṉṉum^toṭakkattaṉa.

{{Par. 5}} ivaṟṟi+ {{"pirippa+ piriyā"}} eṉṟatu eṉṉi eṉiṉ:-- {veṟpaṉ,poruppaṉ} eṉṉum^ toṭakkattu oṭṭuppeyar {veṟpu + aṉ, poruppu+ aṉ} eṉa+ piritta-vaḻi -~um, {veṟpu, poruppu} eṉṉummutaṉili tam poruḷ iṉitu viḷakkum. {tamaṉ emaṉ}eṉpaṉavaṟṟi+ {tam + aṉ, em + aṉ}, eṉa+ pirikkal uṟiṉ {tam,em} eṉpaṉa mutaṉili āy+ poruḷ uṇarttuvaṉa ātal vēṇṭum; aviporuḷ uṇarttāmiyāṉ, {tamaṉ, emaṉ} eṉa vaḻaṅkiyāṅku+ koḷvatuallatu pirikka+ paṭāmiyiṉ, avvāṟu kūṟiṉār eṉpatu. piṟa umaṉṉa. aḵtēl, {tām yām} eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku mutaṉili āka+ pirikkaē, avi um^ tam poruḷ uṇarttum eṉiṉ:-- {tamaṉ emaṉ} eṉpaṉa{taṉ kiḷi eṉ kiḷi} eṉa -~um, {tam^ kiḷi em^ kiḷi} eṉa-~um mutaṉili vakiyāṉ orumikku* -um paṉmikku* -umpotu-~ātal uṭiya, orumi uṇarttuṅkāl {tām yām} eṉpaṉaporuntāmiyiṉ {tāṉ yāṉ} eṉpaṉa -~ē mutaṉili eṉal vēṇṭum.vēṇṭa -~ē, ivvāṟu pirippiṉ {tamaṉ emaṉ} eṉa ōr-oṉṟu iraṇṭucol= ātal vēṇṭutalāṉ, eḻuttu* -āṉum poruḷ -āṉum vēṟupāṭuiṉmiyiṉ oru col= eṉa -~ē paṭum; iraṇṭu col= eṉṟalnirampāmiyiṉ, avvāṟu pirittal um poruttam iṉṟu eṉpatu.

{{Par. 6}} {{"kiḷi nutal+ peyar"}} viḷi marapiṉkaṇ+ peṟappaṭṭamiyāṉ+peyariyal uḷ uṇarttār āyiṉār. ataṉāl āṇṭuiyipu-paṭṭu*_iṉṟu* ākalāl, {{"pirippa+ piriyā"}} orucol=aṭukkōṭu iyiya itaṉi īṇṭu vittār. ((14))

((tokipōl aṭukki varuvaṉa))

TC 411 c

[TRANSLATION]

avi mūṉṟu eṉpa oru col= aṭukku ē.

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: iciniṟi um, acinili um, poruḷ vēṟupāṭṭōṭu puṇarvatuum eṉa oru col= aṭukku a+ mūṉṟu vakippaṭum; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu:
«ē ē ē ē ampaṉ moḻintaṉaḷ» eṉṟatu iciniṟi. {maṟṟō maṟṟō}; {aṉṟē aṉṟē} eṉpaṉaacinili. {pāmpu pāmpu; avaṉ avaṉ; vitēṉ vitēṉ; uṇṭuuṇṭu; pōm pōm} eṉpaṉa, muṟiyāṉ ē virivu um^ tuṇivu umuṭampāṭu um oru toḻil palakāl* ^nikaḻtal um ākiya poruḷvēṟupāṭu uṇarttaliṉ+ {{"poruḷoṭu puṇartal"}}. poruḷ vēṟupāṭupiṟa um uḷa ēl vaḻakku nōkki+ kaṇṭukoḷka.

{{Par. 3}} aṭukku oru collatu vikāram eṉappaṭum; iraṇṭu col= āyiṉ iruporuḷ uṇarttuvatu allatu i+ poruḷ vēṟupāṭu uṇarttāmiyiṉeṉpatu. ((15))

((toki moḻikaḷ))

TC 412 c

[TRANSLATION]

vēṟṟumi+ toki ē uvama+ toki ē

{{Par. 2}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: vēṟṟumi+ toki mutal āka+ tokiccol āṟu ām; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 3}}

{{C CENV}} vēṟṟumi-~urupu* -um uvama-~urupu* -um ummi -~umviṉi+-col=_īṟu* -um paṇpu+-col=_īṟu* -um^ tokutaliṉ+ tokiāyiṉa eṉpār -um, avva+ poruḷ% mēl iraṇṭu* -um pala -~umākiya coṟkaḷ piḷavu-paṭātu oṟṟumi+-paṭa+ tam= uḷ iyitaliṉ+toki āyiṉa eṉpār -um eṉa iru tiṟattar āciriyar. {ceytāṉporuḷ, iruntāṉ māṭattu} eṉa urupu tokku oru col% nīrmi+paṭātaṉa -~um^ toki āvāṉ cēṟaliṉ avaṟṟi nīkkutaṟku* -um,
«vēḻa+-karumpu»
{{[periya_purāṇam:ēṉāti_nātar:2]}}
«kēḻaṟ-paṉṟi»
{{[puṟam:152_4]}} eṉpuḻi+ tokkaṉa-~illi eṉiṉum toki eṉa vēṇṭappaṭum ākalāṉavaṟṟi+ taḻuvutaṟku* -um, urupu mutal āyiṉa tokutaliṉ+toki eṉpārkku* -um oṭṭi oru-col%_nīrmi+ paṭutal -um^toki-~ilakkaṇam eṉal vēṇṭum; ataṉāṉ urupu mutal āyiṉatokutal ellā+ tokiyiṉ -um^ cellāmiyāṉ, ellā+ tokikkaṇ=-um^ cellum -āṟu oṭṭi oru-col=_ātal toki-~ilakkaṇam āymuṭitaliṉ, iv= āciriyarkku itu -~ē tuṇivu eṉa+-paṭum eṉpatu.aṟṟu āyiṉ, {{"urupu toka varutal um"}} {{[col:104]}} eṉa-~um, {{"vēṟṟumi tokka peyar vayiṉ āṉum"}} {{[col:418]}}eṉa -~um, {{"ummi tokka peyar vayiṉ āṉum"}} {{[col:418]}}eṉa -~um, {{"uvami tokka peyar vayiṉ āṉum"}} {{[XXX]}}eṉa -~um ōtalāl avi āṇṭu+ tokkaṉa eṉappaṭum aṉṟō eṉiṉ:--aṟṟu aṉṟu; {{"atu ~eṉ vēṟṟumi uyartiṇi+ toki vayiṉ"}}{{[col:94]}} eṉpuḻi atu ~eṉ urupu niṉṟu keṭṭatu āyiṉ niṉṟakālattu+ tiṇi-vaḻu ~ām; a+ tiṇi-vaḻu amivu*-uṭittu* eṉiṉvirikkiṉṟuḻi nāṉkām urupu toṭarātu atu taṉṉi -~ē virippiṉumamivu*-uṭittu; ataṉāṉ muṟi+ poruḷ* tōṉṟa {nampi-makaṉ}eṉa iraṇṭu col+ tokkaṉa eṉpatu* -ē āciriyar karuttu eṉalvēṇṭum. allatūum, viṉi+-toki paṇpu+-toki aṉmoḻi+-tokieṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ viṉi -~um paṇpu* -um aṉmoḻi -~um^ tokku-nillāmiyāṉ -um,aḵtē karuttu* ātal aṟika. ataṉāṉ {{"urupu*"}} -um {{"uvami"}} -~um {{"ummi"}} -~um^ tokutal -āvatu tam poruḷ oṭṭiya collāṉ+ tōṉṟa+ tām āṇṭu+ pulappaṭātu* -ē -niṟṟal -ē ~ām.

{{Par. 4}} vēṟṟumi+-toki eṉpatu vēṟṟumi+-poruḷi uṭiya toki eṉṟu*āṉum vēṟṟumi+-poruḷ* ^tokka toki ~eṉṟu* āṉum viriyum.uvama+-toki ummi+-toki aṉmoḻi+-toki eṉpaṉa -~um avvāṟuviriyum. aṉmoḻi -~āvatu tokka col= allāta moḻi. viṉi+-toki paṇpu+-toki eṉpaṉa, viṉiyiṉatu tokipaṇpiṉatu toki eṉa viriyum. viṉi paṇpu eṉṟatu avaṟṟi uṇarttum^ colli. oru collāṉ+toki iṉmiyiṉ+ piṟitu* -ōr colloṭu tokutal peṟappaṭum.

{{Par. 5}} i+ cūttirattāṉ+ tokiccol iṉittu eṉa variyaṟutta āṟu.((16))

((vēṟṟumi+ toki))

((uvama+ toki))

TC 414 c

[TRANSLATION]

uvama+ toki ē uvama iyala.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: uvama+-toki uvama-~urupu_toṭar+ poruḷ pōla+ poruḷuṇarttum eṉṟa āṟu. eṉa ē, {puli aṉṉa cāttaṉ, mayil aṉṉamātar} eṉṉum poruṭkaṇ+ {puli+ cāttaṉ; mayil% mātar} eṉa a+poruḷ viḷakkum āṟṟal illaṉa tokā; āṟṟal uṭiyaṉa ē tokuvaṉaeṉpatu ām.

{{Par. 2}} e-ṭu: {puli+ pāyttuḷ; maḻi vaṇ ki; tuṭi naṭuvu; poṉ mēṉi}eṉpaṉa {puli+ pāyttuḷ aṉṉa pāyttuḷ; maḻi aṉṉa vaṇki; tuṭiaṉṉa naṭuvu; poṉ= aṉṉa mēṉi} eṉa+ tam viri+ poruḷ uṇarttiyaāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka. aḵtēl, {puli+ pāyttuḷi okkum pāyttuḷ;maḻiyi okkum vaṇ ki} eṉa viritaliṉ avi ellām vēṟṟumi+toki eṉappaṭum; ataṉāṉ uvama+-toki eṉa oṉṟu* illi eṉiṉ:--aṟṟu* aṉṟu, colluvārkku atu karuttu* āyiṉ vēṟṟumi+-toki-~um ām. a+ karuttāṉ aṉṟi+ {puli aṉṉa pāyttuḷ, poṉ māṉu mēṉi} eṉa vēṟṟumiyōṭu* iyipu*-illā uvama-~urupu_toṭar+ poruṭkaṇ+tokkavaḻi uvama+-toki āvatu* -allatu vēṟṟumi+-toki āṇṭu*iṉmiyiṉ vēṟṟumi+-toki ~ām -āṟu* illi eṉka. uvama-~urupu{{"oppu il vaḻiyāṉ+ poruḷ ceyyum"}} {{[col:250]}} iṭi+-col=ākalāṉ, viṉi -~um viṉi+-kuṟippu* -um paṟṟi-varum. avipaṟṟi
«eṉ pōl+ peru vituppu* uṟuka niṉṉi,
iṉṉātu uṟṟa aṟaṉ il kūṟṟu* -ē»
{{[puṟam:255_3/4]}} eṉpuḻi+ pōla eṉpatu kuṟippu_viṉi-~eccam āy-niṟṟalāṉ um,
«nummaṉōr um* maṟṟu iṉiyar āyiṉ
emmaṉōr ivaḷ+ piṟa alar mātō»
{{[puṟam:210_3/4]}} eṉpuḻi {aṉṉōr} eṉpatu iṭiccol% mutaṉili āka+ piṟantakuṟippu+ peyar ākalāṉ um, {eṉṉi+ pōla} {nummi aṉṉōr, emmiaṉṉōr} eṉa iraṇṭāvatu virittaṟku ēṟpu uṭimi aṟika. ((18))

((viṉittoki))

TC 415 c

[TRANSLATION]

viṉiyiṉ tokuti kālattu iyalum.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: viṉi+ toki kālattiṉkaṇ* ^nikaḻum; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} {{"kālattu iyalum"}} eṉa+ potu vakiyāṉ+ kūṟiyavataṉāṉ mūṉṟukālam um^ koḷka.

{{Par. 3}} {{"tokuti kālattu iyalum"}} eṉa ē, av viṉi pirintuniṉṟavaḻi+ tōṉṟātu tokkavaḻi+ toki āṟṟalāṉ+ kālam^ tōṉṟumeṉṟa āṟu ām.

{{Par. 4}} īṇṭu viṉi eṉṟatu evaṟṟi eṉiṉ:-- viṉiccoṟku umviṉippeyarkku um mutaṉili āy, {uṇ, tiṉ, cel, kol} eṉa,viṉi māttiram uṇartti niṟpaṉavaṟṟi eṉpatu. ivaṟṟivaṭanūlār tātu eṉpa.

{{Par. 5}} e-ṭu: {āṭu araṅku; cey kuṉṟu; puṇar poḻutu; ari vāḷ; kolyāṉi; cel celavu} eṉa varum. kālam uṇarttātu viṉi māttiramuṇarttum peyar, nili+ peyar mutal ākiya peyaroṭu tokkuḻi+kālam uṇarttiya āṟu kaṇṭu koḷka. kālam uṇarttukiṉṟuḻi+peyarecca+ poruḷa āy niṉṟu uṇarttum eṉpatu {{"ceyyum^ ceytaeṉṉum^ kiḷaviyiṉ -- mey= oruṅku iyalum^ toḻil* ^toku moḻium"}} {{[eḻuttu-482i]}} eṉpataṉāṉ+ kūṟiṉār. toki+ poruḷākiya tām pirintavaḻi+ peṟappaṭāmiyiṉ āciriyar ivaṟṟi+{{"puṇar iyal* ^nili iṭi uṇara+ tōṉṟā"}} eṉṟār. ataṉāṉivi tam^ collāṉ virikkappaṭāmiyiṉ+ pirivu il oṭṭu ām.

{{Par. 6}} peyar eccam niṉṟu tokkatu eṉṟār āl uriyāciriyar eṉiṉ:--aṟṟu aṉṟu; āciriyar ivaṟṟi+ pirittu+ puṇarkkappaṭā;vaḻaṅkiya āṟu ē koḷḷappaṭum eṉṟatu, pirittavaḻi+ toki+poruḷ cititalāṉ aṉṟē; {koṉṟa yāṉi} eṉa virinta vaḻi um a+poruḷ citivu iṉṟu ēl {{"puṇar iyal* ^nili iṭi uṇara+tōṉṟā"}} eṉṟaṟku ōr kāraṇam illi ām. ataṉāṉ+ peyareccamniṉṟu tokutal āciriyar karuttu aṉmiyiṉ, uriyāciriyarkku umatu karuttu aṉṟu eṉka. allatūum, ākupeyar uṇarttiya vaḻiviṉittoki uḷappaṭa {{"iru peyar oṭṭu um"}} {{[col:114]}}eṉṟār ākalāṉ um viṉi niṉṟu tokutal avarkku+ karuttu aṉmiaṟika.

{{Par. 7}} aḵtēl, viṉittokikku mutaṉili peyar ām aṉṟō eṉiṉ:-- {uriñ}eṉpatu mutal āyiṉavaṟṟi+ toḻiṟpeyar eṉṟār ākaliṉ, toḻiṉmāttiram uṇarttuvaṉa ellām toḻiṟpeyar eṉpatu āciriyarkaruttu eṉpa. ((19))

((paṇputtoki))

TC 416 c

[TRANSLATION]

vaṇṇattiṉ vaṭiviṉ aḷaviṉ cuviyiṉ eṉṟu
aṉṉa piṟa um ataṉ kuṇam nutali
iṉṉatu itu eṉa varūum iyaṟki
eṉṉa kiḷavi um paṇpiṉ toki ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: vaṇṇam, vaṭivu, aḷavu, cuvi eṉpaṉa um avi pōlvaṉapiṟa um ākiya kuṇatti nutali+ piṉ tokka vaḻi+ kuṇa+ col+kuṇam uṭiyataṉi uṇarttalāṉ iṉṉatu itu eṉa oṉṟaṉi oṉṟuvicēṭittu iru col= um oru poruḷiṉ mēl varum iyalpi uṭiyaellā+ toki+ col= um paṇpu+ toki ām; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} {{"nutali"}} eṉṉum^ ciṉi viṉiyeccam {{"varum"}} eṉṉum*mutal viṉiyōṭu muṭintatu.

{{Par. 3}} {{"iyaṟki"}} eṉṟatu, tokkuḻi+ paṇpu uṭiyataṉi+ kuṟittala+ toki+ collatu iyalpu eṉpatu allatu kāraṇam^ kūṟappaṭātueṉṟa āṟu. tokikkaṇ allatu a+ col taṉi nili āy, {uṇ, tiṉ,cel, kol} eṉpaṉa pōla+ poruḷ uṇarttāmiyiṉ, paṇputtoki umviṉittoki pōla+ pirikkappaṭātu ām.

{{Par. 4}} e-ṭu: {karum^ kutiri} eṉpatu vaṇṇa+ paṇpu. {vaṭṭa+ palaki}eṉpatu vaṭivu. {neṭum^ kōl} eṉpatu aḷavu. {tīm^ karumpu}eṉpatu cuvi. aṉṉa piṟa um eṉṟataṉāṉ, {nuṇ nūl, par āri,mel= ili, nal= āṭi} eṉṉum^ toṭakkattaṉa koḷka. avi,{karitu ākiya kutiri, vaṭṭam ākiya palaki} eṉa+ paṇpu+col= um paṇpu uṭi+ poruḷ ē kuṟittalāṉ, iru col= um oruporuḷa āy iṉṉatu itu eṉa oṉṟi oṉṟu potumi nīkkiya āṟukaṇṭukoḷka.

{{Par. 5}} aḵtēl, {karitu ākiya kutiri, vaṭṭam ākiya palaki} eṉpaṉaa+ tokiyiṉ viri ākaliṉ+ paṇputtoki pirikkappaṭātu eṉṟatueṉṉi eṉiṉ:-- aṟṟu aṉṟu; toki+ poruḷ uṇarttutaṟku+ piṟacol+ koṇarntu virittu allatu, taṉ collāṉ viriyāmiyiṉ aviviri eṉappaṭā eṉka. vaṭanūlār um piriyā+ toki um piṟacollāṉ virikkappaṭum eṉṟār. {kariyatu} eṉṉum {{"paṇpu koḷpeyar"}} {karum^ kutiri} eṉa+ tokkatu eṉṟār āluriyāciriyar eṉiṉ: ataṉi+ peyareccam viṉittoki nilimoḻieṉṟataṟku urittāṅku urittu maṟukka. piṟa col+ koṇarntuvirikkuṅkāl, {kariya kutiri, kariyatu kutiri} eṉa a+toki+ poruḷ uṇarttuvaṉa ellāvaṟṟāṉ um virikkappaṭum.

{{Par. 6}} mutaṉili -āvatu, {kariyaṉ, ceyyaṉ, karumi, cemmi} eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku* ellāmmutaṉili ~āy+ col= āy nirampātu {karu cev=} eṉa+ paṇpu māttiram uṇartti-niṟpatu* ām.

{{Par. 7}} {{"eṉṉa kiḷavi um"}} eṉṟataṉāl,
«cāri+ pāmpu»,
«vēḻa+ karumpu»,
{{[periya_purāṇam:ēṉāti_nātar:2]}}
«kēḻaṟ paṉṟi»
{{[puṟam:152_4]}} eṉa+ paṇpu tokātu peyar tokkaṉa um a+ toki ātal koḷka.ivaṟṟatu cāri mutal ākiya nilimoḻi piritta vaḻi um poruḷuṇarttaliṉ, ivaṟṟi+ pirittu+ puṇarttār. aḵtēl, {pāmpi+cāri} vicēṭittu allatu {cāriyi+ pāmpu} vicēṭittiṉṟuākaliṉ oṉṟi oṉṟu potumi nīkkāmiyāṉ+ {cāri+ pāmpu} eṉpatumutal āyiṉa paṇputtoki āyiṉa āṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ:-- naṉṟucoṉṉāy: vicēṭippatu um vicēṭikkappaṭuvatu um ākiya iraṇṭaṉuḷ vicēṭippatu vicēṭiyākkāl atu kuṟṟam ām;vicēṭikkappaṭuvatu vicēṭittiṉṟu eṉṟal um vicēṭikkappaṭutalākiya taṉ taṉmikku iḻukku iṉmiyāṉ, vicēṭiyātu niṟpiṉumamiyum eṉka. iv vēṟupāṭu peṟutaṟku aṉṟē {{"iṉṉatu itu eṉavarūum"}} eṉa+ piṉmoḻiyi vicēṭippatu āka um, muṉmoḻiyivicēṭikkappaṭuvatu āka um, āciriyar ōtuvār āyiṟṟu eṉpatu. aṟṟu ēṉum, {cāri} eṉa ē kuṟitta poruḷ viḷaṅkaliṉ+ {pāmpu}eṉpatu miki ām piṟa eṉiṉ: -- aṟṟu aṉṟu: ulaka-vaḻakku* -āvatu cūttira-yāppu+ pōla miki+-col+ paṭāmi+ colla+-paṭuvatu* oṉṟu* aṉṟi, mēṟṟoṭṭu+ kēṭṭārkku+ poruḷ iṉitu viḷaṅka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuvaruvatu ākaliṉ, atu kaṭā aṉṟu eṉka. mikiccol+ paṭāmi collappaṭum āyiṉ, {yāṉ vantēṉ, nī vantāy}eṉṉātu {vantēṉ, vantāy} eṉṟu ē vaḻaṅkal vēṇṭum eṉpatu. iṉi oṟṟumi nayattāl {eṉpu um^ tōl um uriya ātal ākiyauṟuppu} um^ {cāri} eṉappaṭutaliṉ avaṟṟi nīkkalāṉ+ pāmpueṉpatūum potumi nīkkiṟṟu eṉpār um uḷar. {{"uyarcol+ kiḷavi"}}, {{"iṭiccol+ kiḷavi"}}, {{"uriccol+kiḷavi"}} eṉpuḻi um, uyarcol, iṭiccol, uriccol= eṉpaṉa col=eṉpataṉkaṇ+ karuttu uṭiya aṉṟi+ kuṟi māttiram āy, uyarvu,iṭi, uri eṉṟa tuṇi āy niṉṟaṉa ākaliṉ, cāri eṉpatu pāmpivicēṭittāṟpōla avi kiḷavi eṉpataṉi vicēṭittu niṉṟaṉaeṉpatu, avvāṟṟāṉ amivu uṭiya āyiṉum, cūttiram ākaliṉ,uyarcol, iṭiccol, uriccol eṉa ē amiyum; kiḷavi eṉpatumiki eṉiṉ:-- miki āyiṉum iṉṉōraṉṉa amivu uṭiya eṉpatuuṇarttutaṟku avvāṟu ōtiṉār eṉpatu. ((20))

((ummittoki))

TC 417 c

[TRANSLATION]

iru peyar pal peyar aḷaviṉ peyar ē
eṇ= iyal+ peyar ē niṟi+ peyar+ kiḷavi
eṇṇiṉ peyarōṭu av= aṟu kiḷavi um
kaṇṇiya nilittu ē ummi+ toki ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: iru peyar mutal āka+ collappaṭṭa aṟu vaki+ col+tiraḷi um taṉakku+ cārpu āka+ kuṟittu niṟkum ummi+ toki;e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} e-ṭu: {uvā+ patiṉāṉku} eṉpatu iru peyarāṉ āya ummittoki.{puli vil+ keṇṭi} eṉpatu pala peyarāṉ āya ummittoki.{tūṇi+ patakku} eṉpatu aḷavu-p-peyarāṉ āya ummittoki,{muppattu mūvar} eṉpatu eṇ= iyal+ peyarāṉ āya ummittoki,{toṭi ari} eṉpatu niṟi+ peyarāṉ āya ummittoki.{patiṉintu} eṉpatu eṇṇu+-peyarāṉ āya ummittoki. iṉi aviviriyuṅkāl, {uvā um patiṉāṉku um} eṉa -~um, {puli um vil=um^ keṇṭi um} eṉa -~um, {tūṇi um patakku um} eṉa -~um,{muppatiṉmar um mūvar um} eṉa -~um, {toṭi um ari um} eṉa-~um, {pattu um aintu um} eṉa -~um viriyum.

{{Par. 3}} vēṟṟumi+ toki mutal āyiṉa pala collāṉ+ tokutal ciṟupāṉmi;ataṉāṉ ummittoki iru collāṉ um pala collāṉ um, oppa+ tokumeṉpatu aṟivittaṟku {{"iru peyar pal peyar"}} eṉṟār. {kal+cuṉi+ kuvaḷi itaḻ, perum^ tōḷ+ pēti} eṉa+ piṟa toki umperumpāṉmi um pala collāṉ varum āl eṉiṉ:-- {kal=} eṉpatuum^ {cuṉi} eṉpatu um^ {kal+ cuṉi} eṉa+ tokku oru col= āy+piṉ {kuvaḷi} eṉpataṉōṭu tokku oru col= āy+ piṉ {itaḻ}eṉpatōṭu tokku+ {kal+ cuṉi+ kuvaḷi itaḻ} eṉa oṉṟu āyiṟṟu.{perum^ tōḷ} eṉṉum^ toki oru col= āy+ {pēti} eṉpataṉōṭutokku+ {perum^ tōḷ+ pēti} eṉa oṉṟu āyiṟṟu. avi iv= āṟṟāṉallatu tokāmiyiṉ iru col+ toki ē ām. {puli vil+ keṇṭi}eṉpuḻi mūṉṟu peyar um^ tokum eṉṉātu mutal+ peyar oḻittu umiṟuti+ peyar oḻittu um, ēṉi iraṇṭu um^ tam= uḷ+ tokku orucol= āy+ piṉ maṟṟiyataṉōṭu tokum eṉiṉ:-- muṉ tokumiraṇṭaṟku um ōr iyipu vēṟupāṭu iṉmiyāṉ um, iru toki+paṭuttal pala ceykittu ākalāṉ um, avi mūṉṟu peyar umoruṅku tokkaṉa eṉa ē paṭum eṉpatu.

{{Par. 4}} {{"aḷaviṉ peyar"}} mutal āyiṉa, iru peyar āy allatu tokā eṉavariyaṟuttaṟku {{"iru peyar pal peyar"}} eṉaaṭaṅkuvaṉavaṟṟi+ peyarttu+ kūṟiṉār. {kalaṉ ē tūṇi+patakku}, {toṭi ē kaḵcu ari} {nūṟṟu nāṟpattu nāṉku} eṉpuḻi+{tūṇi+ patakku, kaḵcu ari, nāṟpattu nāṉku} eṉpaṉa oru col+pōla aḷavu-p-peyar um, niṟi-p-peyar um eṇṇu+ peyar um āyvaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varutaliṉ, {kalam um^ tūṇi+ patakku um, toṭium kaḵcu ari um, nūṟu um nāṟpattu nāṉku um} iru moḻi niṉṟutokka eṉṟal ē poruttam uṭimi aṟika.

{{Par. 5}} ummi+ toki iṉṉa poruḷ paṟṟi tokum eṉṉātu {{"av= aṟu kiḷavium"}} eṉa+ col= ē paṟṟi ōtiṉār ēṉum, `ēṟpuḻi+ kōṭal'eṉpataṉāṉ {{"uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ ummi+ toki ē palar col%naṭittu"}} {{[col:421]}} eṉpataṉāṉ eṇ= ummi+ poruṭkaṇ+tokum eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. eṇṇiṉkaṇ varum iṭiccol+ pala ēṉum,tokku niṟkum āṟṟal uṭiyatu ummi+ peyar ākalāṉ, ummittokiāyiṟṟu. ((21))

((aṉmoḻi+ toki))

TC 418 c

[TRANSLATION]

paṇpu toka varūum^ kiḷaviyāṉ um
ummi tokka peyar vayiṉāṉ um
vēṟṟumi tokka peyar vayiṉāṉ um
īṟṟu niṉṟu iyalum aṉmoḻittoki ē.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: paṇpuccol+ tokum^ colliṉ -um ummi tokka peyarkkaṇ= -umvēṟṟumi tokka peyarkkaṇ= -um iṟuti+ coṟkaṇ ^niṉṟu naṭakkumaṉmoḻi+ toki; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} paṇpu+ toki paṭa um ummi+ toki paṭa um vēṟṟumi+ tokipaṭa um a+ col+ tokkapiṉ a+ toki aṉmoḻi+ toki ākāmiyiṉtokuvataṉ muṉ avaṟṟiṟku nilikkaḷam ākiya col+ paṟṟi varumeṉpatu viḷakkiya, toki vayiṉāṉ um eṉṉātu, {{"paṇpu tokavarūum^ kiḷaviyāṉ um ummi tokka peyar vayiṉāṉ um, vēṟṟumitokka peyar vayiṉāṉ um"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 3}} iṟuti+ col+ paṭuttal ōciyāṉ+ poruḷ viḷakkum āṟu vaḻakkiṉuḷ= um^ ceyyuḷ uḷ= um^ kaṇṭukoḷka.

{{Par. 4}} e-ṭu: {veḷ= āṭi, akara īṟu} eṉpaṉa paṇpu+ tokinilikkaḷattu+ piṟanta aṉmoḻi+ toki. {takara ñāḻal} eṉpatuummi+ toki nilikkaḷattu+ piṟanta aṉmoḻi+ toki. {poṉ+^toṭi} eṉpatu vēṟṟumi+ toki nilikkaḷattu+ piṟanta aṉmoḻi+toki. iṉi, avi {veḷ= āṭi uṭuttāḷ, akaram ākiya īṟṟiuṭiya col} eṉa -~um, {takaram um* ñāḻal um ākiya cāntupūciṉāḷ} eṉa -~um, {poṉ+ ^toṭi toṭṭāḷ} eṉa -~um viriyum.

{{Par. 7}} {{"viṉiyiṉ um^ kuṟippiṉ um"}} {{"ivviraṇṭu"}} āy varutal-āvatu terinili viṉiyāṉ+ teṟṟeṉa+ tōṉṟal -um kuṟippuviṉiyāṉ+ teṟṟeṉa+ tōṉṟāmi -~um ām.

{{Par. 8}} muṟṟi-niṟṟal muṟṟuccoṟku ilakkaṇam ātal {{"muṟṟu iyal% moḻiē"}} eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟṟām. muṟṟi-niṟṟal -āvatu* itu eṉpatu viṉiyiyal uḷ+ kūṟiṉām {{[col:235]}}. tiṇi umpāl um iṭam um viḷakkal ellā muṟṟiṟku um iṉmiyāṉ ilakkaṇamaṉmi aṟika.

{{Par. 9}} uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi viravu eṉṉum poruḷ% mēl viṉi umviṉikkuṟippu um āy varutal paṟṟi {{"av= āṟu eṉpa"}} eṉṟār.kālam um iṭam um mutal āyiṉavaṟṟōṭu kūṭṭi+ pakuppa+ pala ām.

{{Par. 10}} {ūrāṉ ōr tēva kulam} eṉṟāṟpōla meymmiyāṉ um eṉpuḻi {āṉ}eṉpatu {toṟum} eṉpataṉ poruṭṭu āy niṉṟatu.

{{Par. 11}} muṉṉar+ {{"pirinili viṉi ē peyar ē"}} {{[col:430]}}eṉpuḻi+ peyareccam um viṉiyeccam um^ kūṟupa ākaliṉ avaṟṟōṭuiyiya muṟṟuccolli um īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉār. kūṟa ē muṟṟuccol= umpeyareccam um viṉiyeccam um eṉa viṉiccol% mū vakittu ātaliṉitu uṇarappaṭum eṉpatu.

{{Par. 12}} uriyāciriyar viṉiyiyal uḷ ōtappaṭṭaṉa cila viṉiccoṟkumuṟṟu+ col= eṉṟu kuṟi-~iṭutal* ^nutaliṟṟu i+ cūttiram eṉṟārāl eṉiṉ:-- kuṟiyīṭu karuttu āyiṉ {{"av= āṟu eṉpa muṟṟu iyal%moḻi ē"}} eṉṉātu `av= āṟu muṟṟu iyal% moḻi' eṉal vēṇṭumākalāṉ atu pōli uri eṉka. `muṟṟu iyal% moḻi eṉpa' eṉamoḻimāṟṟa ē kuṟiyīṭu ām eṉiṉ:-- kuṟiyīṭu āṭci+ poruṭṭuākaliṉ a+ kuṟiyāṉ ataṉi āḷāmiyāṉ moḻi-māṟṟi iṭar+-paṭuvatueṉṉi -~ō eṉpatu. allatūum {{"muṟṟu iyal% moḻi"}} eṉa+kuṟi-~iṭṭār āyiṉ ivi {{"peyar eñcu kiḷavi"}} eṉa -~um, ivi{{"viṉi eñcu kiḷavi"}} eṉa -~um^ kuṟi-~iṭal vēṇṭum; avvāṟukuṟi-~iṭāmiyāṉ -um atu karuttu aṉṟu ām. ataṉāṉ viṉiccol=uḷ iru vaki eccam oḻittu oḻinta col% muṟṟi-niṟkum eṉṟu*-um, avi iṉittu+ -pāku-paṭum eṉṟu* -um uṇarttal i+cūttirattiṟku+ karuttu* -āka+ koḷka. ((31))

{{Par. 1}}

{{C CENV}} yār, evaṉ, illi, vēṟu eṉpaṉa iṭam uṇarttāmiyiṉ, mēl+kūṟiya kaṭṭaḷiyiṉ aṭaṅkātu piṟāṇṭu vantaṉa ām. ciṟappuīṟṟāṉ varum terinili viṉi um^ kuṟippu viṉi um poruḷtōṟum, viṉi um viṉikkuṟippu um āy varutaṟku eytāmiyiṉ{{"meymmiyāṉ um ivviraṇṭu ākum"}} {{[col:427]}} kaṭṭaḷiuṭpaṭātu piṟāṇṭu vantaṉa ām. kuṟippu viṉikku īṟu ākātuterinili viṉikku īṟu āvaṉa um, terinili viṉikku īṟuākātu kuṟippu viṉikku īṟu āvaṉa um ciṟappu īṟṟa ām. aviviṉiyiyal uḷ+ kūṟi+ pōntām. yār evaṉ eṉpaṉa pāl um, illi vēṟu eṉpaṉa tiṇi um pāl umuṇarttā āyiṉum mēli+ cūttirattāṉ muṟṟuccoṟku+ pāl um^ tiṇium uṇarttal orutali āka eytāmiyiṉ iṭam uṇarttāmi ē paṟṟiīṇṭu+ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. tiṇi um pāl um uṇarttal orutali āyiṉ,{{"taṉmi muṉṉili paṭarkki eṉṉum a+ mū iṭattāṉ"}}{{[col:427]}} eṉa āciriyar muṉṉili iṭam āṇṭu viyār eṉpatu.

{{Par. 3}} muṟṟuccol= ē aṉṟi+ peyareccam um viṉiyeccam um kālam umiṭam um uṇarttum eṉpatu i+ cūttirattiṟku+ poruḷ āka urittārāl uriyāciriyar eṉiṉ:-- avi iṭa vēṟupāṭu uṇarttātu mūiṭattiṟku um potu āy niṟṟaliṉ, atu pōliyuri eṉka.

{{Par. 4}} iṉi ōr uri:-- mēli+ cūttirattāṉ+ kūṟappaṭṭa poruḷ% mēl umvarum ellā viṉi um muṟṟuccol= āy niṟkum eṉṟa āṟu. īṇṭuviṉi ~eṉṟatu viṉiccolli ākkum* mutaṉiliyi. ellā viṉium muṟṟu+ col= ām eṉa ē, eccam ātal orutali aṉṟu eṉpatu ām.āka ē, viṉiccol= ātaṟku+ ciṟantaṉa muṟṟuccol= ē eṉpatu ām.ellā viṉi um muṟṟuccol= ākal um, {kacciṉaṉ, kaḻaliṉaṉ,nilattaṉ, puṟattaṉ} eṉṉum^ toṭakkattu viṉikkuṟippiṉmutaṉili eccam āy nillāmi um, vaḻakku nōkki+ kaṇṭukoḷka.piṟa um aṉṉa.

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: eñcu poruḷ+ kiḷavi um av= eñcu poruḷ+ kiḷaviyāṉmuṭivatu um ākiya ummi ecca vēṟupāṭu iraṇṭaṉkaṇ= um,taṉviṉi ummiyoṭu toṭarnta coṟku+ poruntiya muṭipu ām;e-ṟu.

{{C CENV}} eṉṟatu, {{"eñcu poruḷ+ kiḷavi cem^ col āyiṉ"}} {{[col:284]}}eṉṟataṉāṉ ##ummi uṭittu* āy -um ummi ~iṉṟi -~um varumeñcu-poruḷ+-kiḷavi ummi eccattiṟku muṭipu ātal# eytiṟṟu.eṉṉi? ellā ~eccattiṟku* -um eñcu-poruḷ+-kiḷavi -~ē muṭipu*ākaliṉ. iṉi ummiyoṭu toṭarnta col= iraṇṭaṟku* -um viṉi~oṉṟu* -ē ~ākal vēṇṭum eṉa eytātatu eytuvitta āṟu.

{{Par. 2}} oṉṟaṟku āyatu ē ēṉiyataṟku um ākaliṉ+ taṉviṉi eṉṟār.

{{Par. 3}} e-ṭu: {cāttaṉ um vantāṉ koṟṟaṉ um vantāṉ} eṉa iraṇṭu umoruviṉi koṇṭa āṟu kaṇṭukoḷka. {cāttaṉ um vantāṉ koṟṟaṉ umuṇṭāṉ} eṉa viṉi vēṟupaṭṭavaḻi ummi eccam um eñcu poruḷ+kiḷavi um iyiyāmi kaṇṭukoḷka. aḵtēl,
«pim putal
vēṅki um oḷ= iṇar virintaṉa
neṭu veṇ tiṅkaḷ um ūr koṇṭaṉṟu ē»
{{[akam:2_15/16/17]}} eṉa viṉi vēṟupaṭṭuḻi um^ tam= uḷ iyintaṉa āl eṉiṉ:-- {iṇarvirital um ūr kōṭal um} iraṇṭu um {maṇam^ cey kālam itu}eṉṟu uṇarttutal ākiya oru poruḷ kuṟittu niṉṟamiyāṉ, avioru viṉi+ pāṟpaṭum eṉpatu. piṟa um ivvāṟu varuvaṉa aṟintuoru viṉi+ pāl+-paṭukka.

{{Par. 4}} eñcu poruḷ+ kiḷavi cem^ col āyavaḻi+ taṉviṉi kōṭal īṇṭuaṭaṅkāmiyāṉ, atu `taṉṉiṉamuṭittal' eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟappaṭum.

{{Par. 5}} {{"ummi eccam iru vīṟṟāṉ um"}} eṉṟataṉāṉ, ummi eccattiṟkumuṭipu ākiya eñcu poruḷ+ kiḷavi ummiyoṭu variṉ eccam āmeṉpatu ām. aḵtu eccam ām^ kāl, muṉ niṉṟatu, eñcu poruḷ+kiḷavi ām eṉpatu.

{{Par. 6}} etirmaṟi ummi etirmaṟi eccam āy aṭaṅkutaliṉ, īṇṭu{{"ummi eccam"}} eṉṟatu eccavummi ē ām. ((40))

{{Par. 3}} taṉmikku um muṉṉilikku um uriya tā eṉpataṉāṉ āka potuākiya ī eṉpataṉāṉ āka aṉṟē collal+ pālatu; uyarntāṉ aṅṅaṉam^tāṉ ēṟpāṉ āka+ collātu, koṭu eṉa+ paṭarkki vāypāṭṭāṉ+collum; āṇṭu+ taṉṉi ē piṟaṉ pōla+ kuṟittāṉ ākaliṉ+ taṉ=iṭattu ē ām eṉa iṭa vaḻu amitta āṟu.

{{Par. 4}} uyarntāṉ tamaṉ oruvaṉi+ kāṭṭi ivaṟku+ koṭu eṉṉum eṉṟār āluriyāciriyar eṉiṉ:-- paṭarkkiccol+ paṭarkki+ collōṭuiyitalāṉ vaḻu iṉmiyiṉ amikkal vēṇṭā ām ataṉāṉ atupōliyuri eṉka. ((52))

{{Par. 2}} iḵtu i+ cūttirattiṟku oru cārār uri. oru cārār piṟaurippa.

«putuvataṉ
iyaṉṟa aṇiyaṉ»
{{[akam:66_8/9]}} eṉa -~um, tammi muṭikkum viṉikkaṇ+ kiṭanta toḻil āṉumpaṇpu* āṉum^ kuṟippu* āṉum uṇartti+ terinili viṉi um^kuṟippu viṉi um āy muṭikkum^ colli vicēṭittal um, piṟa umām. ceytu eṉ eccattu īṟu tirital viṉiyiyal uḷ+ kāṭṭi+pōntām.

{{Par. 3}}
«perum^ kiyaṟṟa eṉ pulampu muntuṟuttu» eṉpuḻi+ perum eṉpataṉi oru cārār viṉi-~ecca_vāypāṭu eṉpa;oru cārār viṉiccol+ paṟṟi niṉṟatu* ōr uriccol eṉpa.

{{Par. 4}} iṭiccol= um uriccol= um mēl+ kūṟappaṭṭa ilakkaṇam -ē aṉṟi+piṟa ~ilakkaṇam -um uṭiya eṉpatu uṇarttiṉār. iṉi avi -~ēaṉṟi ##{{"viṉi eñcu kiḷavi -~um"}} pala ilakkaṇattaṉa#eṉpatu paṭa-niṉṟamiyāṉ, ummi iṟantatu taḻīiya ecca-~ummi.av= ilakkaṇam ōr-iyala aṉṟi+ tirital -um vēṟu poruḷuṇarttutal -um vicēṭittal -um* mutal ākiya vēṟṟumi uṭiya~ākaliṉ, {{"vēṟu pal kuṟiya"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 5}} viṉiyeccattu* -uḷ vicēṭittu* -ē -niṟpaṉa um uḷa eṉpatūumuṇarttukiṉṟār ākaliṉ, itaṉi viṉiyiyal uḷ viyātu, īṇṭuvicēṭikkum^ col= uṇarttuvaṉavaṟṟōṭu vittār.

{{Par. 6}}
«peyarttaṉeṉ muyaṅka»
{{[kuṟun:84_1]}} eṉpatu mutal āyiṉa ceytu eṉ eccam muṟṟu āy+ tirintaṉa eṉṟu*-um,
«oṭittu uṇṭu eñciya»
{{[kuṟun:232_4]}} eṉpatu mutal āyiṉa ceya eṉ eccam ceytu eṉ eccam āy+tirintaṉa eṉṟu* -um, muṉṉar urittār āl uriyāciriyar eṉiṉ: -- «peyarttaṉeṉ muyaṅka» eṉpatu mutal āyiṉa ecca+ tiripuāyiṉ ecca+ poruḷ uṇarttuvatu allatu iṭam um pāl um uṇarttal+pāla alla. ecca+ poruṇmi -āvatu mūṉṟu* iṭattiṟku* -um aintupāṟku* -um potu ~ākiya viṉi nikaḻcci aṉṟē; avvāṟu aṉṟimuṟṟuccoṟku ōtiya īṟṟa āy iṭam um pāl um uṇarttaliṉ, avimuṟṟu+-tiri-col= eṉa ē paṭum. coṉṉili uṇarntu viṉi kōṭal%māttirattāṉ viṉiyeccam eṉiṉ:-- māri+ kiḷavi um, viṉiyoṭumuṭiyum vēṟṟumi um, piṟa um ellām viṉiyeccam āvāṉ cellum;ataṉāṉ avarkku atu karuttu aṉṟu eṉka. allatūum, «kaṇṇiyaṉvillaṉ» varum eṉa viṉikkuṟippu muṟṟu āy+ tiritaṟku ēṟpatuōr viṉiyeccam iṉmiyāṉ um, atu karuttu aṉmi aṟika.

{{Par. 7}} «oṭittu uṇṭu eñciya» eṉpatūum {ñāyiṟu paṭṭu vantāṉ} eṉpatūumpiṟa viṉi koṇṭaṉa āyiṉum, ceya eṉ eccattiṟku uriya iṟantakālam uṇarttalāṉ, ēṉi+ kālattiṟku uriya ceytu eṉ eccattiṉtiripu eṉappaṭā; ceya eṉ ecca+ tiripu āyiṉ+ ceya eṉeccattiṟku uriya kālam uṇarttal vēṇṭum. {maḻi peyya maram^kuḻittatu} eṉa+ ceya eṉ eccattiṟku iṟanta kālam um urittueṉiṉ:-- kāraṇa-kāriya+ poruṇmi uṇarttum vaḻi allatu ceya eṉeccam iṟanta kālam uṇarttātu. {oṭittu uṇṭal} um {ñāyiṟupaṭutal} um eñcutaṟku um varutaṟku um^ kāraṇam aṉmiyāṉ,āṇṭu iṟantakālam uṇarttāmiyiṉ, ceytu eṉ eccam āy niṉṟutamakku uriya iṟantakālam uṇarttiṉa eṉappaṭum. ataṉāṉ+ ceyaeṉ eccam^ ceytu eṉ eccam āy+ tirintaṉa eṉṟal um avar karuttuaṉṟu eṉka. {ñāyiṟu paṭṭu vantāṉ} eṉpatu {ñāyiṟu paṭṭa piṉvantāṉ} eṉa iṟantakālam uṇarttal um, {ñāyiṟu paṭa vantāṉ}eṉpatu {ñāyiṟu paṭā niṟka vantāṉ} eṉa nikaḻ kālam uṇarttalum vaḻakku nōkki+ kaṇṭukoḷka. ((61))

((potuvāka+ toṭarmoḻikaḷ))

uri iṭattu iyalum uṭaṉili aṟital.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: vaḻakku iṭattu uṭaṉiṟkal+ pāla allaṉavaṟṟatu uṭaṉilipōṟṟuka; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} uṭaṉiṟkal+ pāla allaṉa āvaṉa tam= uḷ% māṟupāṭu uṭiyaṉa.māṟupāṭu illaṉavaṟṟatu uṭaṉilikkaṇ ārāycci iṉmiyiṉ,uṭaṉili eṉṟatu māṟupāṭu uṭiyaṉavaṟṟatu uṭaṉili ē ām.

{{Par. 3}} e-ṭu: {i+ nāḻikku i+ nāḻi ciṟitu peritu} eṉa uṭaṉiṟkal+ pālaallā+ ciṟumi um perumi um uṭaṉiṉṟa āṟu kaṇṭu koḷka.{ciṟitu} eṉpatu {peritu} eṉappaṭṭa poruḷi nōkkātu {peritu}eṉpataṟku aṭi āy {mika+ peritu aṉṟu} eṉpatu paṭa-niṟṟalāṉ,amivu uṭittu* āyiṟṟu.

{{Par. 4}} {{"aṟital"}} eṉpatu ivvāṟu amivu uṭiyaṉa koḷka eṉṟa āṟu.

{{Par. 5}} māṟupāṭu uṭiyaṉa uṭaṉiṟṟal eḻu vaki vaḻuviṉ uḷ oṉṟuaṉmiyāṉ, itaṉi+ kiḷaviyākkattu uḷ+ kūṟātu īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉār.((62))

((orumi+ peyar paṉmikku ātal))

{{Par. 3}} {{"paṉmikku ākum iṭaṉum ār uṇṭu ē"}} eṉpatu, orumi+ col+paṉmi+ collōṭu toṭartaṟku+ poruntum iṭam uṇṭu eṉpatūum paṭaniṉṟamiyāṉ,
«aḵti tanti

{{Par. 4}} ēṟpuḻi+ kōṭal eṉpataṉāṉ uyartiṇi+ kaṇṇatu i+ mayakkam eṉṟukoḷka.

((āṟṟuppaṭiyil muṉṉili orumi paṉmiyoṭu muṭital))

[TRANSLATION]

muṉṉili cuṭṭiya orumi+ kiḷavi

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: muṉṉili kuṟittu niṉṟa orumi+ col paṉmiyoṭu muṭintatuāyiṉum, variyappaṭātu; a+ muṭipu āṟṟuppaṭi+ ceyyuḷ iṭattu+pōṟṟi uṇarappaṭum; e-ṟu.

{{Par. 2}}

{{C CENV}} kūttar āṟṟuppaṭi uḷ
«kalam peṟu kaṇṇuḷar okkal+ ^taliva»
{{[mali:50]}} eṉa niṉṟa orumi+ col+ pōy
«irum pēr okkaloṭu pata mika+ perukuvīr»
{{[mali:157]}} eṉṉum paṉmi+ collōṭu muṭinta āṟu kaṇṭukoḷka. īṇṭu muṉṉiliorumi+ peyar ātal atikārattāṉ+ koḷka.

{{Par. 3}} {{"orumi cuṭṭiya peyar nili+ kiḷavi paṉmikku ākum iṭaṉumār uṇṭu ē"}} {{[col:461]}} eṉpataṉāṉ itu um aṭaṅkutaliṉ, i+cūttiram vēṇṭā eṉiṉ:-- paṉmiyoṭu muṭiyum iṭaṉ um ār uṇṭu ēeṉṉātu, {{"paṉmikku ākum iṭaṉ um ār uṇṭu ē"}} eṉṟār ātaliṉ,āṇṭu+ paṉmi+ col+ koṇṭu muṭiyātu orumi+ col+ paṉmiuṇarttutal um paṉmi+ colloṭu oru poruṭṭu ākiya tuṇi āymayaṅkutal um uṇarttiṉār; ataṉāṉ i+ koṇṭumuṭipu āṇṭuaṭaṅkātu eṉpatu. allatūum, i+ muṭipu ceyyuṭku urittueṉṟamiyāṉ um āṇṭu aṭaṅkāmi aṟika.

{{Par. 4}} potu vakiyāṉ {{"āṟṟuppaṭi maruṅkiṉ"}} eṉṟār āyiṉum,cuṟṟattōṭu cuṟṟa+ talivaṉi āṟṟuppaṭuttal+ kaṇṇatu i+mayakkam eṉpatu pātukāttu uṇarka eṉpār {{"pōṟṟal vēṇṭum"}}eṉṟār.

{{Par. 5}} {{"pāl% mayakku uṟṟa aiya+ kiḷavi"}} {{[col:23]}} eṉpataṉāṉ+kūṟiya orumi+ paṉmi mayakkam vaḻu amiti āyiṉumilakkaṇattōṭu ottu+ payiṉṟu varum. orumi cuṭṭiya peyarnili+ kiḷavi paṉmikku ātal um, muṉṉili orumi paṉmiyoṭumuṭital um aṉṉa aṉṟi+ ciṟuvaḻakkiṉa ātaliṉ, āṇṭu viyātuīṇṭu vittār.

{{Par. 6}} oruvar oruvari āṟṟuppaṭuttaṟkaṇ muṉṉili orumi paṉmiyoṭumuṭital vaḻakkiṟku um okkum ākalāṉ, āṟṟuppaṭi eṉa+potuvakiyāṉ+ kūṟiṉār. ((66))

((7. collatikārattiṟku+puṟaṉaṭi))

((iruvaki vaḻakkiṉum nūlvaḻakkiṉ mutaṉmi))

TC 463 c

[TRANSLATION]

ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ um vaḻakku iyal% maruṅkiṉ um
mey peṟa+ kiḷanta kiḷavi ellām
pal vēṟu ceytiyiṉ nūl neṟi piḻiyātu
col varintu aṟiya+ pirittaṉar kāṭṭal.

{{Par. 1}}

{{WFW}} i-ḷ: ceyyuḷ iṭattu um vaḻakku iṭattu um iv= atikārattiṉkaṇporuḷ peṟa+ collappaṭṭa col= ellāvaṟṟi um pala vēṟu ceykiuṭiya tol% ^nūl* ^neṟiyiṉ+ piḻiyātu colli vēṟupaṭuttuuṇarum āṟṟāṉ+ pirittu+ kāṭṭuka; e-ṟu.

{{C CENV}} eṉṟatu, {{"nila+ peyar kuṭi+ peyar"}} eṉa -~um, {{"am= āmem= ēm"}} eṉa -~um potu vakiyāṉ+ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. {aruvāḷanilattāṉ} eṉṉum poruṭkaṇ {aruvāḷaṉ} eṉa -~um; {cōḻanilattāṉ} eṉṉum poruṭkaṇ {cōḻiyaṉ} eṉa -~um;iṟantakālattiṉkaṇ {uṇṭaṉam, uṇṭām} eṉa -~um;nikaḻkālattiṉkaṇ {uṇṇāniṉṟaṉam, uṇṇāniṉṟām, uṇkiṉṟām} eṉa-~um; etirkālattiṉkaṇ {uṇkuvam, uṇpām} eṉa -~um vēṟupaṭṭuvarum aṉṟē; a+ vēṟupāṭu ellām kūṟiṉ+ palkum eṉṟu añci+kūṟiṟṟu ilaṉ āyiṉum, tol% ^nūl* ^neṟiyiṉ+ piḻiyāmal a+vēṟupāṭu uṇara+ pirittu+ kāṭṭuka nūl vallār eṉṟa āṟu āyiṟṟu.

{{Par. 2}} itu piṟa nūl% muṭintatu tāṉ uṭampaṭutal eṉṉum^ tantira utti.piṟa um aṉṉa.

{{Par. 3}} ceyki -- viti.

{{Par. 4}} {{"col varintu aṟiya"}} eṉa ē, varintu ōtātu potu vakiyāṉōtappaṭṭavaṟṟiṉ mēṟṟu i+ puṟaṉaṭi eṉpatu ām.

{{Par. 5}} iṉi ōr uri:-- ceyyuḷ iṭattu um vaḻakku iṭattu um eṉṉāṉ+kiḷakkappaṭātu tol% ^nūl āciriyarāṉ+ kiḷakkappaṭṭu eñciniṉṟa col= ellāvaṟṟi um avva+ tol% ^nūl* ^neṟiyiṉ+piḻiyāmi+ colli varintu uṇara+ koṇarntu pirittu+ kāṭṭukaeṉṟa āṟu.

{{Par. 6}} eṉṉāṉ+ kiḷakkappaṭāta eṉpatu peṟṟa āṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ:--kiḷantaṉa piṟa nūliṉ+ koṇarntu kāṭṭal vēṇṭāmiyiṉ+kiḷakkappaṭātaṉa eṉpatu peṟappaṭum eṉka.

{{Par. 7}} puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ+ koḷḷappaṭuvaṉa:-- {yāṉ um* nī um avaṉ um^celvēm} eṉa -~um, {yāṉ um* nī um^ celvēm} eṉa -~um ēṉiiṭattiṟku uriya col taṉmi+ collōṭu iyintavaḻi+ taṉmiyāṉmuṭital um, {avaṉ um* nī um^ ceṉmiṉ} eṉa+ paṭarkki+ col%muṉṉiliyōṭu iyintavaḻi muṉṉiliyāṉ muṭital um,
«nillātu peyarnta pallōr uḷ= um»
{{[akam:110_20]}} eṉpuḻi+ {pallēm uḷ= um} eṉa+ taṉmi ākal+ pālatu {pallōr uḷ=um} eṉa+ paṭarkki+ paṉmi āya vaḻi amital um,
«muracu keḻu tāṉi mūvar uḷ= um
aracu eṉappaṭuvatu niṉatu ē peruma»
{{[puṟam:35_4/5]}} eṉpuḻi mūvir uḷ= um eṉa muṉṉili ākal+ pālatu mūvar uḷ= umeṉa+ paṭarkki āya vaḻi amital um, {iraṇṭaṉ uḷ+ kūrm^ kōṭṭakāṭṭuval} eṉpuḻi+ {kūrm^ kōṭṭatu} eṉa orumi ākal+ pālatu{kūrm^ kōṭṭa} eṉa+ paṉmi āya vaḻi amital um ām. piṟa umuḷa ēl+ koḷka.

{{Par. 8}} akattiyam* mutal āyiṉa ellā ilakkaṇam um^ kūṟaliṉ+ {{"palvēṟu ceytiyiṉ* ^nūl"}} eṉṟār.

{{Par. 9}} iv= iraṇṭu uri um i+ cūttirattiṟku uri āka+ koḷka. ((67))

((ecca iyal muṟṟiṟṟu.))

((collatikāra+ cēṉāvariyar uri muṟṟu+ peṟṟatu.))
